### [1] [ObjectTwin: Measuring the Asset Correspondence Gap for Capture-Anchored Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35904)
*Zhenghe Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究通过ObjectTwin方法在捕获场景中冻结特定对象，摄像头和支持表面，并替换对象表示，以测量生成的资产与扫描衍生的可见几何之间的差异，指出生成资产的质量可以通过捕捉对应关系进行独立的测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决对象级重建中生成网格与真实场景中的对象匹配的问题，以评估生成资产的质量。

Method: 研究基于扫描的测量协议ObjectTwin，它在捕获状态下冻结实例、相机、扫描投影支持和记录的深度、度量锚点和评估分母，然后才替换对象表示，从而测量资产对应的缺口。

Result: 实验结果表明，可见且经过锚定的生成资产在替换后，MaskIoU分别为0.302-0.497。所有检测到的场景级估计值均为正，而来自同一输入的生成器偏好在不同捕获域中相互逆转。覆盖和重叠深度分析发现分歧存在于可见支持和表面定位中；评估的保守姿态细化没有系统地恢复这些差异。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，捕获对应关系可以作为生成资产质量和更强大的物理或任务级评估之间的可测量接口，这对于评估生成资产的质量具有重要意义。

Abstract: Object-wise reconstruction increasingly inserts generated meshes into captured scenes as editable proxies for observed objects. This use creates a requirement absent from isolated-asset scores and pose estimates: at the captured metric state, the proxy should reproduce calibrated observations of the same instance. We present ObjectTwin, a scan-grounded measurement protocol that freezes the instance, cameras, scan-projected support and recorded depth, metric anchor, and evaluation denominator, then replaces only the object representation. The resulting asset correspondence gap measures lost observation agreement relative to scan-derived visible geometry without treating the scan as complete physical ground truth. Across two indoor RGB-D capture domains and two generation backends, visible, anchored generated assets lose 0.302-0.497 MaskIoU under this replacement. Every tested scene-level estimate is positive, while same-input generator preference reverses across capture domains. Coverage and overlap-depth analyses locate disagreement in both visible support and surface placement; the evaluated conservative pose refinement does not systematically recover it. These results establish capture correspondence as a separately measurable interface between generated-asset quality and stronger physical or task-level evaluation.

</details>


### [2] [Task-Utility IQA: Rethinking Image Quality Assessment in Embodied Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35905)
*Jirong Zha,Yanwei Liao,Yemin Wang,Xiangmin Yi,Siqi Peng,Yingfeng Chen,Chen Gao,Xinlei Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于MLLM的实体图像质量评估（IQA）代理框架，该框架通过理解任务需求、提供质量证据并生成面向决策的评估，从评分回归转向基于实体任务效用的目标条件判断。实验表明，传统感知度量和通用MLLM基线评分与下游VLM任务的成功弱相关，而基于任务条件的效用分数能够更好地预测视觉输入是否支持目标任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大多数IQA方法倾向于学习与通用人类评级一致的任务无关评分，但与实体和交互式场景中的成像管道需求不符。

Method: 提出了一种基于MLLM的实体IQA框架，包括理解和任务需求相关的推理、基于质量证据的判断以及面向决策的评估。

Result: 通过对比研究发现，传统感知度量和通用MLLM基线评分与下游VLM任务的成功之间关系较弱，而任务条件下的效用评分更能预测视觉输入是否支持目标任务。

Conclusion: 该研究支持一种基于任务效用重新定义实体IQA的观点，以促进实体、解释性和决策感知的视觉感知。

Abstract: Image quality assessment (IQA) underpins embodied imaging pipelines by judging whether visual quality satisfies downstream tasks, yet most existing methods learn task-agnostic scores aligned with generic human ratings on static benchmarks. This objective mismatches embodied and interactive settings, where image adequacy depends on task goals, context, and action requirements that shape an agent's decisions. We argue that IQA should shift from score regression to goal-conditioned judgment defined by embodied task utility. To this end, we propose an MLLM-based embodied IQA agent framework that reasons about task requirements, grounds quality evidence, and produces decision-facing assessments. A compact VisDrone evidence study shows that conventional perceptual metrics and generic MLLM-based IQA scores are weak proxies for downstream VLM task success, while a task-conditioned utility score better predicts whether visual input supports the target task. These findings support a task-utility-oriented reframing of IQA for embodied, explainable, and decision-aware visual perception.

</details>


### [3] [Physiognomy Revisited: How Vision-Language Models Encode Sensitive Attributes Through Image Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35910)
*Soumyajit Datta,Arka Dutta,Ashiqur R. KhudaBukhsh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 此研究探讨两个流行的视觉语言模型在生成图像时如何编码保护身份信息，例如种族和性取向。模型在特定请求下的响应表现出一致性，但这种响应往往加剧了刻板印象。这不仅限于种族和性取向，图像生成也可能传播关于犯罪性的偏见，引发AI辅助定罪的担忧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究新兴的生成AI技术存在的潜在问题，特别是它们对某些特定群体（如种族和性取向）可能产生的歧视性影响。

Method: 研究团队分析了两个视觉语言模型在面对将人脸图像转化为特定种族和性取向类别（如西班牙裔、黑人、白人、亚洲人、同性或异性恋）请求时的表现，并评估了生成图像的偏见。

Result: 研究发现，模型在请求转换面部图像以反映特定种族和性取向时常会遵从这些请求，而这种响应具有非确定性和组敏感性，生成的图像往往强化了深层存在的刻板印象。生成偏见不仅限于种族和性取向，还可能在刑事定罪等文本推理任务中重新出现。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了一个令人担忧的问题，即生成AI技术的使用可能加剧关于种族、性取向及其他属性的偏见，这在AI辅助定罪等情境中尤为严重。

Abstract: Emergent generative AI technologies, while remarkably beneficial across several tasks and domains, can suffer from unforeseen vulnerabilities and difficult-to-anticipate failure modes. Such failure modes can disparately affect vulnerable communities. This paper investigates two well-known vision language models (VLMs) and inspects how these models encode protected identities while generating images. We show that when VLMs are requested to transform human face images to reflect race identities (e.g., Hispanic, Black, White, or Asian) and sexual orientation (e.g., gay or straight), VLMs often comply with such requests. We demonstrate that compliance (and refusal) is non-deterministic, group-sensitive, and generated images are statistically distinguishable, often accentuating deep-seated stereotypes. We further show that representation biases introduced during image generation can re-emerge in textual reasoning and characterization tasks. Finally, we show that this worrisome finding is not limited to racial identity or sexual orientation, image generation can perpetuate bias around criminality, raising concerning possibilities of AI-assisted profiling.

</details>


### [4] [Learning and Reasoning with Geometry: How Generation Helps Spatial Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36022)
*Rui Yang,Yanwei Li,Sili Chen,Jingjia Huang,Yi Lin,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种统一的几何理解和生成模型（GeoUG），结合深度和多视图生成来增强空间理解。GeoUG首先生成初步答案，然后决定是否生成几何证据来确认或修订答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在利用图像和视频生成器的空间一致视觉内容生成能力，提升空间理解。

Method: GeoUG模型采用多尺度深度编码器预训练视觉自编码器生成度量深度，并通过3DCueBench控制几何难度，引入自适应交错推理机制根据需要生成几何证据。

Result: GeoUG在CV-Bench-3D、3DCueBench和DA-2K上的环形准确率分别为97.9%、90.8%和94.2%，且生成调用次数少于始终生成。

Conclusion: 研究表明，结合深度和多视图生成的GeoUG模型可以有效提高空间理解能力，在保持准确率的同时减少生成成本。

Abstract: Image and video generators can produce spatially consistent visual content, but how to use this capability to improve spatial understanding remains underexplored. We study this problem from two perspectives: geometric supervision during training and explicit geometric evidence during inference. To this end, we introduce a unified geometric understanding and generation model (GeoUG) that integrates depth and multi-view generation with spatial understanding. First, we design a multi-scale depth codec that enables metric-depth generation using a pretrained visual autoencoder, and show that joint training yields task-dependent gains in spatial understanding. Second, we introduce 3DCueBench to control geometric difficulty while balancing selected 2D cues, and find that generated depth provides larger reasoning gains on questions requiring fine-grained geometric discrimination. Building on this finding, we develop adaptive interleaved reasoning, in which GeoUG first drafts an answer and then decides whether to generate geometric evidence to confirm or revise it. We train this decision through reinforcement learning, balancing accuracy gains over retaining the draft against generation cost. GeoUG achieves circular accuracies of 97.9%, 90.8%, and 94.2% on CV-Bench-3D, 3DCueBench, and DA-2K with fewer generation calls than always generating.

</details>


### [5] [Any3R: Feedforward 3D Reconstruction from Any Camera Type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36150)
*Jingong Chen,Qingwen Zhang,Youming Deng,Chulwoo Pack,Olaf Wysocki,Boris Jutzi,Kwanghee Won,Kyle Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Any3R 是一个前馈模型，能够恢复任意无透镜中心相机的相机姿态和密集几何结构，不受镜头类型的影响，适用于混合不同相机类型的场景，且在基准测试中表现最为稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的几何基础模型假设为针孔图像，即依赖于镜头类型、畸变系数和视野范围。这限制了模型的泛化能力，特别是在包含多种不同相机类型的场景中。Any3R 通过条件设置在密集光线场的基础上工作，不受镜头类型的影响，从而克服了现有模型的局限性。

Method: Any3R 使用任意中心相机，不依赖于镜头类型，而是通过密集光线字段来表示，每一像素对应一个单位方向。通过角坐标测量每个像素片段的测量光线角度，并按视图进行评价，以及通过球面上每个像素覆盖的面积进行固有角度加权，从而不对监督和编码依赖于镜头。

Result: Any3R 在包含13种不同中心相机的基准测试中表现出色，尤其在混合不同类型的相机场景中具有最高的重建准确性。它能够在无需针对不同镜头类型重新训练的情况下，从异质的众包和档案图像集中实现大规模3D重建。

Conclusion: Any3R 为基于密集光线场的3D重建提供了一个通用解决方案，能够处理多种相机类型，解决了现有方法的限制，并展示了在实际应用场景中的潜力。

Abstract: Geometry foundation models recover camera poses and dense geometry from a set of images with a feed-forward network, but they expect pinhole images. That assumption is inside the backbone too: the positional encoding and the training loss both bind an image position to a direction in space, and both depend on the lens. We condition both on the dense ray field, the unit direction through every pixel, which describes any central camera without a lens type, distortion coefficients or a bound on field of view. Angular coordinates give the rotary encoding the measured ray angle of each patch, evaluated per view, and solid-angle weighting scales the point loss by the area each pixel covers on the sphere, so neither the encoding nor the supervision depends on the lens. Any3R is therefore a feed-forward model that takes any central camera, and is robust to mixing many camera types per scene, with no retraining per lens. On a benchmark of thirteen central cameras across three datasets, where every method reconstructs with its own predicted camera poses, it is the most consistently accurate method. Any3R enables scalable 3D reconstruction from heterogeneous crowdsourced and archival imagery collections sourced across diverse camera types. Project page: https://jingong-chen.github.io/Any3R/

</details>


### [6] [PrinGS: Privacy Preservation in Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36168)
*Gaurav Behera,Abhinav Raundhal,Archisha Panda,Saumya Balina,Gargi Shroff,Ponnurangam Kumaraguru*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种针对3D高斯点（3DGS）表示的隐私保护框架，能够直接匿名化已训练的3DGS点，同时保留整体场景几何结构和视觉保真度。研究还引入了一种隐私设计的训练策略，从源头上防止敏感信息被嵌入到表示中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着3D高斯点在新型视角合成和3D场景重建中的广泛应用，其学习表示会包含敏感视觉信息如人脸、车牌、文档和电脑屏幕等，导致隐私泄露问题。

Method: 研究提出了一种基于表示级别的匿名化框架，通过提供用户指定的参考视图来识别敏感内容区域，并直接对这些区域进行匿名化处理。此外，还提出了一种隐私设计的训练策略，在优化过程中防止敏感信息被嵌入到表示中。

Result: 该方法在不到1秒的时间内实现了后训练匿名化，且具有较低的Face Similarity Measure（FSM）为9.79%，保留了39.47%的Face Detection Rate，使得敏感文本完全不可读。

Conclusion: 研究展示了在保持3D场景几何结构和视觉保真度的同时，在已训练的3D高斯点上实现隐私保护的可能性，为进一步研究提供了基础。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has rapidly become the preferred representation for high-fidelity novel view synthesis and 3D scene reconstruction. However, the learned representation encodes sensitive visual information including human faces, license plates, documents, and computer screens, raising significant privacy concerns when trained splats are shared or deployed. In this work, we address the underexplored problem of privacy preservation in Gaussian Splatting by proposing a representation-level anonymization framework that directly anonymizes a trained splat. Given a trained splat and a user-provided reference view identifying sensitive content, our method localizes the corresponding regions in the underlying 3D representation and directly anonymizes them, ensuring that the sensitive information is absent from all subsequently rendered novel views while preserving the overall scene geometry and visual fidelity. We achieve this post-training anonymization in less than 1 second. Furthermore, we extend our framework with a privacy-by-design training strategy that prevents sensitive content from being embedded into the representation during optimization, enabling the generation of privacy-preserving splats directly from training images. Our method achieves a remarkably low Face Similarity Measure (FSM) of 9.79% at a retained Face Detection Rate (FDR) of 39.47% and makes sensitive text completely unreadable with an OCR Character Error Rate of 1.00. Our work aims to serve as the primary stepping stone towards privacy-aware 3D representations and motivates further research along these directions.

</details>


### [7] [Beyond the Estimate: Wiener-Informed Decoding for Easier Training of Pixel-Space Flow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36192)
*Ziqiao Wang,Yang You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为WiD的方法，利用冷冻的Wiener估计和统计知识加速和改进像素空间中的速度场学习。WiD结合了可训练的Wiener预测路径和系数接口，这些接口可以在不修改原生模块的情况下组织解码器计算。该方法在多种模型规模和解码器架构下都提升了训练速度和生成质量，并在不同分辨率下提高了类条件和文本到图像生成的质量。实验结果显示，该方法在ImageNet 256x256数据集上，PixelDiT-XL使用WiD可以在500个epoch后达到FID 1.53，比未使用WiD的PixelDiT节省了37.5%的训练epoch。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法学习像素空间中的速度场既困难又耗时，本文通过利用粗略的第二阶图像统计信息（如冻结的Wiener估计、冻结的基础库以及像素级解码）来简化这一任务，进而提出WiD方法。

Method: WiD方法引入了一种训练可调节的Wiener预测分支，同时利用统计信息引导预测过程和解码器计算过程。方法的具体实现包括一个训练可调的Wiener预测路径以及一组能够注入特征到原生解码器并返回其预测系数修正的系数接口。

Result: 该方法在多个模型规模和解码器架构上提升了训练速度和生成质量。它还产生了更平滑的梯度轨迹，并在各种分辨率的类条件和文本到图像生成任务中都表现良好。在ImageNet 256x256数据集上，使用WiD后的PixelDiT-XL的500个epoch后达到了FID 1.53，而未使用WiD的PixelDiT需要更多的训练epoch才能达到相似的质量。

Conclusion: WiD方法有效地利用了统计知识来加快像素空间中的速度场学习，并实现了较好的训练效率和生成质量。

Abstract: Learning velocity fields directly in pixel space is difficult and slow. We show that coarse second-order image statistics can ease this task: frozen Wiener estimates improve velocity learning, with gains extending beyond the linear predictions they explicitly provide. Moreover, we observe similar but complementary gains from frozen bases and pixel decoding. These observations motivate Wiener-informed Decoding (WiD), which uses statistical knowledge both for direct prediction and to organize decoder computation without modifying native blocks. WiD combines a trainable Wiener prediction path with coefficient interfaces that inject features into the native decoder and return its predicted coefficient corrections, with these components initialized with data statistics. All introduced parameters, including the spectrum and coordinate transforms, are trained jointly under the original pixel-space velocity objective. Across model scales and decoder architectures, WiD accelerates training and improves generation quality with modest parameter overhead. It also yields smoother gradient trajectories and remains effective with representation alignment. WiD improves class-conditional and text-to-image generation across different resolutions. On ImageNet 256 x 256, PixelDiT-XL with WiD reaches FID 1.53 after 500 epochs, achieving comparable quality to PixelDiT with 37.5% fewer training epochs. Statistical denoisers contribute beyond the estimate: their computational structure can also ease pixel-space training.

</details>


### [8] [Learning to Read Braille: Benchmarking and Improving Braille Recognition Capabilities of VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36195)
*Kartik Jangra,Atharva Gawane,Siddharth Pulipaka,Hemachandran Karah,Raj Dabre*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究介绍了BrailleBench基准，包含259张真实世界的Grade-1和Grade-2盲文图像。评估结果显示专用OCR和协助工具系统优于通用视觉语言模型。合成数据的预训练提高了Blind阅读准确性，但未达到真实图像的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于填补现代视觉语言模型在读取图片中盲文的能力空白，强调了对无障碍技术的需求和当前模型存在的差距。

Method: 该研究首先建立了BrailleBench数据集，由人工精挑细选的真实盲文图像组成。然后评估了不同模型（包括专用OCR系统、开放权重模型、工具协助系统等），并设计了一套合成数据生成方法，用于训练视觉语言模型。

Result: 使用合成数据训练的2B大小的视觉语言模型在Blind Bench打字比赛中获得了Grade-1 87%和Grade-2 61%的精准匹配率，超过所有独立视觉语言模型的表现，并在Grade-2上取得了最高分。

Conclusion: 研究结论表明，专门针对合成数据进行监督可以提升模型在真实盲文图像中的性能，表明了为特定任务设计合成数据的重要性。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress in visual understanding and document transcription, modern vision-language models (VLMs) struggle with a fundamental accessibility task: reading Braille from images. We introduce BrailleBench, a manually curated benchmark of 259 real-world Grade-1 and Grade-2 Braille images. We evaluate proprietary, open-weight, OCR-specialized, and tool-assisted multimodal systems and find that standalone general-purpose models perform poorly at directly transcribing Braille images, whereas tool-assisted systems perform much better. We design a synthetic Braille generation pipeline with diverse layouts and image perturbations. A 2B VLM model trained on our synthetic data reaches 87% exact match on Grade-1 and 61% on Grade-2 BrailleBench, outperforming standalone VLMs and achieving the highest Grade-2 exact match score among the evaluated systems. We further show that high accuracy on synthetic validation data does not guarantee high accuracy on real photographs in our experiments: models trained on prior synthetic data reach up to 96% on their synthetic validation set but at most 3.26% on Grade-1 BrailleBench. Our results identify an accessibility-relevant capability gap in multimodal systems and show that targeted synthetic supervision can improve real-world Braille transcription.

</details>


### [9] [UT-HMR: Uncertainty-Guided Temporal Modeling for Robust 3D Human Mesh Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36203)
*Zhibo Wang,Sirisha Rambhatla,Yuhao Chen,Maria Koshkina,David Clausi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了UT-HMR，一种用于单目3D人体网格恢复的新框架，能够捕捉关节间姿势不确定性，并通过时间早融合注入相邻帧证据以解决歧义问题，取得了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在长摄像机-人体距离、遮挡和运动模糊等弱观察情况下，单目3D人体网格恢复问题的不确定性。

Method: UT-HMR预测了整个SMPL姿态的高斯分布，并使用紧凑的低秩加对角线协方差。将该分布转化为针对异方差监督的关节精度加权，同时保持推断的确定性。通过时间早融合将相邻帧的证据注入姿势解码之前，提供了解决不确定性所需的关键视觉证据。

Result: UT-HMR在VI-PhockeyPose-v2（冰球）和WorldPose（足球）数据集上实现了最先进的结果，分别减少了MPJPE、PA-MPJPE和PVE最高达4.7%、3.8%和5.6%。即使与使用更大骨干网络和更高输入分辨率的方法相比，UT-HMR仍保持竞争力。

Conclusion: UT-HMR通过捕捉关节间姿势不确定性并引入相邻帧证据解决了单目3D人体网格恢复的挑战性问题，达到了卓越的性能水平。

Abstract: Monocular 3D human mesh recovery (HMR) remains unreliable under weak observations caused by long camera-to-person distances, occlusion, and motion blur. In challenging scenarios such as sports, these factors make the problem highly ambiguous, as different 3D poses may yield similar 2D projections. This ambiguity is especially severe for one-stage HMR, which recovers all meshes directly from the full image without person crops or external 2D cues. To handle this ambiguity, we present UT-HMR ( Uncertainty-guided Temporal HMR), to our knowledge the first one-stage HMR framework to capture correlated pose uncertainty across joints. Each person query predicts a Gaussian distribution over the full SMPL pose with a compact low-rank-plus-diagonal covariance, which is converted into joint-wise precision weights for heteroscedastic supervision while keeping inference deterministic. Temporal early fusion further injects neighboring-frame evidence before pose decoding, supplying the visual evidence needed to resolve the ambiguity that the uncertainty captures. With a lightweight ViT-B backbone, UT-HMR achieves state-of-the-art results on VIP-HockeyPose-v2 (ice hockey) and WorldPose (soccer), reducing MPJPE, PA-MPJPE, and PVE by up to 4.7%, 3.8%, and 5.6%, and remains competitive on 3DPW against methods with larger backbones and higher input resolutions.

</details>


### [10] [Diagnosing and Correcting Bias in MLLM for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36210)
*Xusheng Liang,Jianqiao Sun,Hao Zhang,Yulei Niu,Hengshuang Zhao,Jiawei Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了S-MME基准来诊断MLLMs的偏见行为，并提出了一种名为CLEAR的训练-free框架，用于通过仅保留问题或局部显著的视觉片段来估计和减轻偏差影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于长视频中答案依赖于各种分散的关键时刻，MLLMs往往因为记忆限制而无法捕捉到这些关键证据。本文旨在通过设计S-MME基准来诊断偏见行为，并提出CLEAR框架来减轻这一问题。

Method: 该研究通过构建S-MME基准来评估模型的错误依赖，并提出了一个名为CLEAR的框架，通过处理完整视频-问题对并使用掩盖技术来减轻偏见影响，在推理时干预隐藏状态以提升决策的准确性。

Result: 通过在五个基准上的实验研究，作者展示了该方法的普适性和在多种MLLMs中的可合并性，不仅提高了预测准确性，还增强了模型的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文工作表明，通过诊断偏见和使用CLEAR框架，可以在测试时扩大长视频理解的规模，并提升了模型的性能和稳定性。

Abstract: Long-video question answering is challenging since the answer often hinges on a few decisive moments scattered throughout the video, while memory constraints force Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to sample frames at extremely low rates, resulting in sparsity that risks omitting key evidence. Though aggressively enlarging the context size is intuitive, we argue that performance remains fundamentally constrained by the bias, \emph{i.e.}, the misleading cues caused by spurious \textit{linguistic} correlations and salient yet irrelevant \textit{visual} observations. In this paper, we first introduce {S-MME}, a benchmark designed to systematically diagnose shortcut-biased behavior in MLLMs. To mitigate this failure, we further propose a training-free framework, Counterfactual Long-video Evidence-Aware Reasoning (CLEAR). By treating the full video-question pair as a complete view, CLEAR estimates bias effects via augmented views by only keeping the question or locally salient visual clips. Then, we intervene hidden states to mitigate the discrepancy at inference such that the correct answer can be predicted confidently. With experimental studies across five benchmarks, we show that our method is generalizable and can be integrated in different MLLMs, yielding consistent gains in prediction and robustness. We hope CLEAR can offer a complementary direction in test-time scaling for long-video understanding.

</details>


### [11] [Hierarchical Multimodal Interaction Reasoning for Dyadic Relationship Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36249)
*Wang Tang,Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Linbo Qing,Ce Zhu,Hatice Gunes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为HiRe-DRR的框架，它通过层次多模态交互推理方法来识别个体之间的隐含社交关系，相比现有方法，该方法在三个双人关系基准上表现更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在关系识别中往往忽略了不同类型关系所依赖的不同交互模式，而提出的HiRe-DRR框架旨在解决这一问题。

Method: HiRe-DRR采用层次多模态交互推理方法，从特征水平、交互类型和关系层次三个视角组织关系证据。通过Mixture-of-Interaction Transformer和Hierarchical Conditional Loss，能够有效地识别关系相关的证据，同时保持交互背景信息。

Result: 实验表明，HiRe-DRR在三个双人关系基准上表现出色，优于现有图像、视频和多模态方法。

Conclusion: HiRe-DRR框架为双人关系识别带来了新的思路，提供了一种更为精准的交互模式识别方式。

Abstract: Dyadic relationship recognition aims to identify the latent social relationship between two individuals from their multimodal interactions. Existing methods typically project heterogeneous cues into a shared representation and optimise a flat classification objective, overlooking that relationships may rely on different interaction patterns. For instance, distant relationships can often be inferred from direct behavioural correspondence, whereas close relationships are better reflected by coordinated affective, behavioural, and multimodal responses. To address this limitation, we propose HiRe-DRR, a Hierarchical Multimodal Interaction Reasoning framework that organises relationship evidence from three complementary perspectives: feature level, interaction type, and relationship hierarchy. HiRe-DRR decomposes facial, bodily, acoustic, and textual cues into low- and high-level representations and constructs homogeneous and heterogeneous cross-participant interactions. We further introduce the Mixture-of-Interaction Transformer (MoI-Former), which treats diverse interactions as specialised experts and employs relation-aware sparse routing to identify relationship-relevant evidence while preserving contextual interaction information. The proposed Hierarchical Conditional Loss (HC-Loss) exploits the hierarchical nature of social relationships through coarse-to-fine supervision, enabling structured relationship reasoning. Extensive experiments on three dyadic relationship benchmarks demonstrate that HiRe-DRR outperforms recent representative image-, video-, and multimodal methods. Our routing analysis further reveals that distant relationships tend to rely on low-level homogeneous interactions, whereas closer relationships focus on high-level heterogeneous interactions. Our code is publicly available at: https://github.com/tw-repository/HiRe-DRR.

</details>


### [12] [Cross-modal Steering Vectors Exist Across Both Image and Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36269)
*Donghao Zeng,Nishant Subramani,Tamar Rott Shaham,Benno Krojer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析统一跨模态模型（UMMs）中的跨模态引导，发现引导向量在语言和图像之间可以零样本跨模态转移，即使对于高度特定于某一模态的概念也是如此。这表明视觉和语言在统一模型中具有交互作用，并揭示了控制这些模型行为的更广泛、模态无关的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究探讨当前AI模型中概念和机制是否在不同模态间统一，而不仅仅是为每个模态单独实现。

Method: 通过研究跨模态引导，具体包括找到能够引导生成的向量，这些向量可以从一个模态的标记零样本转移到另一个模态引发相似效果的过程。

Result: 研究在多种统一多模态模型中显示出，在不同概念（如情绪和物体大小）情况下，自语言到图像或从图像到语言的跨模态引导具有零样本迁移能力。即使是高度特定于一个模态的概念（如正式和非正式语言），也能在另一个模态中产生一致且可解释的效果，如正式的服饰和设计。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了在统一多模态模型中视觉和语言的交互作用，并指出了控制模型行为的更多通用和模态无关的方法。

Abstract: Are concepts and mechanisms unified across modalities in current AI models, or instead implemented separately for each one? We operationalize this broad question of modality unification by studying cross-modal steering: 1) finding vectors that successfully steer generation on tokens of one modality, which then 2) transfer zero-shot to steer generation when added to tokens of another modality. Our study goes beyond simple correlational alignment (e.g. representational similarity) because steering is fundamentally causal. We study cross-modal steering in several unified multimodal models (UMMs), since they possess shared parameters for language and image generation, focusing on these two modalities. Across diverse steered concepts such as emotion and object size, we show that steering vectors transfer zero-shot in both directions (text-to-image & image-to-text). Even for concepts that are highly unique to one modality (e.g. formal vs. colloquial language), steering vectors often induce a consistent, interpretable effect in the other modality, such as formal clothing and design. This transfer has a geometric signature too: despite a clear modality gap separating text and image representations, steering vectors with strong cross-modal transferability tend to point in similar directions for the same concept. Our findings shed light on how vision and language interact inside unified multimodal models and point towards more general, modality-agnostic approaches for controlling their behavior.

</details>


### [13] [Training-Free Inference Acceleration for Video Large Language Models: Methods, Trade-offs, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36327)
*Yuqi Li,Chuanyue Yan,Neil Rodrigues,Xianglong Wang,Xiaoqin Feng,Junming Liu,Xi Xiao,Zhongwei Cheng,Wei Li,Lin Chen,Yingli Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该摘要概述了训练后推理加速方法，特别关注减少推理成本同时保留任务相关的时间空域证据。研究比较了不同干预阶段对证据保存和计算节省的影响，并通过先前研究和自己的实验结果评估了准确性和延迟之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于视频大型语言模型（VLMs）处理长视觉序列时面临的计算、延迟和内存开销问题，需要研究如何在不进行训练的情况下加速推理过程。

Method: 通过按干预阶段（输入、视觉编码、投影器、预填充和解码）对现有方法进行分类，并探讨训练后量化作为一种跨阶段扩展方法。分析不同干预对证据保存和计算节省的影响。

Result: 分析了不同干预阶段对准确性和延迟的影响，并通过与先前研究和自己实验结果的对比，提供了准确性和延迟之间权衡的量化证据。

Conclusion: 研究表明，局部效率改进不一定能转化为端到端的加速，而跨阶段的训练后量化可能是一个有效的方法来减少推理成本。

Abstract: Video Large Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in video understanding and reasoning, but processing long visual sequences incurs substantial computational, latency, and memory overhead. This survey examines training-free inference acceleration, focusing on reducing inference costs while preserving task-relevant spatiotemporal evidence. We organize existing methods by their intervention stages in the inference pipeline, covering input, visual encoding, projector, prefill, and decoding. Post-training quantization (PTQ) is discussed separately as a cross-stage extension with varying calibration requirements. We analyze how intervention position affects evidence preservation and computational savings, and why local efficiency improvements may not translate into end-to-end acceleration. We complement this analysis with quantitative evidence from prior studies and our own experiments under a shared evaluation setting to assess accuracy-latency trade-offs.

</details>


### [14] [CRLoMTL: Conflict-Resolution through Low-rank matrices for Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36337)
*Ahmed Agiza,Tasneem Shaffee,Rahil Badkul,Sherief Reda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CRLoMTL 提出了一种参数高效框架，通过任务特定的低秩矩阵更新空间解决任务间的冲突，提高了多任务学习模型的准确性和参数效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在多任务学习中往往遇到负迁移问题，CRLoMTL 提出了一种通过低秩矩阵更新空间解决任务冲突的新方法，旨在提高模型效率。

Method: CRLoMTL 通过任务特定的通过低秩矩阵更新空间解决任务冲突，它利用 singular value decomposition (SVD) 提取共同低秩子空间中的或正交基，然后将任务更新投影并合并。

Result: 在 Swin Transformer 上的实验显示，CRLoMTL 在 PASCAL-Context, NYUD-v2, 和 CelebA 上分别提高了 2.77%, 5.03%, 和 3.50% 的准确率，同时仅使用单任务模型参数的一小部分。

Conclusion: CRLoMTL 作为一种有效的参数高效多任务学习方法，解决了传统多任务学习中遇到的负迁移问题。

Abstract: Multi-task learning (MTL) improves model efficiency by sharing representations across related vision tasks, but it often suffers from negative transfer when task updates interfere with one another. Existing conflict-mitigation methods typically operate in gradient space, requiring explicit task-gradient manipulation and additional optimization steps. We propose Conflict-Resolution through Low-Rank Matrices for Multi-Task Learning (CRLoMTL), a parameter-efficient framework that mitigates task interference in the low-rank parameter-update space. CRLoMTL learns task-specific LoRA updates through task-specific passes, where each task updates its own low-rank matrices, and integrates these updates through global shared-subspace fusion. Specifically, we introduce a shared-subspace resolver based on singular value decomposition (SVD), referred to as the SVD resolver, which extracts a common low-dimensional orthonormal basis across task adapters and projects task updates into this shared space before merging them into a single deployment-time update. The resulting update can be folded into the backbone, preserving the inference structure of the original model without additional runtime branches. Experiments with Swin Transformer backbones on PASCAL-Context, NYUD-v2, and CelebA show that CRLoMTL achieves a favorable accuracy-parameter trade-off, improving over single-task baselines by up to 2.77% on PASCAL-Context, 5.03% on NYUD-v2, and 3.50% on CelebA, while training only a small fraction of the parameters used by single-task models.

</details>


### [15] [CrowdCC: Jointly Benchmarking Corruption Robustness and Deployment Quantization in Crowd Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36340)
*S Mahmudul Hasan,Lu Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CrowdCC提供了一个基准，用于评估边缘设备上的行人计数模型在面对输入退化和模型量化时的鲁棒性。该基准使用了23种部署导向的退化，四种人群计数数据集，和26种模型，结合 throughput 和能耗的测量，发现退化和人群密度对模型的性能影响不同，精度变化会影响鲁棒性和成本前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对于群体计数模型针对输入退化和模型量化鲁棒性的评估通常是独立进行的，这导致无法全面考虑实际部署环境中的影响。CrowdCC旨在创建一个基准，用于联合评估这两种影响，为其选型提供有效依据。

Method: CrowdCC使用了23种部署导向的退化，涵盖了六类家庭，并对几何或可见性发生变化时的头部注释进行了调整。实验对比了FP32、FP16和后训练INT8精度下的26种模型，并获得了相应的吞吐量和能耗数据。

Result: 实验结果表明，采集链退化对模型性能的影响最大，而人群密度则影响绝对和相对误差的不同。清洁精度并不能准确预测退化性能。INT8量化在退化鲁棒性上比FP16变化更显著，但没有系统方向性，且不能总是改善延迟和能耗。INT8推断效率与编译引擎中的重格式化开销密切相关，而在有限的INT8覆盖率下也会观察到减速。

Conclusion: CrowdCC的评估显示选择模型时必须同时考虑退化鲁棒性与部署成本。精度的变化会影响鲁棒性成本前沿，强调了结合退化鲁棒性和实际部署成本进行模型选型的重要性。

Abstract: Crowd-counting models deployed on edge devices face two shifts: inputs are degraded along the capture and transmission pipeline, while models are quantized to satisfy resource constraints. Yet corruption robustness and deployment quantization are typically evaluated separately. We introduce CrowdCC, a controlled corruption benchmark that jointly evaluates these effects using 23 deployment-motivated corruptions at three severity levels, four crowd-counting datasets, and 26 models using A100 TensorRT engines at FP32, FP16, and post-training INT8 precision, paired with throughput and energy measurements from corresponding Jetson Orin NX configurations. The corruptions span six families, with head annotations adjusted when geometry or visibility changes. Our evaluation shows that capture-chain corruptions consistently produce the largest degradation across the evaluated models, while crowd density affects absolute and relative error differently. Clean accuracy does not fully predict corrupted performance. INT8 alters corruption robustness substantially more than FP16, with no systematic direction, and does not consistently improve latency or energy. INT8 inference efficiency is strongly associated with reformat overhead in the compiled engine, while slowdowns are also observed under limited INT8 coverage. Precision changes corrupted-error rankings and robustness-cost frontiers, showing why model selection must consider corruption robustness and measured deployment cost together. The code is available at https://github.com/Tulane-Computer-Architecture-Systems/CrowdCC-Release.

</details>


### [16] [BoxWorld: Let World Engines Control and Video Generators Render](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36371)
*Zike Wu,Ahmed Hatem,Chintan Trivedi,Wallace Michel Pinto Lira,Xuebin Qin,Lele Wang,Renjie Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BoxWorld通过分离交互推理和生成渲染，使用户能够定义对象行为并实时控制，同时保持生成历史的连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端视频世界模型学习交互动力学，使得难以指定对象级规则并推理其后果。

Method: BoxWorld引入了一个编码代理来创建Box World Engine，该引擎基于用户指定的规则处理交互结果，并通过轻量级的布局盒、持久对象属性和动态状态变量界面暴露结果状态。同时，引入了一种三阶段训练策略来学习对象基础和状态转换，并进一步提出了PairedStateBench，以评估细粒度交互控制。

Result: BoxWorld能够在实时中控制对象的运动、状态切换和条件事件，并且在多个公开基准测试中表现良好，超过了端到端的世界模型。

Conclusion: BoxWorld通过分离交互推理和生成渲染，提供了一种灵活控制视频世界对象行为的方法。

Abstract: We tackle the challenge of building real-time interactive video worlds with user-defined behaviors. Such worlds must track object states, apply interaction rules, and render the resulting consequences as users act. Yet existing end-to-end video world models typically learn interaction dynamics within the generator, making it difficult to specify object-level rules and reason about their consequences. In this work, we introduce BoxWorld, which separates interaction reasoning from generative rendering. Given user-specified rules, a coding agent creates a Box World Engine that determines interaction outcomes and exposes the resulting object states through a lightweight interface of layout boxes, persistent object attributes, and dynamic status variables, leaving appearance and physical effects to generative rendering. A Box-State Generative Renderer then renders this evolving state stream into video autoregressively, enabling online control over object motion, state switches, and conditional events. Rendering this state stream is non-trivial: the renderer must respond to object-level state updates in real time while remaining coherent with the generated history. To this end, we introduce a three-stage training strategy that learns object grounding from open-domain videos and state transitions from gameplay videos, both annotated automatically, and finally performs rollout distillation on self-generated histories. We further propose PairedStateBench, a paired benchmark for fine-grained interaction control. BoxWorld substantially outperforms end-to-end world models and remains competitive across multiple public benchmarks. Project page: https://boxworld-web.github.io.

</details>


### [17] [Evaluating Federated Vision-Language and Vision-Only Models for Privacy-Aware Breast Cancer Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36379)
*Malithi Mithsara Wanniarachchi Kankanamge,Ning Yang,Sree Bash Debnath,Md Zobaer Islam,Abdur R. Shahid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种结合预训练视觉-语言模型(VLMs)和客户端差分隐私的隐私感知联邦学习框架，用于解决乳腺影像数据分析中的数据有限、数据碎片化和隐私保护问题。实验结果显示，在联邦学习中，所有五种模型均表现出高灵敏度鉴别率，但实际病理识别能力存疑。移除文本路径在保持分类准确性的同时降低了通信成本，而差分隐私则降低了鉴别率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在利用联邦学习在数据共享与隐私保护之间的平衡点，具体克服乳腺影像诊疗领域数据标注不足、机构间数据分布不均及个人隐私保护需求的问题。

Method: 研究利用预训练的CLIP、BiomedCLIP以及ImageNet预训练的ViT-B/16模型，在两个公开的乳腺影像数据集上进行联邦学习实验，通过患者级别的ROC-AUC进行评估，同时进行基础模型对比，阈值敏感性分析和跨模型一致性评估。此外，研究还对文本路径进行去除以观察其对模型效果的影响，并探讨差分隐私对模型性能的具体影响。

Result: 在联邦学习框架下，五种模型均实现高正常与患病图像的鉴别能力（ROC-AUC：0.940-0.990），尽管如此，仅仅基于采集时间即可达到0.974的鉴别率，表明存在混淆因素而非实际病理特征的区分。恶性与良性图像的鉴别结果可靠性不高，ROC-AUC范围为0.504-0.637，所有置信区间均包括0.50。移除文本路径不但维持了分类精度，还将通信成本降低了一半。差分隐私的应用导致ROC-AUC下降0.375，主要由于影响了整个模型状态而非仅限于可训练参数的变化。

Conclusion: 综合研究结果强调了在开发应用于乳腺影像分析的联邦人工智能系统时，必须要评价混淆因素、预测可靠性、隐私保护措施的有效性及部署效率的重要性。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) shows promise for breast imaging, but limited labeled data, institutional data fragmentation, and privacy concerns hinder centralized training. We investigate a privacy-aware federated learning framework combining pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) with client-level differential privacy. We compare general-purpose CLIP models, biomedical BiomedCLIP, and an ImageNet-pretrained ViT-B/16 baseline on two public breast imaging datasets, with and without differential privacy. We also evaluate a CLIP ViT-B/16 ablation that removes the text pathway. Evaluation includes patient-level ROC-AUC with bootstrap 95% confidence intervals, baseline comparisons, threshold sensitivity, inter-model agreement, and communication cost. Under federated learning, all five models achieve high normal-versus-sick discrimination (ROC-AUC: 0.940-0.990). However, acquisition year alone achieves 0.974 on the same test partition, raising concerns about confounding rather than genuine pathology recognition. Benign-versus-malignant discrimination remains uncertain (ROC-AUC: 0.504-0.637), with all confidence intervals including 0.50. Removing the text pathway preserves classification accuracy while halving communication cost. Differential privacy reduces ROC-AUC by 0.375, attributable to perturbing the full model state rather than only trainable parameters. These findings highlight the importance of evaluating confounding, predictive reliability, privacy implementation, and deployment efficiency when developing federated AI systems for breast imaging.

</details>


### [18] [SOMA: A Benchmark for Fine-Grained Egocentric Behavior and Body-Motion Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36384)
*Matea Tashkovska,Naël Ouerghemi,Haozhe Qi,Alexander Mathis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SOMA的新基准，用于评估多模态大型语言模型（MLLMs）在仅基于第一人称视频理解细粒度身体运动的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前评估MLLMs大部分侧重于物体、场景和粗略动作，而忽视了其理解身体本身的性能。特别是在第一人称视角下，身体部分隐藏且运动需要通过遮挡和时间分布的线索进行推理。

Method: 收集1,632个由人类验证的问题，涵盖了身体配置、运动动力学和时间推理三个维度，对37个主流的闭源、开源和专门的第一人称MLLMs进行了评估。

Result: 大多数评估的MLLMs在零样本设置下无法回答这类问题到人类的水平，平均准确率为50.0%，而人类从视频中完成任务的准确率为68.5%。错误分析显示，模型易误读自己身体、时间判断错误以及存在偏见。

Conclusion: 为了缩小与人类的差距，促进进一步研究，该研究提供了标注视频、身体动作和语言指导的数据集，并开发了一个多模态基础模型，以同时学习这些模态。同时，SOMA有望激励未来研究身体运动理解及多模态推理。

Abstract: Perceiving human body motion is critical for multimodal systems that understand and assist people. Yet current evaluations of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) largely focus on objects, scenes, and coarse actions, leaving their ability to reason about the body itself poorly assessed. This is especially challenging from the egocentric perspective, in which the body is only partially visible and motion must be inferred from occluded and temporally distributed cues. We introduce SOMA, a benchmark for fine-grained body-motion understanding from egocentric video, comprising 1,632 human-verified questions across three capability axes: body configuration, motion dynamics, and temporal reasoning. We evaluate 37 leading closed-source, open-source, and specialized egocentric MLLMs, complemented by an extensive human evaluation. We find that humans also find this task difficult from video alone (68.5% accuracy), yet clearly outperform the strongest MLLM (Qwen3.6-27B), which reaches 50.0% zero-shot accuracy. Our error analysis shows that MLLMs often misread the wearer's own body, misjudge events in time, and are prone to biases such as favoring the right hand or under-counting repetitions. To narrow the gap to human performance and support further progress, we introduce a training dataset of aligned video, body motion, and language supervision, and a multimodal baseline that jointly learns from these modalities. By identifying common failure modes and missing capabilities in current MLLMs, we hope SOMA will inspire future research on body-motion understanding and multimodal reasoning. Full benchmark, training data, and code will be released at: https://cnai.epfl.ch/soma

</details>


### [19] [ORBITAL: Optimization of Robust Brain--Computer Inference Through Architecture-Aware Low-Precision Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36411)
*Saim Rehman,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ORBITAL框架通过结合硬件可实现的结构化压缩、恢复感知的操作点选择、多种攻击评估和边缘验证，提升了压缩EEGNet模型的鲁棒性，同时减少了参数和计算量，并且在Jetson Orin Nano上优化了能效和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的EEG解码器在压缩过程中会改变其对抗鲁棒性，ORBITAL框架旨在通过综合考虑硬件限制和攻击性测试，提高压缩EEG解码器的鲁棒性，确保其在嵌入式系统中的实际应用。

Method: ORBITAL框架通过实现结构化压缩、选择恢复感知的操作点、评估多种类型的攻击模式，并结合硬件验证来优化压缩解码器的性能和鲁棒性。

Result: ORBITAL框架在BCI Competition IV-2a数据集上，使用S25-TRADES模型实现了最高的真交鲁棒性（23.06%），参数减少了26.27%，MACs减少了25.10%。在Jetson Orin Nano平台上，FP16压缩可以减少高达16.20%的板载能耗和8.00%的延迟。同时发现，TRades算法是提升鲁棒性恢复的主要来源。

Conclusion: ORBITAL框架不仅提高了压缩EEGNet模型的鲁棒性，还优化了嵌入式设备的能耗和延迟，表明在硬件优化的同时，也需要关注模型的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Compact electroencephalography (EEG) decoders enable embedded brain--computer interfaces (BCIs), but compression can alter adversarial robustness without proportional hardware savings. We present ORBITAL, a robustness-constrained framework combining hardware-realizable structured compression, recovery-aware operating-point selection, multi-attack evaluation, and native edge validation. Across nine subjects and three seeds on BCI Competition IV-2a, S25-TRADES achieves the highest observed mean true-union robustness among compressed EEGNet models (23.06% vs 21.27% for standard Full), with 26.27% fewer parameters and 25.10% fewer MACs. Matched controls identify TRADES as the main source of robustness recovery, improving S25 union accuracy by 6.74 percentage points over clean recovery (p=0.0039), while per-example analysis reveals boundary redistribution rather than uniform margin expansion. On Jetson Orin Nano, FP16 compression reduces board energy by up to 16.20% and latency by 8.00%. Despite 99.3% prediction agreement, native INT8 Q/DQ incurs about twice the FP16 latency and energy, demonstrating the need for joint robustness and hardware-aware optimization.

</details>


### [20] [SpeeDiff: Scalable Pixel-Anchored End-to-End Latent Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36419)
*Bingliang Zhang,Wenda Chu,Yizhuo Li,Linjie Yang,Yisong Yue,Katherine L. Bouman,Yang Song,Qiushan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpeeDiff提出了一种新的latent diffusion方法，通过在VAE和diffusion模型之间进行联合训练，提高了模型的训练效率和生成质量，特别是在大规模架构下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的两阶段方法使用预训练的VAE，效率较低且效果不如联合训练方法。SpeeDiff旨在通过改进的联合训练策略来解决这些问题。

Method: SpeeDiff通过引入Tweedie Pixel Reconstruction (TPR)损失，改善了共同训练过程中的latent空间表现，同时支持了大规模transformer架构的共同放大。

Result: 实验表明，SpeeDiff-XL在生成Imagenet 256x256的图像时比Vanilla SiT快140倍，比REPA快61倍，同时保持了1.50的FID质量。当使用压缩的VAE时，该模型在生成Imagene 512x512的图像时FID达到1.53。

Conclusion: SpeeDiff通过联合训练VAE和扩散模型展示了显著的性能提升，特别是在大模型架构中，证明了一种新的高效生成模型的可行性。

Abstract: We present Scalable Pixel-anchored End-to-end Diffusion (SpeeDiff), a latent diffusion method that jointly trains the VAE and the diffusion model from scratch. In principle, joint training allows the diffusion loss gradient to directly guide the VAE encoder, encouraging the formation of a generation-friendly latent space and potentially yielding faster convergence than the conventional two-stage approach with a pretrained frozen VAE. However, a naive end-to-end implementation severely degrades performance, as unrestricted backpropagation of the diffusion loss leads to latent space collapse. Our main technical contribution is a simple yet effective Tweedie Pixel Reconstruction (TPR) loss, which provides additional pixel-level feedback by decoding a predicted clean latent from an intermediate noisy state using Tweedie's formula, thereby alleviating collapse. Furthermore, our method enables jointly scaling a fully transformer-based architecture and enhances representation alignment within the end-to-end framework. Our SpeeDiff-XL model achieves over 140x and 61x faster training compared to Vanilla SiT and REPA, respectively, while attaining an FID of 1.50 without guidance on ImageNet 256x256 generation. With a more efficient 32x compressed VAE, our model further reaches an FID of 1.53 without guidance on ImageNet 512x512 generation.

</details>


### [21] [SO-101 Bench: Accessible Spatial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36420)
*Truman Hickok*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SO-101 Bench 是一个基于 Isaac Lab 数字孪生的模拟基准，专为语言指导的空间操纵任务设计。它包括四种评估任务、138 个物体的不同类别分割、3629 个模拟演示，并评估了多种策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决现有低成本机械臂如 SO-101 在执行语言条件下的空间操作任务的不足，开发了一个模拟环境，以便更广泛地测试和比较策略。

Method: SO-101 Bench 基于 Isaac Lab 数字孪生环境，设计了四个任务，并使用了各种虚拟对象进行评估。通过模拟环境训练并比较多种策略的表现。

Result: 仿真环境下的策略排名与实际机械臂的排名高度一致（Spearman秩相关系数 = 1.00），但仿真环境可能高估了成功的概率。部分策略的最佳表现达到了约61%的评分，尤其是对于未见过的物体类别和旋转匹配规划任务。

Conclusion: LLM 剂使得效果最好，这是一种结合了代码生成和抓取动作的混合方法，显示出一种独特的优势来源于仿生工具的选择和语言建模的策略。

Abstract: Low-cost arms such as the SO-101 have become the most common entry point to robot learning, yet no released policy performs spatially constrained, language-conditioned tasks on them out of the box, and existing SO-101 benchmarks run only on physical hardware with simple tasks and few held-out objects. We introduce SO-101 Bench, a simulated benchmark for language-conditioned spatial manipulation built on an Isaac Lab digital twin of a real SO-101 setup. It has four tasks scored as five families, 138 objects in seen, unseen-instance and unseen-class splits, 916 evaluation episodes with subsets for held-out appearances, rotate-to-fit planning and occlusion memory, and 3,629 simulated demonstrations on which every learned policy is fine-tuned under an equal compute budget. The twin ranks policies as the real robot does: five policies trained without real data, four VLAs and an LLM agent, keep their exact ranking when the same weights run on the physical arm in an unseen environment with unseen objects (Spearman rho = 1.00, MMRV = 0.000), although simulation overestimates success. Across nine VLAs and world-action models, the best learned policies score near 61%, with spatial placement, unseen object classes and rotate-to-fit planning remaining open. An LLM agent that writes code and hands grasping to a learned policy scores 83.2%, and an ablation shows that this lead comes from the choice of harness and the choice of language model.

</details>


### [22] [FORESEE-MS: Toward Prospective Spatial Forecasting of Future Multiple Sclerosis Lesions from Baseline FLAIR MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36428)
*Maryam Akhavan Aghdam,Sudip Mittal,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究采用FORESEE-MS评估协议，探索FLAIR MRI是否能识别风险区域，结果显示，尽管个别深度学习模型在局部病灶定位上表现更好，但整体而言，基线FLAIR表现良好，未见模型能显著优于其。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了验证基线FLAIR MRI是否能够识别潜在的未来病灶形成区域，并探索其相对于现有方法的优势。

Method: 使用FORESEE-MS评估协议，结合患者级别自举置信区间，采用五分交叉验证对五个深度学习模型进行了评估。

Result: 基线FLAIR在总体体素水平的AUROC和AUPRC上表现最佳，而没有深度学习模型能稳定地在这些度量上超过它。然而，3D U-Net和Attn+ResBlock+VAE在患者的局部病灶定位上表现更好，虽然大多数差异配对置信区间包括零。

Conclusion: 基线FLAIR可能包含了患者特有的预测信息，但在这个小规模的队列中，无法确定这些信息是否能比人口先验图层更好地预测病灶。

Abstract: Traditional multiple sclerosis (MS) image analysis has primarily focused on retrospectively detecting and segmenting lesions. This study investigates whether baseline FLAIR MRI can identify regions at risk for future lesion formation beyond population-level spatial patterns. We introduce FORESEE-MS, an evaluation protocol that uses a fold-specific population-prior atlas as a baseline and assesses predictions using pooled voxel-level discrimination (AUROC and AUPRC) and within-patient lesion localization (Recall@Top-K%), with patient-level bootstrap confidence intervals. We evaluated five deep learning models (3D U-Net, Attn+ResBlock+VAE, ViT, and two SwinUNETR variants) using 5-fold cross-validation on 39 subjects from the MSSEG-2 dataset. The atlas achieved the highest pooled voxel-level AUROC and AUPRC, with no deep learning model reliably outperforming it on either metric. However, the 3D U-Net and Attn+ResBlock+VAE achieved higher mean voxel-level within-patient Recall@Top-K% than the atlas at all evaluated thresholds, although most paired confidence intervals for the differences included zero. These findings suggest that baseline FLAIR may contain patient-specific predictive information, but in this small cohort, it remains uncertain whether this information improves lesion forecasting beyond a population-prior atlas.

</details>


### [23] [LociArt: A Unified Paradigm for Free-Style Local Video Stylization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36456)
*Yuxuan Chen,Feng Xie,Jiagao Hu,Fuhao Li,Zepeng Wang,Fei Wang,Daiguo Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为LociArt的统一框架，用于自由风格的局部视频风格化，明确指定了要风格化的位置及应用的样式，同时保留非目标内容。核心方法GRNStyle基于生成精炼网络（GRN），并通过极坐标样式感知掩码将样式身份嵌入选择区域中，结合编码掩码特征与原始掩码空间支持，整合时空上下文与几何控制。通过开发自动化流水线并构建LociArt-100K数据集以及LociArt基准，实现局部风格化的质性与量化评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前视频风格化方法通常对整个场景进行处理，而局部艺术编辑需要在精确区域内进行风格化操作，涉及风格和位置的精确调用，以及时间的连贯性。LociArt旨在提供一种解决方案，允许用户明确指定风格化位置和样式，同时保留非目标内容，从而解决了现有技术的局限。

Method: LociArt的核心方法GRNStyle基于生成精炼网络，通过极坐标样式感知掩码在选定区域内嵌入样式身份，并结合编码掩码特征与原始掩码的空间支持，整合了时空上下文与几何控制。

Result: 该研究通过开发自动化流水线并构建LociArt-100K数据集，包括100,000个局部风格化对，以及LociArt基准，涵盖80个来源视频和84个目标掩码，共计168个前景/背景风格化案例，提出了一种新的评估方案即样式原型相似度（SPS）来评估局部风格的质量，并提供了非目标内容的保留和时间一致性的补充评估标准。

Conclusion: 实验表明，GRNStyle在局部风格质量、空间控制和时间一致性方面优于现有方法。此外，通过训练单个区域、单个样式对，展示了多个区域的联合风格化，即使这些区域使用了不同的学习样式。这些组成部分共同奠定了可控局部视频风格化学习和评估的基础。

Abstract: Video stylization typically transforms entire scenes, while local artistic editing requires precise region--style association, spatial control, and temporal consistency. We introduce LociArt, a unified paradigm for free-style local video stylization that explicitly specifies where to stylize and what style to apply while preserving non-target content. At its core, GRNStyle builds on Generative Refinement Networks (GRN) and introduces a polar style-aware mask that embeds style identities as equal-norm directions within selected regions. Complementary representation--geometry conditioning combines encoded mask features with raw-mask spatial support, integrating spatiotemporal context with geometric control. To provide paired supervision, we develop an automated pipeline combining global stylization, video object removal, matting, and compositing, and construct the LociArt-100K Dataset with 100K local stylization pairs. We further establish the LociArt Benchmark, comprising 80 source videos and 84 target masks covering 168 foreground/background stylization cases, with a Style Prototype Similarity (SPS) evaluation scheme for regional style quality and complementary measures of non-target preservation and temporal consistency. Experiments show that GRNStyle outperforms existing methods in regional style quality and spatial control while maintaining strong temporal consistency. Qualitative examples further illustrate joint stylization of multiple regions with the same or different learned styles, despite training only on single-region, single-style pairs. Together, these components provide a unified foundation for learning and evaluating controllable local video stylization. Project page: https://higherhu.github.io/lociart/.

</details>


### [24] [Full Collapse Is Suboptimal: Controlled Partial Neural Collapse for Long-Tailed Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36460)
*Hyeontaek Hwang,Cheonghwan Hur,Daeyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受控部分神经塌缩方法，通过Class-Adaptive Sparse Activation (CASA)分类器和ETF-constrained adaptive margin (ETF-AM)损失，允许头部类别崩溃同时保留尾部类别内部多样性，从而提高长尾识别任务的整体准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长尾识别方法通过神经塌缩将特征对齐到固定的等角紧框（ETF），但这会导致尾部类别的样本过度收缩，削弱泛化能力。因此，提出了一种受控的多层次神经塌缩策略，以解决尾部类别过度收缩的问题。

Method: 该方法包括两个部分：Class-Adaptive Sparse Activation (CASA)分类器和ETF-constrained adaptive margin (ETF-AM)损失。CASA通过根据特征进行门控实现类别相关的节点专业化，确保尾部类别占据自己的特征方向。ETF-AM则采用频率自适应的边际，为头部类别设置较小的边际，为尾部类别设置较大的边际。

Result: 在CIFAR100-LT、ImageNet-LT、Places-LT和iNaturalist-2018数据集上，本文的方法在长尾识别任务中取得了最佳的整体准确性。此外，经验分析表明尾部类别的内部散布随类别的降低而增加。

Conclusion: 该方法通过控制特征的塌缩程度，能更有效地处理长尾识别问题。

Abstract: Recent long-tailed recognition (LTR) methods incorporate neural collapse (NC) and align features to a fixed Equiangular Tight Frame (ETF) as a uniform target for all classes. However, forcing tail classes to fully collapse over-contracts their samples and weakens generalization. To address this, we propose a controlled partial neural collapse, realized through two coordinated controls on a backbone: the Class-Adaptive Sparse Activation (CASA) classifier and the ETF-constrained adaptive margin (ETF-AM) loss. Rather than forcing a uniform full collapse, this regime allows head classes to collapse along the ETF while tail classes retain within-class diversity. This prevents the tail from being forced into full collapse, which is suboptimal under imbalance. To set the dimensionality of collapse, CASA induces class-related node specialization through feature-conditioned gating so that tail classes occupy their own feature directions. For the clearance axis, ETF-AM applies a frequency-adaptive margin, with smaller margins for head classes and larger for tail classes. On CIFAR100-LT, ImageNet-LT, Places-LT, and iNaturalist-2018, our method achieves the best overall accuracy over prior methods. In the empirical analysis, within-class spread increases monotonically from head to tail classes, consistent with tail classes retaining variability rather than fully collapsing.

</details>


### [25] [ClusterSplat: Semantic Cluster Selection for 3D Visual Grounding in Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36462)
*Jaesung Lee,Hyeontaek Hwang,Gabriel Manalu,Daeyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ClusterSplat在3D视觉定位中的表现优于现有基线，通过cluster-level的选择机制，能够生成准确的3D目标和2D掩码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在目标选择上依赖于场景和提示变化较大的稠密原始相关得分，这使得它们容易受到启发式阈值或顶比率筛选的影响，导致不稳定的定位。

Method: ClusterSplat通过学习实例感知的高斯特征、形成特征感知的种子体素，并在描述长度标准减少时合并相邻的种子体素，从而形成特定于场景的簇候选集。簇选择由查询条件化的簇评分器通过轻量级MLP完成。

Result: 在ScanRefer上，ClusterSplat在渲染的2D分割和显式3D目标指标上达到最佳效果，在细粒度的参考描述下保持了同样的趋势。在ScanNet v2类别名称查询上，ClusterSplat实现了最强的渲染2D结果和最高的3D匹配准确性，同时保持了竞争力的3D IoU。

Conclusion: ClusterSplat提出了一个有效的3D视觉定位方法，该方法能够更准确地选择和定位3D目标，尤其在场景和描述发生变化时表现优异。

Abstract: We study 3D visual grounding in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), where a referring expression should identify a target object as both a rendered 2D mask and a set of explicit 3D Gaussians. Prior methods typically extract 3D targets by applying heuristic thresholding or top-ratio filtering to dense primitive-level relevance scores. However, these scores often vary substantially across scenes and prompts, making such heuristics sensitive and leading to unstable targeting. ClusterSplat addresses this by reframing 3D target selection as cluster-level selection over scene-adaptive candidates. The method first learns instance-aware Gaussian features, forms feature-aware seed voxels, and merges neighboring seed voxels when a description-length criterion decreases, producing a scene-specific set of cluster candidates. Given a referring expression, a query-conditioned cluster scorer ranks the cluster candidates with a lightweight MLP, and the selected cluster directly defines the explicit 3D target while its cluster score is rendered for the 2D mask. On ScanRefer, ClusterSplat achieves the best rendered 2D segmentation and explicit 3D target metrics among the compared 3DGS baselines. It also obtains the best average Ref-LERF scores, supporting the same trend under fine-grained referring descriptions. On ScanNet v2 class name queries, ClusterSplat achieves the strongest rendered 2D results and the highest 3D matching accuracy, while maintaining competitive 3D IoU. Our project page is available at https://jaesung8.github.io/clustersplat/ .

</details>


### [26] [Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion-Uni: Elastic Self-Refining Unified Diffusion LLM with Cross-Paradigm On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36466)
*Shufan Li,Hanrong Ye,Greg Heinrich,Yonggan Fu,Haotian Zhang,Sifei Liu,Aditya Grover,Pavlo Molchanov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NLD-Uni 是一种统一的离散扩散大语言模型，通过引入统一的跨范式在策略蒸馏（OPD）框架和渐进式自我精炼扩散过程，实现了视觉理解和生成任务的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的统一扩散模型在视觉理解和生成任务上存在局限，NLD-Uni 旨在通过创新性设计克服这些局限，提高综合性能。

Method: 该模型通过两种主要创新：1. 统一的跨范式在策略蒸馏（OPD）框架；2. 渐进式自我精炼扩散过程实现了目标。OPD框架能够迁移自回归视觉语言模型（VLMs）和连续流模型的视觉理解与生成能力至统一的离散扩散模型（DLLM）。渐进式自我精炼扩散过程在训练过程中逐步使每个token失效，以避免直接从干净图像 token 腐蚀到无信息掩码 token。

Result: 经过广泛的实验，NLD-Uni 在包括视觉问答、多模态推理、物体定位、文本到图像生成和图像编辑等多样任务中实现了统一离散扩散模型的最佳性能。

Conclusion: 这种统一的离散扩散大语言模型（NLD-Uni）展现了强大的视觉理解和生成能力，有望在这些领域中带来显著的进步。

Abstract: We propose Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion-Uni (NLD-Uni), a unified discrete diffusion large language model (DLLM) for visual understanding and generation tasks. Compared with prior unified diffusion models, NLD-Uni introduces two major innovations. First, we propose a unified cross-paradigm on-policy distillation (OPD) framework that successfully transfers the visual understanding ability of autoregressive VLMs and visual generation ability of continuous flow models, to a unified DLLM. Second, NLD-Uni incorporates a novel progressive self-refining diffusion process that gradually corrupts each token across multiple steps during training, as opposed to adopting the conventional masked diffusion formulation that directly corrupts a clean image token to an uninformative mask token. At inference, this design allows NLD-Uni to make intermediate commit to a token, enabling progressive self-refinement for better visual quality. Through extensive experiments, we show that NLD-Uni achieves state-of-the-art performance among unified DLLMs on diverse tasks including visual question-answering, multi-modal reasoning, object grounding, text-to-image generation, and image editing.

</details>


### [27] [Confident First, Reflective Second: Reinforcement Learning of Confidence-Preserving Reflection for Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36467)
*Zihao Lin,Jingyuan Liu,Yucheng Zhang,Yuguang Yao,Lifu Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种防止模型在处理视频时自信度下降的方法CoFire，通过奖励激励、参数漂移和梯度干扰机制来对抗自信度下降的问题，最终在多个数据集上取得了更好的初始和最终准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的视频模型可能错过关键时刻，依赖于对视频的多次审视。研究发现，强化学习（RL）可能会降低模型的初始置信度，因此提出了CoFire方法以增强初始模型的自信度。

Method: CoFire方法通过三种机制来防止自信度下降：奖励激励以提高初期置信度；参数漂移机制来防止模型参数随时间变化而变得不可靠；梯度干扰来防止模型优化过程中的干扰。

Result: CoFire方法在Video-MME、MLVU和LongVideoBench数据集上分别取得了67.0%的平均准确率，比最强的7B方法提高了1.7分，并且与基于结果奖励的RL方法相比，在初始准确率和最终准确率上分别提高了4.1分和3.7分。

Conclusion: CoFire方法通过增强初始置信度，成功解决了视频处理过程中模型自信度下降的问题，提高了模型的整体准确率并促进了更好的结果生成。

Abstract: Video models answer from sampled frames and may miss the decisive moment. A common remedy is to commit to an answer, re-examine the video, and confirm or revise it, a loop now mainly learned with reinforcement learning (RL). We find that RL erodes the initial answer, a phenomenon we term confidence erosion, where confidence means the label-verified reliability of the initial answer instead of self-reported certainty: because errors can be repaired later, a correct initial answer earns less reward, and the initial answer stalls even as the final answer's gain over it rises. We propose the principle confident first, reflective second: the initial answer must stay reliable on its own, above a floor set by a reference policy, and reflection may only add to it. Our method, CoFire, implements this principle with one mechanism for each erosion channel: reward incentives, parameter drift, and gradient interference. CoFire reaches 67.0% mean accuracy on Video-MME, MLVU, and LongVideoBench, 1.7 points above the strongest compared 7B method, and improves initial-answer and final accuracy over outcome-reward RL by 4.1 and 3.7 points on average: a stronger initial answer carries through to a stronger final one.

</details>


### [28] [Spatial-Frequency Learning for Scanning Transmission Electron Microscopy Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36487)
*Hesong Li,Ying Fu,Ziqi Wu,Ruiwen Shao,Tao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种空间-频率学习框架来增强STEM图像，通过在频域中利用周期性原子排列并恢复局部原子细节，同时构建了一个经过噪声校准的数据集来提高原子定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于STEM成像过程中受到多种退化的影响，包括扫描噪声、点噪声和背景噪声等，现有的深度学习方法在严重退化情况下的性能仍有局限性。为了克服这一问题，研究提出了一种创新的空间-频率学习框架。

Method: 研究设计了一个空间-频率融合网络，分别在空间域和频域增强特征，然后进行融合。同时，研究提出了一种STEM噪声校准方法，通过统计分析和现实STEM图像的拟合来估计扫描噪声、点噪声和背景噪声的参数，进而构建了一个包含了更宽泛原子排列、成像模式和退化类型的多样化数据集。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在合成数据集和先进材料的真实图像上实现了最先进的增强性能，并且提高了一旦原子的定位准确性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的方法为STEM图像的增强提供了一种新的解决方案，有助于更好地观察材料的原子结构。

Abstract: Scanning Transmission Electron Microscopy (STEM) enables the observation of atomic arrangements at sub-angstrom resolution and plays a key role in the discovery and characterization of advanced materials. Raw STEM images are often noisy and blurred due to multiple degradations. Although recent deep learning methods improve STEM images, their performance remains limited under severe degradations. In this work, we propose a spatial-frequency learning framework for STEM image enhancement. Specifically, we design a spatial-frequency fusion network that exploits periodic atomic arrangements in the frequency domain and restores local atomic details in the spatial domain. Spatial- and frequency-domain features are enhanced separately and then fused. We also present a STEM noise calibration method for synthesizing more realistic STEM images. The parameters of scan noise, pointwise noise, and background noise are estimated through statistical analysis and fitting of real STEM images. Using these calibrated parameters, we construct a diverse dataset containing a wider range of atomic arrangements, imaging modes, and degradations. Random masks combine different atomic arrangements to simulate interfaces between multiple materials. Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art enhancement performance on synthetic datasets and real images of advanced materials while improving atomic localization accuracy.

</details>


### [29] [GarmentSegAgent: Autonomous Garment Segmentation for Web Fashion Images via Textual Self-Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36506)
*Jia Chen,Chao Huang,Xinrong Hu,Mingdi Huang,Ruyang Zhao,Jun Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GarmentSegAgent 是一种自主框架，通过迭代的文本自校正机制（CAST）来纠正服装分割中的视觉错误，该机制连接了视觉错误归因与目标文本优化，从而在不依赖人类提供的空间提示的情况下，实现服装图像特定错误的实时修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网页上的时尚图像由于视觉多样性导致可靠的服装分割变得困难，现有的提示式分割方法仍难以自主识别和更正服装特定的错误。

Method: GarmentSegAgent 使用迭代的文本自校正机制（CAST），通过约束归因指导的选择性文本梯度连接视觉错误归因与目标文本优化。

Result: 实验结果表明，GarmentSegAgent 在 DeepFashion2, ModaNet 和 Fashionpedia 上均表现出在区域重合度和边界准确度上的改进，并且比传统 TextGrad 提供了更可靠的细化。

Conclusion: GarmentSegAgent 为自主理解服装提供了一条有希望的方向，未来可能应用于可扩展的时尚内容分析、视觉检索和网络上的消费者到商店的匹配。

Abstract: Web fashion images support applications such as visual product search and consumer-to-shop matching, yet their visual heterogeneity makes reliable garment segmentation challenging. Despite advances in promptable segmentation, autonomously identifying and correcting garment-specific errors without human-provided spatial prompts remains difficult. We propose GarmentSegAgent, an autonomous framework that integrates semantic planning, spatial execution, and visual evaluation through iterative textual self-refinement. At its core, we introduce CAST (Constrained Attribution-guided Selective TextGrad), which connects visual error attribution with targeted textual optimization. CAST identifies instruction components responsible for segmentation errors, selectively updates them through textual gradients, and validates the resulting instructions against garment-specific constraints. This enables image-specific error correction at inference time while keeping the underlying foundation models frozen. Experiments on DeepFashion2, ModaNet, and Fashionpedia demonstrate consistent improvements in region overlap and boundary accuracy, alongside more reliable refinement than conventional TextGrad. By enabling visual feedback to guide controlled instruction updates, GarmentSegAgent offers a promising direction for autonomous garment understanding, with potential applications in scalable fashion content analysis, visual retrieval, and consumer-to-shop matching on the Web.

</details>


### [30] [ARISE: Authentic Reference-Informed Stability Enhancement for Continual Multimodal Forgery Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36509)
*Yuchen Zhang,Yaxiong Wang,Yujiao Wu,Lianwei Wu,Qi Zhang,Li Zhu,Chaofeng Dong,Le Wu,Zhedong Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Fable-Bench的新基准，它通过互补联合模态、文本和图像协议组织欺骗攻击的演变。同时，提出了ARISE方法来处理不断出现的新欺骗，方法包含使用几何信息保持稳定性的GRASP和估计脆弱阶段的VAR策略。实验结果表明，该方法在现有持续学习基线下表现出更好的历史保留和最终平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法通常基于静态假设，即训练攻击将接近部署后的实际攻击。然而，随着欺骗机制和证据的不断变化，这种方法逐渐变得不够稳健。本文提出了一种攻击增量的持续多模态欺诈检测方法，以适应新攻击的学习而不遗忘旧攻击。

Method: 提出了一种名为ARISE的方法，它包括一个使用图像和文本模态历史真实数据的紧凑高斯混合模型作为参考，以及一个自适应的各向异性度量，该度量以历史稳定向量为主导。此外，引入了Vulnerability-Aware Replay (VAR)，用于估计不同阶段的脆弱性，从而调整回放的权重。

Result: 在三个攻击演化协议和多个检测器架构上进行的实验表明，ARISE方法在持续学习基线下表现出更好的历史保留和最终平均性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的Fable-Bench和ARISE方法为持续多模态欺诈检测提供了新的思路和方法，并展示了在实际应用中的潜在价值。

Abstract: Multimodal forgery detection is commonly studied under a static assumption: training attacks are expected to approximate those encountered after deployment. This assumption becomes increasingly fragile as generators, editing systems, and multimodal large language models continually introduce new forgery mechanisms and forensic evidence. We formulate this problem as attack-incremental continual multimodal forgery detection, where the binary real/fake interface remains fixed while emerging attacks must be learned without forgetting previous ones. We introduce Fable-Bench, which organizes attack evolution through complementary joint-modality, text-side, and image-side protocols. We further propose ARISE (Authentic Reference-Informed Stability Enhancement), motivated by the open-ended forged class and the comparatively reusable geometry of authentic content. ARISE fits a real-only Gaussian mixture model to projected authentic replay representations as a compact historical reference. Meanwhile, the proposed Gaussian-Responsibility-Aware Stability Preservation (GRASP) derives a sample-adaptive anisotropic metric that strongly constrains historically stable representation directions while retaining plasticity elsewhere. Our Vulnerability-Aware Replay (VAR) further estimates stage-wise vulnerability from authentic-density degradation, forged-to-authentic boundary confusion, and authentic-mode occupancy drift, then assigns bounded replay emphasis to fragile stages. All adaptive signals are obtained from training-time replay statistics, while inference uses only the original multimodal backbone and binary classification head. Experiments across three attack-evolution protocols and multiple detector architectures demonstrate improved historical retention and final average performance over representative continual-learning baselines.

</details>


### [31] [Identity or Evidence? Auditing Evidence Dependence in Agentic Life-Science Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36510)
*Danai Brilli,Maria Georgila*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了临床结果基准以及BioMNi和LAB-Bench发布的任务中，身份信息在不同提示类型下的影响。结果表明，当提示主要是命名实体和候选列表时，盲化身份对性能有显著影响；而在包含关键序列或段落的提示中影响较小。研究建议，旨在测量以证据为基础的推理的基准应报告盲化身份的结果、检索遥测数据和正向控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者希望通过分析临床结果基准以及BioMNi和LAB-Bench发布的任务，确定身份信息对模型性能的影响差异，从而推进更公平和有效的模型评估。

Method: 研究通过独立变化身份信息和证据来检验模型的推理能力。使用96个LAB-Bench LitQA2问题以及一个特定的片段删除方案在临床基准测试上评估模型的性能差异。并通过无证据召回探针检查预测准确性和数据库特定程序计数之间的匹配情况。

Result: 在结果上，研究发现当提示主要是命名实体和候选列表时，盲化身份能显著提升模型的性能；而在包含关键序列或段落的提示中，这种盲化效果减少。同时，研究观察到一个固定的片段删除方案下96个问题的性能差异有所增加，且每个实例的斜率为0.147，P值为0.004。此外，在临床基准上，无证据召回探针匹配到预测准确性和数据库特定程序计数。

Conclusion: 研究得出结论，旨在衡量证据依赖推理能力的基准应报告盲化身份的结果、检索遥测数据和正向控制。这有助于避免由于身份信息带来的偏差，促进更公正科学的评价模型性能。

Abstract: Agentic life-science benchmarks give an agent evidence about a named entity and score an answer against a recorded outcome. That score tests reasoning over evidence only if the evidence, rather than the name, determines the answer. We examine this distinction by varying identity and evidence independently on a clinical-outcome benchmark and published tasks from Biomni and LAB-Bench. Blinding identity matters little when a prompt contains a decisive sequence or passage. It matters a great deal when the prompt is mostly a name and candidate list (0.742 named versus 0.082 blinded; chance 0.093). Across 96 LAB-Bench LitQA2 questions, the gap increases under one fixed passage-redaction scheme (+0.000 to +0.135; per-instance slope +0.147, p= 0.004). On the clinical benchmark, a no-evidence recall probe matches prediction accuracy and recovers a database-specific programme count. So, we argue that benchmarks intended to measure evidence-dependent reasoning should report identity-blinded results, retrieval telemetry, and positive controls.

</details>


### [32] [Frugal Handwritten Text Recognition for Sub-Saharan Civil-Status Registers: A Smartphone-to-Records Pipeline with CPU-Only Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36540)
*Ousmane Hamadou,Jean Louis Kedieng Ebongue Fendji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究介绍了一种经济高效的管道，使用智能手机图像及消费者级预处理和仅CPU推断，成功将赤道几内亚市政登记处的手写出生、结婚和死亡记录转化为机器可读格式，尽管存在少量识别错误，但仍表明在单个登记处中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非洲部分地区缺乏高效数字化手段的情况下，研究提出了一种新的管道方法，利用智能手机拍摄的图像和低成本的预处理和仅CPU推断技术，以解决手写记录内容不可搜索的问题。

Method: 研究使用了一个包含1,568张手机拍摄的手写记录的语料库，并引入了一个结合完全经典预处理阶段（约2.6张图像/秒）和使用预训练模型进行微调的识别器的新管道。该识别器在六核笔记本电脑CPU上仅占用约61 MiB的空间，峰值内存使用量低于2.6 GiB，平均每张图像处理时间不到6秒。

Result: 经过微调的识别器在处理文字和单词错误率方面显著优于预训练模型，字符错误率从14.53%降至0.50%，单词错误率从56.93%降至3.37%。进一步下游信息提取任务表明，即使存在少量识别错误，但对于职务解析这一任务，仍能取得9个F1分数。

Conclusion: 研究表明，使用有限的计算资源和技术，依然可以在市政登记处实现手写记录的机器可读性，但还存在一定的局限性，如对于未见作家或地点的表现尚未得到验证。

Abstract: Handwritten civil-status registers across Sub-Saharan Africa are being photographed, but rarely transcribed, which leaves their content unsearchable. Local administrations that hold these records typically lack GPUs, reliable connectivity, and the budget for external digitization services. We study whether a frugal pipeline, with consumer-grade capture and CPU-only inference, can make such registers machine-readable. We introduce a corpus of 1,568 smartphone images of handwritten birth, marriage, and death records from a municipal registry in Chad, with line-level transcriptions for 185 records. Our pipeline combines a fully classical preprocessing stage, running at about 2.6 images per second on a laptop CPU, with a Kraken recognizer fine-tuned from a pretrained model in about four hours on a free-tier GPU. On manually corrected line segmentation and a single-registry test set whose writers also appear in training, fine-tuning reduces the character error rate from 14.53% to 0.50% and the word error rate from 56.93% to 3.37%. On a six-core laptop CPU, the recognizer weighs 61 MiB, peaks below 2.6 GiB of RAM, and processes an image in under 6 s on average, excluding line segmentation. Finally, a downstream information-extraction task shows that residual recognition errors cost about 9 F1 points on role resolution, compared with a 36.7-point gap between the fine-tuned and pretrained recognizers. These results are evidence of feasibility within one registry, not of performance on unseen writers or sites; we discuss these limitations in detail.

</details>


### [33] [FrameForge: Harnessing Priors of Video Foundation Models for Unified Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36545)
*Yijing Lin,Zhendong Mao,Mengqi Huang,Yuxin Wang,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FrameForge 提出了一种通过输入自适应的虚拟时间关系来建模条件生成的新方法，包括 MoTE 和 CoTR 两个模块，实验表明其在主题驱动生成、边缘图生成和图像编辑任务中显著优于其他方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的条件图像生成方法通常将条件和目标分配到固定的时序位置，而 FrameForge 则旨在通过建模输入自适应的时间关系来克服这一限制，从而使模型能够灵活地处理不同时间尺度的视觉变换。

Method: FrameForge 使用 Mixture of Temporal Experts (MoTE) 和 Chain of Temporal Reasoning (CoTR) 两个模块来捕捉时间关系。MoTE 通过动态选择不同虚拟间隔的 RoPE 专家来预测目标与条件之间的时序距离。CoTR 则通过隐状态建模条件到目标的转换过程，并使用注意力掩码促进信息通过该路径的传播。

Result: 实验证明 FrameForge 在多个任务上表现出色，包括主题驱动生成、边缘图生成和图像编辑，优于其他已比较的方法。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种新的框架 FrameForge，通过建模输入自适应的时间关系来实现条件生成，展示了其在多种图像生成任务中的优越性能。

Abstract: Unified conditional image generation requires a single model to preserve different aspects of a visual input while producing a target image that follows textual guidance. Pretrained video models provide a promising foundation by encoding visual transformations across time, yet existing approaches typically assign the condition and target to fixed temporal positions. We observe that condition-target relationships vary substantially across generation tasks, while video models naturally encode visual transformations at different temporal scales. This motivates us to model conditional generation through input-adaptive virtual temporal relations. We propose FrameForge, which captures two complementary aspects of such relations. Mixture of Temporal Experts (MoTE) models how far the target lies from the condition by dynamically selecting temporal RoPE experts with different virtual intervals. Chain of Temporal Reasoning (CoTR) models how the condition is transformed into the target through a latent intermediate state, with a reasoning-bottleneck attention mask encouraging information propagation through this pathway. Experiments on DreamBench, COCO2017, and GEdit-Bench show that FrameForge achieves superior performance in subject-driven generation, canny-to-image generation, and image editing among the compared methods.

</details>


### [34] [PRISM-BENCH: Diagnosing Grounded and Faithful Multimodal Reasoning Beyond Final-Answer Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36554)
*Vansh Bhatia,Mehdi Rezagholizadeh,Parsa Ashrafi Fashi,Igor Gilitschenski,Vikram Appia,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRISM-BENCH 基准测试提出了1000个专家选择的问题，覆盖了五个未充分代表的推理类别。每个问题附有理想化的推理路径，可以用来诊断准确率无法提供的模型失败原因。研究发现，近85%的前沿模型失败是由于感知失误，并且指出瓶颈在于语义映射。Trace Alignment 是一种可以预测答案正确性的诊断工具，不依赖于答案的长度。引入的一种新的效率感知度量 CORE 在模型排序与响应长度无关的同时，在25个系统中提升了24.2%的排序结果。总体而言，即使是最先进的系统，准确率最好的也只有66.0%，四个类别中最好的系统也比人类低18.4分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于传统的最终答案准确率无法衡量模型的真实推理内容、感知误差、成本等因素，因此需要开发新的评估基准来更全面地评估模型性能。

Method: PRISM-BENCH通过设计新问题，并提供包含理想推理路径的理想答案，来评估模型的推理能力和效率。

Result: PRISM-BENCH成功识别了感知错误的原因成为主要的失败点，并提出了 Trace Alignment 诊断工具，能够评估模型的真实推理能力而不受答案长度影响。同时引入的 CORE 指标提高了模型排序结果，并且与响应长度没有相关性。研究表明，当前模型的最新进步主要是基于识别能力，而非空间感知。

Conclusion: PRISM-BENCH 提供了一个新的基准来更全面地评估模型性能。虽然有一些模型取得了进步，但在某些领域与人类仍存在较大差距。

Abstract: Final-answer accuracy reports whether a vision-language model was right, but not whether its answer was grounded in the image, whether its stated reasoning reflects that evidence, or what the answer cost to produce. Those are the properties that decide whether a system can be deployed. We present PRISM-BENCH, a benchmark of 1,000 expert-curated questions spanning five under-represented reasoning categories, in which every item carries an idealized reasoning trace written by one human annotator and validated by another. The traces support two diagnostics that accuracy cannot provide. A perception probe, in which the model answers from a description it wrote itself, attributes roughly 85% of frontier-model failures to misperception and places the bottleneck at grounding. Trace alignment predicts answer correctness for all 25 systems tested (macro AUROC 0.66) and survives length control, so it measures reasoning content and not verbosity. We further introduce CORE, an efficiency-aware metric that reorders 24.2% of model pairs relative to accuracy while remaining uncorrelated with response length. Across 25 open and closed systems, including GPT-5 and Claude Opus 4.6, the best accuracy is 66.0%. On the four categories with a human baseline the best system trails humans by 18.4 points, and recent frontier gains fall on recognition and not on spatial grounding.

</details>


### [35] [Decoupling Consistency and Conflict across MLLM Layers for Fake News Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36569)
*Kai Qian,Dongjie Xu,Weijie Shi,Jiaqi Wang,Haolun Ma,Jia Zhu,Jiajie Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 文章提出了一种新的方法MLCCD，利用多模态大型语言模型的不同层来捕捉不同层次的矛盾和一致性，从而提高对假新闻的检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的方法通常以粗略和静态的方式处理一致性与矛盾，无法捕捉稀疏矛盾并适应不同层次的变化。

Method: MLCCD 方法包含三个步骤：1) 使用 MLLM 的最终决策表示选取与任务相关的视觉和文本标记；2) 保留层间表示变化剧烈的标记以捕捉各层增加的知识；3) 将这些知识分解为一致性和矛盾性证据并逐层聚合。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上，MLCCD 方法优于现有方法。

Conclusion: MLCCD 可以更准确地检测多模态虚假新闻，因为它能够灵活适应不同层次的矛盾水平。

Abstract: The trustworthiness of Web content faces a growing threat from multimodal fake news. Real news usually shows aligned image-text semantics, while fake news often contains a few conflicts at varying levels, such as mismatches in objects, scenes, events, and claims. However, existing methods usually treat consistency and conflict in a coarse and static way, limiting their ability to capture sparse conflicts and adapt to varying conflict levels. Inspired by the distinct knowledge encoded across layers in large language models, we propose Multi-Level Consistency and Conflict Decoupling (MLCCD), which captures conflicts at different levels from different layers of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Specifically, we first use the MLLM's final decision representation to select visual and textual tokens relevant to the task, preventing irrelevant content from diluting sparse conflicts. Then, we preserve tokens whose representations change sharply between adjacent layers, capturing the knowledge each layer adds. Finally, we decouple this knowledge into consistency and conflict evidence and aggregate each across layers with its own router, adapting to varying conflict levels. Experiments on three benchmarks show that MLCCD outperforms existing methods.

</details>


### [36] [LabWorld: Learning Interactive 4D Worlds for Scientific Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36592)
*Suozhi Huang,Kelsey Fu,Binxu Li,Luna Lyu,Kaize Wu,Wenbo Lu,David Smerkous,Jiahan Li,Siqi Zhu,Zhizhou Sha,Jiacheng Guo,Zhixuan Liang,Shilong Liu,Le Cong,Mengdi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了LabWorld框架，用于从单目RGB视频重建交互式4D实验室世界，实现了实验环境的3D资产、度量场景、物体状态和事件的恢复，通过与基准线对比，展示了有效性和优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的实验室模拟器依赖手工构建的资产和预定义的步骤，而实验室视频则记录了实际设备、布局和交互，但这些信息是显式的。因此，需要一种方法来从视频中重建实验室环境，并使其能够被自主智能体理解和使用。

Method: LabWorld框架结合多帧观测的互补证据进行资产和场景重建，利用运动恢复的视觉和几何观测，结合事件约束，以满足实验室交互的空间和时间需求。

Result: 在真实离心和移液视频上评估了LabWorld，对于移液，LabWorld实现了0.638的平均管长归一化PCK-AUC，比最强的基准线高0.035。对于旋转，则实现了0.501的平均掩码IoU，比最强的基准线高0.033。此外，对于五个移液视频，事件条件下的重建使模型的物理限制满足的比例从14.3%提升到了100%。

Conclusion: LabWorld框架能够从单目RGB视频中重建交互式4D实验室世界，并为未来的实体化科学系统提供了显式的、可编辑的状态。

Abstract: Autonomous scientific agents must not only reason about experiments but also interact with the physical environments in which they are executed. Existing laboratory simulators typically rely on manually constructed assets and predefined procedures, while laboratory videos capture real equipment, layouts, and interactions but leave this information implicit in pixels. We introduce LabWorld, an agent-driven Real2Sim framework for reconstructing interactive 4D laboratory worlds from monocular RGB video. LabWorld recovers reusable 3D assets, a metric scene, time-varying object states, and procedure events. Multi-frame observations provide complementary evidence for asset and scene reconstruction, while motion reconstruction combines visual and geometric observations with event constraints encoding the spatial and temporal requirements of laboratory interactions. We evaluate LabWorld on real pipetting and vortexing videos collected in our laboratory against general-purpose agentic and dedicated reconstruction baselines. For pipetting, LabWorld achieves a mean Mask IoU of 0.501, exceeding the strongest baseline by 0.033. For vortexing, it achieves a mean Tube-Length-Normalized PCK-AUC of 0.638, exceeding the strongest baseline by 0.035.
