Extensive experiments reveal that while leading proprietary MLLMs demonstrate promising code captioning capabilities, they still struggle to faithfully capture complex temporal dynamics, with open-source models lagging substantially behind.

</details>


### [109] [TimeLineXR: An Interactive Exploration of Time in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37349)
*Nidham Tekaya,Markus Passecker,Matthias Zeppelzauer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TimeLineXR的交互演示系统，将时间轴转换为可探索的3D场景，用户可以上传图像并在颜色编码的3D时间轴上查看，通过CLIP编码、余弦核KPCA投影和Bézier时间轴上的Bézier曲线对齐，使用户能够比较不同物体的时间位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Vision-Language模型虽然可以量化地估计物体的时间原点并编码时间信息，但这些结构对用户不透明，用户无法直观地查看和使用这些时间信息。因此，本文开发了一个TimeLineXR系统，旨在使时间信息更加直观易用。

Method: TimeLineXR系统使用CLIP对图像进行编码，通过余弦核KPCA降维投影，并将其映射到Bézier时间轴上。用户可以上传或选择图像，并在3D时间轴上看到它们的位置，该系统提供Web和WebXR版本，支持缩放和查询。

Result: 用户可以将图像上传到TimeLineXR系统，并在3D时间轴上看到它们的时间位置。Web或WebXR版本均可提供交互体验，使用户能够直观地比较不同图像的时间信息。

Conclusion: 本文提出TimeLineXR系统，通过将时间轴可视化和交互化，使得Vision-Language模型中的时间理解更加直观易用，为用户提供了一种新的时间分析方法。

Abstract: Recently, it has been shown that vision-language models (VLMs) have a certain intrinsic understanding of time. They can estimate the temporal origin of objects and encode time along a low-dimensional, non-linear manifold inside their embedding space~\cite{tekaya2025matter}. That result is quantitative and reproducible, but the structure itself stays out of reach for end users: they cannot see it, query it with their own images, or inspect where a query lands relative to other years on the time axis. We present TimeLineXR, an interactive demonstration that turns this time axis into an explorable 3D scene. A web demo lets users upload or select images and see each placed on a color-coded 3D time axis; a Three.js WebXR scene renders the same time axis as a room-scale representation that can be grabbed, scaled, inspected, and queried by throwing image cards into it. Both clients run the same prediction pipeline against the same cached temporal index: encode the query with CLIP, project it through cosine-kernel KPCA, and snap it to the Bézier time axis. The system turns each image into a point on the time axis, so users can compare where different objects land and read off their estimated first year of appearance.Code source and reproduction steps can be found in: https://github.com/sterngefeuert/timeline-xr.

</details>


### [110] [CTBakeGS: training-free baking of photorealistic CT volume renderings into 3D Gaussian splats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37406)
*Haruki Fukuda,Constantin Lux,Wolf Schweitzer,Tadashi Hosoya,Lars Ebert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个无需训练过程即可将CT体积渲染直接转换为3DGS模型的方法，该方法能在普通计算机上实现实时显示，且在骨骼、对比增强血管和皮肤骨骼的PSNR值分别为32.6、30.1和32.3，具有足够的保真度用于交互式查看。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于3D高斯点云计算的实时场景显示方法需要大量时间进行训练，而CT体积渲染则依赖于专用软件和高性能图形处理器，本研究旨在寻找一种既能实现实时显示又能保留细节的方法。

Method: 研究中提出了一种将CT体积渲染直接转换为3DGS模型的算法。通过在毫米级细胞的自适应八叉树上放置点，并利用着色器中的光照计算颜色和透明度，将细胞透明度解析转换为点的透明度，并且对透明度低于1/255的点进行随机保留。

Result: 研究将六种公开的CT扫描图像转换为模型，并与收敛参考进行了比较。结果显示，百万级点模型的峰值信噪比分别是：骨骼32.6 dB，对比增强血管30.1 dB，皮肤骨骼32.3 dB。模型使用笔记本电脑通常在4-30秒内完成转换，使用带有4GB显卡的台式机则需9-60秒。在笔记本电脑上，以800x800像素渲染时，模型可以达到每秒68帧。

Conclusion: 此研究提出的无需训练过程、能在普通计算机上实现实时显示CT图像的方法已达到了足够的保真度，适用于交互式查看，证明了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Photorealistic volume rendering of computed tomography (CT) may convey anatomy intuitively, but it needs the CT data, dedicated software and a powerful graphics processor. Three-dimensional Gaussian splatting (3DGS) displays photorealistic scenes in real time on less powerful devices, but existing medical approaches train each scene for minutes to hours. We aimed to convert a CT volume rendering directly into a 3DGS model without training. Splats were placed on an adaptive octree of millimetre-sized cells. Their colour and opacity were computed on the graphics processor with the renderer's lighting, cell opacity was converted analytically into splat opacity, and faint splats were retained stochastically above the 1/255 opacity floor of standard 3DGS rasterisers. Six public CT scans (1.5-mm voxels) were converted with three transfer functions and compared with converged references by peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR), structural similarity and a learned perceptual metric. Two-million-splat models reached a PSNR of 32.6 +/- 0.9 dB for bone, 30.1 +/- 1.0 dB for contrast-enhanced vessels and 32.3 +/- 1.5 dB for skin and bone. A conversion pass (usually two or three per model) took 4-30 s on a current laptop and 9-60 s on a desktop with a 4-GB graphics card. On the laptop, the models rendered at 68 frames per second at 800 x 800 pixels. Training-free conversion produced real-time 3DGS models of photorealistic CT renderings in about a minute or less per conversion pass on ordinary computers, with fidelity that we consider sufficient for interactive viewing.

</details>


### [111] [Segmentation Confidence for Arbitrary CNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37431)
*Baris Oguz,Xing Yao,Claudia Tawil,Alison Pouch,Brett Byram,Gabriel Arenas,Nadav Schwartz,Ipek Oguz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了用于医用图像分割的卷积神经网络的不确定性评估方法，提出了一种无需重新训练即可添加到现有分割流水线中的体素级不确定性评估方法，并提出了一种从不确定性图估计单一置信度得分的新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的置信度估计方法通常需要定制网络架构和/或专门的训练设置，并且需要从头开始训练。此外，从不确定性图中提取单一的置信度得分并不总是直观的。

Method: 本文探索了可以在现有分割流水线中直接使用的体素级不确定性估计方法，并提出了基于不确定性图估计单一置信度得分的新方法。

Result: 在不同的ROI和模态上的评估表明，本文提出的置信度得分能够预测未见过的数据的分割性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法可以在不重新训练模型的情况下，为现有的医用图像分割任务提供有效的不确定性评估和置信度估计，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Convolutional neural networks (CNN) are widely used for medical image segmentation. However, they lack an inherent measure of confidence. Existing methods for estimating prediction uncertainty often require a custom network architecture and/or dedicated training setups and need to be trained from scratch. In addition, the deduction of a single confidence score per input from the uncertainty maps is not always straightforward. In this study, we explore voxel-based uncertainty estimation methods that can be added to existing segmentation pipelines without training from scratch. We also propose a novel method to estimate a single confidence score from the uncertainty maps. Our evaluations on different ROIs and modalities suggest that our confidence score can predict segmentation performance in unseen data.

</details>


### [112] [PerceptRL: Making Visual Perception Rewardable in MLLMs from Answers to Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37471)
*Tongkun Guan,Zhibo Yang,Mingkun Yang,Haocheng Wang,Wei Shen,Xiaokang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PerceptRL 提出了一种改进 MLLM 在细粒度视觉感知上表现的方法，通过多智能体管道和 QA 方法提取验证错误，并通过多轮检验纠正空间操作中的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模多模态语言模型的后训练阶段，细粒度的视觉感知仍然是一个瓶颈，因为感知错误会限制来自推理优化的提升。为了克服这一挑战，PerceptRL 提出了增强视觉感知的方法，包括使用多智能体管道提取验证错误和构建 QA 出题机制。

Method: PerceptRL 采用了一种多智能体的管道来从密集图像标题中提取验证错误，并将相应的视觉事实转化为带有固定答案标签和基于规则的奖励的 QA 对。另外，它通过多轮检验和视觉工具校正空间操作中的错误，并采用了专业知识驱动的校正路线和通用校正路线。

Result: 在七个共享工具使用协议的基准测试中，PerceptRL 在平均准确性上达到了 71.6%，超越了基线模型和最强的已评估视觉语言模型（VLM），分别提高了 15.7 和 5.2 个百分点。

Conclusion: PerceptRL 通过改进视觉感知提高了 MLLM 的表现，并通过多轮检验与视觉工具增强了细粒度和密集场景的问题解决能力。

Abstract: Fine-grained visual perception remains a bottleneck during post-training of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), as perceptual errors can constrain gains from reasoning-focused optimization. Although image-caption pre-training improves perception, its influence can weaken as subsequent stages change data mixtures and objectives. Continuing caption supervision during post-training is challenging because subjective, information-dense descriptions complicate verifiable reward design. We present PerceptRL, which strengthens perception at two complementary levels. Perception RL uses a multi-agent pipeline to extract verified errors from dense image captions and convert the corresponding visual facts into QA pairs with fixed answer labels and rule-based rewards. Chain QA links correlated errors, while multiple-choice QA combines unrelated errors. A specialized route targets high-error domains with tailored verification, and a domain-agnostic general route covers errors beyond these domains. Perception Agentic RL addresses fine-grained and dense cases unresolved by single-turn Perception RL through multi-turn inspection with visual tools. We observe coordinate drift even in models trained to use tools, where incorrect crops or annotations distort evidence and propagate errors. Coordinate Injection corrects spatial operations in cold-start trajectories, while a grounding-aware process reward supervises intermediate spatial fidelity alongside final-answer correctness. Training proceeds through shared cold-start SFT, single-turn Perception RL, and multi-turn Perception Agentic RL. Across seven benchmarks under a shared tool-use protocol, PerceptRL achieves 71.6% average accuracy, surpassing the base model with the same tools and the strongest evaluated frontier VLM by 15.7 and 5.2 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [113] [How Reliable Is Individualized Functional-Network Attribution in Resting-State fMRI Cognitive Prediction in ADNI?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37472)
*Sheethal Bhat,Mahfuzur Rahman Chowdhury,Imam Ahmed Shaik,Rose Dawn Bharath,Siming Bayer,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这项研究评估了冻结静息态功能连接（rs-fMRI）预测器中基于个体脑网络归因的重复测试可靠性，结果显示整体模式共享较强，但存在小量个体特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机是量化基于个体脑网络归因的rs-fMRI预测器的重复可靠性，因为此类归因通常作为解释性rs-fMRI生物标记物被提出，但其重复一致性尚未被充分研究。

Method: 研究使用了ADNI的N=875个受试者的数据，构建了基于Schaefer-200分区的静态功能连接预测器，通过扫描重测和主置换对照方法评估了个体脑网络归因的重复可靠性。

Result: 半分割可靠性接近天花板（Spearman p=0.948/0.950），但在扫描-重扫描测试中降低（p=0.609/0.616）。在不匹配受试者的对照下，相关性仍保持显著（p≈0.46-0.47）；但在匹配对数中，超过了对照（z≈6.6-6.9，p≤10⁻⁴）。表明ADNI中个体脑网络归因包含既有的个体特异性部分和强烈的人口共享结构。

Conclusion: 研究得出结论，在ADNI中，个体化网络归因包含适度但非零的人体特定成分在强烈人口共享模式之上，建议报告扫描-重扫描可靠性并附上对照置换空值以避免高估个体特定性。

Abstract: Individualized attribution estimates which functional networks drive a model prediction for a given subject and is often proposed as an interpretable resting-state fMRI (rs-fMRI) biomarker, but its test-retest reliability is rarely quantified. We evaluate per-subject network-ablation attribution for a frozen resting-state functional connectivity (FC) predictor (Schaefer-200 FC predicting MMSE/ADAS-Cog13) trained on N=875 subjects from the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI), using, and (iii) a subject-level permutation null. Split-half reliability is near-ceiling (Spearman p=0.948/0.950) but drops on scan-rescan (p=0.609/0.616). Under the mismatched-subject null, correlations remain substantial (p~0.46-0.47), indicating strong population-shared structure, yet matched pairs exceed this null (z~6.6-6.9, p<=10-4). Thus, in ADNI, individualized network attribution contains a modest but non-zero person-specific component atop a strong population-shared pattern. We recommend reporting scan-rescan reliability together with a permutation-null control, rather than within-session proxies alone, to avoid overstating individual specificity.

</details>


### [114] [ALADDIN: Adversarial Latent Diffusion Distillation for One-Step Identity Swapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37503)
*Ivan Shchekotov,Timofei Gritsaev,Viacheslav Pirogov,Dmitry Vetrov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ALADDIN 是一种一步式面部互换方法，通过使用预训练的多步流匹配模型初始化生成器并在保有身份和目标属性约束下进行对抗训练，实现了在保持交换质量和目标属性的同时，相比最佳多步模型减少了 28 倍的网络评估次数和数量级的训练对数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面对当前方法存在的网络评估耗时问题和生成器从噪声中学习真实性的困难，作者提出了一种新颖的方法 ALADDIN，结合了预训练模型和对抗训练的优势。

Method: ALADDIN 方法首先从预训练的多步流匹配模型初始化生成器，然后在目标保全和身份保全的约束下进行对抗训练。为了控制身份转移与目标属性保留之间的权衡，训练了一种学生模型，并使用三重伪标签监督。还通过独立的身份编码器对基准方法进行了重新评估以修正评估偏差。

Result: 实验结果表明，ALADDIN 在单步骤方法中实现了最佳的现实感和目标属性保留，且与最先进多步骤模型相比，仅需 28 倍的网络评估次数和数量级更少的训练对。

Conclusion: ALADDIN 方法有效解决了一步式面部互换面临的现实感和效率问题，不仅提高了互换质量，还大幅减少了计算资源的消耗。

Abstract: Face swapping transfers a source identity onto a target image while preserving the target's pose, expression, hair, and lighting. The most realistic current methods are diffusion-based, but they need many network evaluations per image. GAN-based methods are fast, yet trained from scratch they must learn realism from the adversarial signal alone and produce visibly weaker results. We present ALADDIN (Adversarial Latent Diffusion Distillation for One-Step Identity Swapping), a one-step face swapper obtained by initialising the generator from a pre-trained multi-step flow-matching model and training it adversarially under identity and target-preservation constraints. We expose a controllable trade-off between identity transfer and target-attribute preservation by training a student model with triplet pseudo-label supervision. We additionally identify a bias in the standard evaluation protocol, which favours methods trained against the same identity encoder used to score them, and re-evaluate all baselines with an independent one. ALADDIN is state-of-the-art among one-step methods in realism and target preservation at comparable identity transfer, and matches the best multi-step model with 28 times fewer network evaluations and an order of magnitude fewer training pairs.

</details>


### [115] [A note on data-efficient finetuning of vision foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37510)
*Niclas Hergenröther,Antonio Orvieto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了最新的模型性能，对比了早期架构和替代微调方法，探讨了在资源限制下的高效微调进展、挑战和机会。本文通过扩展评估模型和方法的选择，探讨了基础模型家族（注意-、卷积-和Mamba基于的）与转移范式：视觉提示、线性探测、全量微调和低秩适应之间的相互作用，同时还研究了正则化技术在视觉提示下的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更好地理解在资源限制下的高效微调进展、挑战和机会，评估最新模型的性能，对比早期架构和替代微调方法。

Method: 本研究通过扩展评估模型和技术的选择，探讨了基础模型家族（注意-、卷积-和Mamba基于的）与转移范式之间的相互作用，包括视觉提示、线性探测、全量微调和低秩适应，并研究了正则化技术在视觉提示下的应用。

Result: 本研究提供了基础模型家族和转移范式之间的交互的简明实证概述，同时也探讨了正则化技术在视觉提示下的可能应用。

Conclusion: 本论文的研究结果有助于为用户提供实用的指导，以提高在资源限制下的模型性能。

Abstract: Adapting foundation models to a new task within or beyond the training domain is the de facto standard for building specialized machine learning models. Recently, Visual Prompting (VP) has emerged as a promising technique for efficient knowledge transfer. As new foundation model families~(like Mamba) get introduced and VP pipelines such as AutoVP reach greater maturity, we find a growing need for a systematic evaluation of current approaches. In this work, we assess the performance of the latest models, comparing them to earlier architectures and alternative fine-tuning methods, to better understand the progress, challenges and opportunities in the field of efficient fine-tuning under resource limitations. Towards this goal, this paper provides a concise empirical overview of the interactions among foundation model families (Attention-, Convolution-, and Mamba-based) and transfer paradigms: VP, Linear Probing (LP), Full Fine-tuning (FFT) and Low Rank Adaptation (LoRA). Our work builds up on previous findings by broadening the selection of evaluated models and techniques while tuning hyperparameters. In the interest of delivering practical guidelines for the user, we also explore application of prevalent regularization techniques to boost performance in the context of VP.

</details>


### [116] [GroundControl: Satellite-Conditioned Multi-View Diffusion for Reconstruction-Free Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37545)
*Akshay Krishnan,Matias Turkulainen,Jamie Wynn,Zawar Qureshi,Mohamed Sayed,Guillermo Garcia-Hernando,Sara Vicente,James Hays,Michael Firman,Daniyar Turmukhambetov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种结合卫星图像和街景图像条件的多视角扩散模型，以解决多视角生成模型在户外场景合成中出现的不现实的空间布局问题，实验表明该模型在新颖视角合成方面优于卫星图或街景图单独条件下的基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多视角生成模型在合成超出输入视角的新视角（场景外推）时，往往会产生不现实的空间布局。为解决这一问题，研究利用卫星图像作为额外信息源条件，提出了一种新的卫星图条件下的多视角扩散模型。

Method: 该方法采用了跨注意力机制进行条件化，结合卫星图像和街景图像生成新颖视角。模型不依赖3D场景重建，仅依赖于街道和卫星图的视角。

Result: 实验结果表明，该模型在户外数据集中的新颖视角合成性能优于仅使用卫星图或街景图条件下的基线模型，特别是在目标视角与源视角重叠较少的情况下。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的方法能够生成无缝、逼真的新颖视角，并且在户外场景合成中表现更优。

Abstract: Multi-view or video diffusion models have recently emerged as state-of-the-art approaches for novel-view synthesis from sparse image collections. While they can produce high quality images, they tend to hallucinate unrealistic spatial layouts when synthesizing views outside the input views (scene extrapolation). We propose to address this challenge for outdoor scenes by using satellite images as an additional information source to condition the multi-view generative model. We introduce a new satellite-conditioned multi-view diffusion model that combines conditioning from a satellite image and street-level input images when generating novel-views. Our cross-attention based conditioning allows us to retain pretrained multi-view diffusion priors to generate seamless, realistic, novel views from a handful of source views, while also adhering to the scene layout as seen from satellite images. Our model does not use 3D scene reconstruction, and only relies on posed street and satellite views. Our experiments on outdoor datasets show that our model is better than both satellite-conditioned or street-view conditioned baselines on novel-view synthesis, especially on challenging cases where target views have very little overlap with source views. Project page: https://nianticspatial.github.io/groundcontrol

</details>


### [117] [ReferStream: Interactive Subject Injection for Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37566)
*Shuolin Xu,Guanyuan Li,Boyu Li,Kelu Yao,Xiaogang Xu,Hongchuan Yu,Shuicheng Yan,Xiaobin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReferStream 提出了一种无需额外训练的框架，能够将新提供的时间视觉参考融合到现有视频中，同时保持已存在的主题和场景，通过结合当前场景和临时缓存注入优化视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的视频生成方法在注入新主题时，要么对新特征的指导不足，要么会干扰已有的上下文。为了克服这些挑战，本文提出了一种无需额外训练的框架，可以更好地将新时间视觉参考融入正在生成的视频中，保护已存在的主题和场景。

Method: 该方法涉及两部分：Joint Latent 和 Cache Restoration。Joint Latent 将参考特征融入最新的场景特征中，Cache Restoration 则根据这些特征调整当前片段的条件，同时恢复被覆盖的全局缓存条目，保持新生成的历史记录。

Result: 使用的 ReferStream Bench 测试显示，该方法在文本一致性、主题相似性和计数准确性方面，无论是否所有参考都在生成开始时提供，得分都优于基准方法。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种新的视频生成框架 ReferStream，该框架无需额外训练，能够有效融合新时间视觉参考，同时保持原有主题和场景的完整性。通过 ReferStream Bench 测试证明了其在多个评估指标上的优越性。

Abstract: Injecting new subjects naturally during streaming video generation requires integrating newly supplied visual references into the existing video while preserving established subjects and the scene. Encoding references independently of the current scene provides little guidance for this integration, while keeping their features in persistent memory can interfere with established context. We propose ReferStream, a framework that requires no additional training and combines reference construction using the current scene with transient cache injection. Joint Latent places reference latents in target regions of the latest scene latent, allowing reference features to be extracted in context. Cache Restoration uses these features to condition the injection chunk, then restores the overwritten global cache entries while retaining the newly generated history. This procedure incorporates new visual conditions without retraining the generator or revising emitted frames. We further introduce ReferStream Bench, a new benchmark with scheduled reference arrivals, evolving instructions, and explicit annotations of subject presence for InShot and MultiShot generation. Evaluation follows the event schedule and measures both the fidelity of new subjects and the preservation of established subjects, alongside video quality, text adherence, and count accuracy. Experiments on ReferStream Bench show higher scores for text adherence, subject similarity, and count accuracy than the compared baselines, including offline methods given all references in advance. Project page: https://xushuolin.github.io/ReferStream/.

</details>


### [118] [The Noise Plane: A Diffusion Bridge Across Exponent and Dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37576)
*Xuran Hu,Yuru Jia,Tengxi Wang,Miloš Daković,Ljubiša Stanković,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的基于度量指标的概率桥梁（p-Bridge）模型，用于处理透威分布噪声。该模型通过测量累加，结合了广义自回归条件均值模型和逆均值更新机制，适用于不同的信号依赖噪声场景，并在合成图像、FMD荧光显微镜和多传感器SAR图像实验中展示了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的去噪方法主要采用高斯噪声假设，默认无法满足传感器噪声的信号依存特性。因此，本文旨在提出一种能够处理透威分布噪声的新方法，以扩大去噪方法的适用范围。

Method: p-Bridge模型通过引入一个度量指标控制系统中的噪声类型（p值描述了方差-强度关系，积累度量提供噪声级别），并使用测量累加的方式连接不同信号的概率分布，提供了一种渐进测量累积的物理解释。该模型还结合了自回归条件均值模型和逆均值更新机制，可以在不为每个噪声模型分别训练的情况下自适应地应用于不同的透威噪声模型。

Result: 该方法首先使用单独的网络针对不同指数进行优化，然后通过估计指数将共享网络应用于不同噪声模型。在合成图像、FMD荧光显微镜成像和多传感器SAR图像中的实验表明，估计的指数能够使共享网络实现与为不同指数手动选择的五个专家模型相当的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种有效的去噪方法p-Bridge，它统一地处理多种信号依赖噪声，如伽马噪声、泊松噪声和高斯噪声。实验结果表明，该方法在各种信号依赖噪声条件下的性能与手动选择的专家模型相当。

Abstract: Diffusion-based denoising commonly uses Gaussian perturbations, whereas sensor noise follows diverse signal-dependent statistical laws. In this paper, we propose $p$-Bridge, a unified measurement-indexed diffusion framework for Tweedie noise. The noise exponent $p$ describes the variance--intensity relation, while accumulated measurement $ν$ controls the noise level. Together, they define a noise plane that includes Gaussian, Poisson, and Gamma models. The stochastic bridge connects measurement distributions through independent increments, giving denoising a physical interpretation as progressive measurement accumulation. Both the starting and stopping indices can be specified, allowing restoration from an observed measurement level to a prescribed higher level. Measurement superposition yields a shared reverse-mean update, paired with a deviance-based objective for learning the required conditional mean. A latent compound Poisson--Gamma construction enables sampling at intermediate exponents. A single exponent-conditioned network adapts to different Tweedie noise models without separate training for each exponent. Experiments show that exponent conditioning improves cross-exponent robustness over fixed-exponent models. Using estimated exponents, the shared network achieves robustness comparable to an oracle-selected set of five specialist models. Experiments on synthetic images, FMD fluorescence microscopy, and multi-sensor SAR images further demonstrate its effectiveness across different signal-dependent noise regimes.

</details>


### [119] [As the Story Unfolds: Watching a Film and Identifying Characters as a Human Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37579)
*Zhongrui Gui,Junyu Xie,Tengda Han,Weidi Xie,Andrew Zisserman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了直接从电影中识别主要角色及其命名的任务，而不依赖于外部演员名单、照片或预建的角色库。该研究提供了一个用于评估角色命名和首次登场的数据库和注释，并开发了一个结合视频和对话的模型，并探讨了随着电影的发展构建角色库的逐步框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前识别电影角色的方法依赖于外部资源或预建的角色库，本文旨在提供一种无需外部资源的方法来直接从电影中识别角色及其命名，以实现在线的人类级电影理解。

Method: 本文采用了结合视频和对话的模型进行角色命名任务的研究，并开发了一个逐步框架来随着电影的发展构建角色库。

Result: 本文提供了一个用于评估角色命名和首次登场的新数据库和注释，开发了一个结合视频和对话的模型，并与专有的多模态大型语言模型进行了比较。

Conclusion: 本文的研究结果表明，通过直接从电影中识别角色及其命名是可行的，并且随着电影的发展逐步构建角色库是一种有效的方法，这有助于实现在线的人类级电影理解。

Abstract: Films introduce characters incrementally as they progress. We study the problem of identifying principal characters and assigning their names directly from the movie itself, without using external cast lists, actor photographs, or pre-built character banks. We introduce the character naming task: given a name mentioned in dialogue and the associated video context, the model must determine which visible character bears that name. This task is challenging because name resolution often depends on cinematic cues such as shot-reverse-shot structure, self-introduction, direct address, third-party reference, gaze, and temporal continuity. We provide a dataset and annotations for evaluating character naming and first character appearances, develop a video-and-dialogue model for the task, and compare it with proprietary multimodal large language models. We further introduce a progressive framework for building a character bank as the film unfolds. Together, these contributions support online, human-like movie understanding in which character identities are inferred from the film itself.

</details>


### [120] [Zero-Shot Multimodal Event Spotting in Soccer Broadcasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37583)
*Sadia Kamal,Mahmudul Hasan,Tony Braskich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种零样本框架，可以在无需特定任务参数训练的情况下，检测、分类并时间定位全长度足球广播中的事件，通过紧凑的视觉故事板和对齐的评论脚本进行表示，显著降低了推断成本并提高了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的足球事件检测方法依赖于大规模标注数据集和特定任务的训练，而零样本框架可以在没有特定任务参数训练的情况下完成这些任务，降低了对特定资源的需求。

Method: 该方法将每场比赛分割成重叠片段，表示为紧凑的视觉故事板和对齐的评论脚本，然后由泛化的多模态模型联合处理。

Result: 最佳配置在包含130个事件的手动标注比赛中取得了72.0%的F1@5s性能，在跨数据集设置中优于SoccerNet训练的CALF和NetVLAD++。故事板表示将推断成本降低了72%。此外，还引入了基于证据的兴奋度评分阶段，用于以自然语言解释的形式选择关键时刻。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了零样本方法在足球事件检测中的有效性和潜力，为未来的工作奠定了基础，并提供了改进的策略和工具。

Abstract: Automatic soccer event spotting supports highlight generation, video indexing, and sports analytics, but existing methods depend on large annotated datasets and soccer-specific training. We present a zero-shot framework that detects, classifies, and temporally localizes events in full-length soccer broadcasts without task-specific parameter training. Each match is segmented into overlapping clips represented by a compact visual storyboard and the aligned commentary transcript, which a general-purpose multimodal model processes jointly. On three manually annotated matches containing 130 events, the best configuration achieves 72.0% F1@5s and outperforms SoccerNet-trained CALF and NetVLAD++ in a cross-dataset setting, while the storyboard representation reduces inference cost by 72% compared with individual frames. We further analyze the effects of model family, input representation, and prompt optimization, and introduce an evidence-grounded excitement-scoring stage that ranks detected events with natural-language explanations for highlight selection.

</details>


### [121] [MoI-Bench: A Benchmark for Image Generation and Editing on Mobile Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37599)
*Kailai Feng,Haoxiang Rao,Yuxiang Wei,Bo Chen,Junsong Li,Yang Pan,Chengkun Cai,Jagmohan Chauhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoI-Bench 是一项针对移动设备的基准测试，包含64个子任务和1150个样例，旨在评估边缘计算模型在特定任务上的性能，特别是对于复杂的视觉操作。MoI-Eval 提供了更细化的任务评估标准和评估方法，提出了质量与部署性二维评估框架来更全面地评估边缘计算模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准测试提供了有限的指导来识别边缘设备模型和服务器模型之间的能力差距。MoI-Bench 和 MoI-Eval 的引入旨在填补这一空白，提供更符合移动用户需求的任务特定评估标准，以更好地指导模型改善。

Method: MoI-Bench 通过定义操作（如移除和替换）和视觉目标类别（包括人物肖像和文本），构建了一个多层次的任务结构。MoI-Eval 则进一步引入了任务特定的标准，覆盖指令遵循、视觉质量和细节保留。同时，该研究还提出了基于操作、目标和标准的样本级别评价问题，将这些评分汇总为整体质量和能力概览。

Result: 研究结果表明，这个操作 x 目标设计的解耦结构可以为改进紧凑模型提供更精细的指导。例如，该框架强调特定能力差距（如文本替换）的优先级高于广泛的应用目标（如改进文本编辑）。与传统的使用单一评分排名模型不同，MoI-Bench 引入了质量-部署性二维能力地图来评估模型的实用移动使用情况。该地图揭示出当前的边缘计算模型可以实现有效的生成或编辑，但扩大任务覆盖范围比进一步提高部署效率更加重要。进一步的进展需要提高编辑质量，特别是身份保留和文本呈现。

Conclusion: MoI-Bench 和 MoI-Eval 为评估边缘设备上的图像模型提供了新颖的方法和维度，这将有助于未来工作的改进，特别是提高模型的编辑质量和任务覆盖范围，同时也强调了在移动设备上提高部署效率同样重要。

Abstract: Developing on-device image models requires considering practical user needs, task types and device resource constraints. However, existing benchmarks provide limited guidance on identifying capability gaps between on-device models and server-side models. We introduce MoI-Bench, a benchmark grounded in mobile user needs, comprising 64 subtasks and 1150 samples. It defines 16 operations, such as removal and replacement, and eight visual target categories covering content such as portraits and text. These form two orthogonal axes whose applicable combinations define specific tasks, such as removing facial makeup. To better guide model improvement, we further propose MoI-Eval, which introduces task-specific criteria covering instruction following, visual quality and detail preservation. We further construct sample-level evaluation questions and aggregate their scores into overall quality and capability profiles along the operation, target and criteria axes. We evaluate representative on-device and server-side models and measure mobile deployment costs. Results show that this decoupled Operation x Target design provides finer-grained guidance for improving compact models. For example, it highlights the need to prioritize specific capability gaps (e.g., text-replacement) over broad application-level goals (e.g., improving text editing). Unlike conventional benchmarks that rank models using a single score, we introduce a two-dimensional Quality-Deployability capability map to assess models for practical mobile use. The map shows that current on-device models can achieve efficient generation or editing but expanding their task coverage deserves greater attention than further improving deployment efficiency. Further progress requires improving editing quality, particularly identity preservation and text rendering, while broadening task coverage.

</details>


### [122] [VideoLVR: Latent Visual Reasoning over Grounded Video Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37613)
*Shilong Li,Xingyuan Bu,Hao Shi,Yun Xing,Weixin Feng,Qiangpeng Yang,Shilei Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoLVR 通过构建潜伏视觉证据并引入视觉token预测目标，允许模型在推理过程中高效地访问任务相关视觉信息，从而增强视频理解能力，而无需大幅扩展多模态上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的方法使用文本坐标或其他方式仅在一定程度上保留了视觉证据，而 VideoLVR 旨在通过从视频中的关键帧构建潜伏视觉证据，增强模型在推理过程中的视觉支持。

Method: VideoLVR 方法涉及两个关键组件：一是视觉token预测目标，即将视觉token重建视为基于输入视频词汇表的预测；二是通过将抽样视觉token作为动作与文本token一同进行策略优化。

Result: 实验表明，VideoLVR 在多个视频基准测试中表现出色，尤其是在需要精细视觉证据的任务上。

Conclusion: VideoLVR 在不大幅扩展多模态上下文的情况下，提高了视频理解能力，并且适用于强化学习场景。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought reasoning has substantially advanced video understanding in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, language-based reasoning traces do not provide a reliable mechanism for models to revisit relevant visual evidence, leading to visual forgetting and impairing their ability to reason over video content. Existing approaches maintain visual grounding during reasoning in two main ways: they either refer to evidence using textual coordinates, which specify symbolic locations rather than the visual content itself, or reinsert cropped regions or frames into the multimodal context, preserving visual details at the cost of substantial context expansion. To address these limitations, we introduce VideoLVR, which constructs latent visual evidence from local regions at relevant video moments, thereby providing visual support for intermediate reasoning steps. To supervise latent visual evidence generation, we introduce a visual token prediction objective that formulates visual token reconstruction as prediction over a vocabulary derived from the input video, thereby unifying visual evidence reconstruction and text generation within the same token prediction framework. This shared prediction framework enables MLLMs to reason over interleaved text tokens and latent visual states while integrating evidence from different moments. We further extend VideoLVR to reinforcement learning by treating sampled visual tokens as actions alongside text tokens and jointly optimizing the policy using final-answer rewards. By efficiently revisiting task-relevant visual information during reasoning, VideoLVR improves video understanding without substantially expanding the multimodal context. Experiments on multiple video benchmarks demonstrate its effectiveness, especially on tasks requiring fine-grained visual evidence.

</details>


### [123] [Perceiving and Predicting Dynamic Sports: Spatiotemporal Intelligence in Vision Models for Egocentric Tennis Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37619)
*Tudor Braicu,Joseph DelPreto,Jared Boyer,Mónika Farsang,Maximilian Stölzle,Tim Niklas Seyde,Daniela Rus*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究通过构建网球数据集，评估了基础视觉模型在处理从机器人自身视角获取的时空任务时的表现，尤其关注了当前和未来球与球员位置的预测，以及击球类型和下次球接触时间的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在评估基础视觉模型在帮助敏捷机器人理解场景、进行推理和预测未来事件方面的能力，特别是在从移动的机器人第一视角拍摄的视频上。以网球运动为应用场景，构建数据集和模型为这一目标提供支持。

Method: 该研究通过构建一个涵盖球员和球的2D/3D定位、未来轨迹预测、击球类型预判、下次接触时间预测以及3D球位置预测等多个任务的网球数据集，并训练了多种视觉模型。实验对比了图像编码器、视频编码器及视觉语言模型在这些任务上的表现。

Result: 研究结果显示，最佳模型在当前帧的平球员和球的定位误差分别为0.14米和0.54米，在1.1秒后球员和球的预测误差分别为0.34米和0.91米，且击球时间和下一次接触时间的预测误差为0.14秒。此外，额外的深度信息显著提高了3D球位置预测的准确性。尽管时间序列图像序列增加没有提升球轨迹的预测表现，但模型能通过利用场景上下文信息来进行预测。

Conclusion: 研究结论认为，基础视觉模型可以在一定程度上支持实时动态机器人的任务需求，特别是在使用第一视角视频进行动作预测方面，表明进一步的研究可以集中在提高模型对3D空间信息的解释能力和对场景理解的长时记忆上。

Abstract: Agile robots require spatiotemporal intelligence to interpret scenes, reason about interactions, and anticipate future events. Yet it remains unclear how well foundation vision models support these capabilities, particularly from a moving egocentric camera, which is a natural view for robots. To investigate this, we collect an annotated tennis dataset and train a suite of vision models on spatiotemporal tasks. Our dataset contains egocentric video synchronized with five allocentric videos, estimated global positions, labeled contact times, and annotated stroke types. We then evaluate seven prediction tasks from egocentric images: 2D ego player and 3D ball localization, future player and ball trajectories, forehand or backhand stroke anticipation, time to the next ego player ball contact, and 3D ball position at that contact. We compare image encoders, video encoders, and vision-language models with jointly trained prediction heads. The best performing models achieve mean current-frame localization errors of 0.14 m for the ego player and 0.54 m for the ball, future forecasting errors of 0.34 m for the ego player and 0.91 m for the ball over 1.1 s, and a mean contact-time prediction error of 0.14 s. Auxiliary depth information substantially improves 3D ball localization, suggesting that inferring metric depth remains a challenge. Longer image histories do not improve ball forecasting, indicating that representations exploit scene context rather than rely solely on inter-frame motion. Finally, we propose a conditional diffusion model to generate whole-body kinematic tennis motion from vision encoder predictions on egocentric video at real-time rates, adapting movements as predictions are refined. These results move towards a deeper understanding of how foundation vision models can support real-time dynamic robots using egocentric video.

</details>


### [124] [Know, then Draw: A New Learning Paradigm for Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37626)
*Yucheng Mao,Chongjian Ge,Zhuowen Tu,Hao Tan,Hanwen Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种学习范式，通过自回归预训练获得语义知识，利用 diffusion 模型实现高保真渲染。它还发现自回归预训练比像素扩散模型具有优势，并采用纯语义标记化器，提高了最终生成质量。基于此，实现了具有86分的文本到图像模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的视觉生成需要结合高层语义知识和精细化像素细节两方面，现有的方法可能存在效率不高或质量不理想的问题，因此需要开发一种新的学习范式来提高生成效率和质量。

Method: 该研究主要采用自回归预训练和diffusion后训练的方式，探索了知识获取和渲染的目标、表示及扩展性。通过训练了纯语义标记化器，独立于图像解码器和重建损失。建立了知识获取和渲染的计算最优扩展法则，以指导大规模训练。

Result: 实验结果显示，自回归预训练增强了下游渲染任务的学习效率和最终生成质量，而纯语义标记化器在生成质量上更为重要。基于此，训练了包含6,600个H100 GPU小时的3亿参数模型，达到了86的GenEval分数和87的DPG-Bench分数。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的方法在视觉生成任务中表现良好，特别是在知识获取和渲染的计算扩展性及生成质量上有显著提升。

Abstract: Visual generation requires both high-level semantic knowledge and fine-grained pixel details. We propose a learning paradigm that assigns these roles to autoregressive pretraining for knowledge acquisition and diffusion post-training for high-fidelity rendering. Within this framework, we study the objective, representation, and scaling of knowledge acquisition and rendering. Controlled comparisons identify autoregression as the stronger pretraining objective for downstream rendering, improving both learning efficiency and final generation quality. We further find that AR-only generation quality does not necessarily correlate with final performance; semantic quality matters more. This motivates a purely semantic tokenizer trained with language supervision alone, without an image decoder or reconstruction loss. We establish compute-optimal scaling laws for knowledge acquisition and rendering, and find that autoregressive pretraining lowers the fitted asymptotic rendering loss compared with pixel diffusion models trained from scratch. Guided by the laws, we train a 3B-parameter text-to-image model with only 6,600 H100 GPU-hours, achieving 86 on GenEval and 87 on DPG-Bench and validating the proposed paradigm at scale.

</details>


### [125] [Repurposing Video Diffusion Transformers for Cross-View Temporal Object Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37633)
*Siyoon Jin,Dahyun Chung,Honggyu An,Sangbeom Lim,Wonjin Nam,Jaewoo Jung,WonJun Moon,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: XTrack 是一种新的跨视角视频对象跟踪框架，通过改进的注意力机制和负样本条件优化跨视角对应的准确性，并引入伪标签润色管道以利用稀疏注释进行有效训练，从而保证了时间一致性和身份保真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖帧级匹配或序列跟踪，容易在外观变化或遮挡下传播对应错误。因此，提出跨视角的时序对象对应，需要在两个视角和时间上保持对象身份，以解决以上问题。

Method: XTrack 采用了预训练的视频 DiT 并引入了语义和时空先验，联合建模跨视角匹配和时序跟踪。同时，通过焦点跨视角注意力对齐、强硬的负样本条件和局部上下文裁剪策略，确保了可靠的跨视角对应。此外，还引入了伪标签润色管道将稀疏注释转换为密集监督，用于视频级别的训练。

Result: 实验结果表明，XTrack 框架能够有效保证跨视角对应的准确性和时序一致性。

Conclusion: XTrack 提出了一种新的跨视角视频对象跟踪方法，通过架构优化和伪标签润色管道，实现了准确的跨视角对应和有效的视频级别训练。

Abstract: Cross-view object correspondence identifies the same object across views. However, existing methods often rely on frame-wise matching or sequential tracking, which can propagate correspondence errors under appearance changes or occlusions. Therefore, we introduce cross-view temporal object correspondence, which requires preserving object identity across both views and time from a query mask. To address this, we propose XTrack, a unified framework which repurposes a pretrained video DiT with semantic and spatiotemporal priors for RGB-to-mask latent transport, jointly modeling cross-view matching and temporal tracking. However, naive video DiTs do not guarantee accurate cross-view grounding and often attend to semantically similar yet incorrect objects across views. Thus, we introduce focal cross-view attention alignment, hard negative conditioning, and local context crop strategies to ensure reliable grounding. Finally, beyond these architectural enhancements, we also tackle the lack of dense annotation by introducing a pseudo-label curation pipeline that converts sparse Ego-Exo4D annotations into dense supervision to enable effective video-level training. Our results demonstrate the effectiveness of XTrack as a unified framework, ensuring robust temporal consistency and identity preservation.

</details>


### [126] [Can Discrete Tokens Serve as Semantically Lossless Representations of Visual Signals?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37662)
*Jialv Zou,Xiaotong Li,Zhengrong Yue,Xu Huang,Yaxin Luo,Wenhao Yan,Haodong Li,Wenyu Liu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种因子化向量量化方法（Factorized Vector Quantization, FVQ），它通过使用对称并行代码书对视觉特征的互补、固定维度视图进行量化，从而克服了传统量化方法在大型比特预算下的饱和问题。该方法在大规模多模态数据上训练，展示了与连续表示模型（如Qwen3-VL）相当甚至更好的性能，特别是在细粒度的OCR任务上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索离散视觉表示在多模态建模中的潜力，挑战传统认为量化误差不可避免地导致语义损失的观点，提出因子化向量量化方法以提高其在大型数据集上的表现。

Method: 引入Factorized Vector Quantization方法，采用对称并行代码书对视觉特征的不同固定维度视图进行量化，从而优化量化效果。

Result: FVQ方法在工业规模的多模态数据上表现突出，优于UniAR和Emu3-Chat模型，在平均九个评价指标上分别领先22.8%和59.2%。首次实现离散表示模型与强大的连续表示模型（如Qwen3-VL）具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 研究证实了离散视觉表示在多模态模型中的潜力，并提出了一种有效的实施策略，有望支持大规模的原生多模态基础模型构建。

Abstract: Discrete visual tokens represent vision in a discrete form analogous to language, offering a native interface for multimodal modeling under a shared next-token prediction objective. However, quantization error is widely assumed to cause unavoidable semantic loss, fundamentally limiting the semantic fidelity of discrete visual tokens and positioning continuous visual representations as an intrinsic upper bound. Using multimodal understanding as a proxy for semantic fidelity, we revisit this assumption and find quantization error unreliable, as discrete representations with substantially larger errors can achieve better understanding. Instead, the effective bit budget of discrete indices emerges as a key determinant of downstream performance. However, existing quantizers eventually saturate as additional codebooks contribute progressively less semantic information. As one approach to alleviating this saturation, we introduce Factorized Vector Quantization, which quantizes complementary, fixed-dimensional views of the visual feature with symmetric parallel codebooks. It continues to improve at larger bit budgets, ultimately matching continuous visual representations in multimodal understanding. Trained on industrial-scale multimodal data, our model achieves state-of-the-art performance among discrete-representation models, outperforming UniAR and Emu3-Chat by 22.8% and 59.2%, respectively, on a nine-metric average. To our knowledge, it is also the first discrete-representation model competitive with strong continuous-representation models such as Qwen3-VL, including on fine-grained OCR tasks. We further identify the key principles and a practical recipe enabling this competitiveness. These results and insights support the potential of discrete visual representations to underpin large-scale native multimodal foundation models. Code and models are available at https://github.com/hustvl/FVQ

</details>


### [127] [Honeycomb-Surfels: Compact Hexagonal Primitives for Efficient Scene Rendering and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37668)
*Prakhar Kaushik,Soumava Paul,Alan Yuille*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Hexels通过引入新颖的六边形表面元素，提供了在低内存和计算资源限制下的高效3D场景和2D图像表示，证明了在PSNR、FPS、准确度等方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的场景表示方法，如基于辐射场和散点图的方法，虽然功能强大但内存和计算需求较高，限制了在资源受限环境中的应用。Hexels旨在提供一种更高效的方法以降低资源消耗，并在图像质量和效率之间寻找平衡。

Method: Hexels利用平面局部区域和具有连续颜色场的六边形支持来表示3D场景和2D图像。这种方法通过减少更多、更大的原语来利用均匀变化的外观，并且其六边形支持形式在一个具有面积和边界的高效区域内提供颜色变化。

Result: 在34个场景中，Hexels在PSNR上较匹配内存的2DGS提高了4.53-7.38 dB，同时消耗更少的训练VRAM。在15个DTU扫描中，Hexels将误差降低了28.9%。在6个水箱和庙宇表面前景色中，Hexels的方法得分最高，F1分数提高了34.38%，而2DGS的方法得分为31.72%。此外，Hexels在2D图像表示中也优于 Image-GS。

Conclusion: Hexels在3D表面重建和2D图像表示方面表现出色，为表面对齐的原语在质量和效率之间的权衡上提出了前沿。

Abstract: Novel view synthesis and 3D reconstruction have advanced rapidly with radiance fields and splatting, yet their memory and computational demands limit their use in resource-constrained settings. We introduce Hexels: novel planar hexagonal surface elements with a first-order spatial color field for efficient 3D scene and 2D image representation. Hexels exploit locally planar regions with smoothly varying appearance using fewer, larger primitives. Their hexagonal support provides an area- and boundary-efficient domain, while the local color field captures appearance variation within each primitive. Across 34 scenes, Hexels improve PSNR over memory-matched 2DGS by 4.53-7.38 dB across datasets while requiring lower training VRAM. We further ablate Hexels by showing that per primitive, Hexels outperform 2DGS by upto 4.74 dB while using lower training VRAM and having 2.31 times faster FPS. Even in unconstrained settings, Hexels offers complementary quality-memory trade-offs. For efficient 3D surface reconstruction, Hexels reduce error by 28.9% compared with memory-matched 2DGS across 15 DTU scans. On 6 Tanks and Temples surface scenes, Hexels achieves the highest mean F1 among compared methods (34.38%, versus 31.72% for 2DGS). We further also show that Hexels, as a general primitive, improves 2D image representation over Image-GS. Hexels push the Pareto frontier on the quality-efficiency trade-off of surface-aligned primitives.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [ReCoPII: A Relation-Grounded Framework for Building Contextual PII Masking Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35925)
*Taehwan Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一个新的方法ReCoPII来构建个人身份信息（PII）掩码数据集，并验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前训练上下文掩码系统需要区分可屏蔽和保留提及的示例，但隐私限制限制了访问真实数据。SPY方法在单个数据主体假设下对未涉及作者的提及进行非PII标记，忽视了其他人。

Method: ReCoPII框架通过计划基于关系的实体关系、编写包含占位符的插入句子、用代码重建注释、使用单独的LLM检查结果，以及从实体关系中推导MASK/KEEP标签来构建数据。

Result: ReCoPII-Ko数据集含有关系、隐私和MASK/KEEP注释，与SPY风格的增强相比，其标签一致性高达88.42%。基于此数据集训练的分类器在检测的基础上少屏蔽了数据。结合PII检测器进行双向微调的上下文掩码方法能够以固定的泄露预算内最大程度地减少过度屏蔽，对人类验证的实体记录的过度屏蔽最多为5.04%，并且泄露不超过4.18%。PII检测器的参数量约为XLM-R-large的一 quarter。

Conclusion: 通过ReCoPII框架，提出了一种新的个人身份信息（PII）掩码数据集ReCoPII-Ko，并通过实验证明了其有效性和在减少过度屏蔽方面的改进。

Abstract: Training contextual masking systems for personally identifiable information (PII) requires examples distinguishing maskable from preservable mentions of one entity type, but privacy restrictions limit access to real data. Author-based augmentation (SPY) labels mentions unrelated to the author as non-PII, presuming a single data subject and overlooking others. We propose ReCoPII (Relation-grounded Contextual PII masking), a framework that builds such data without this premise: it plans ontology-constrained entity relations, writes insertion sentences with placeholders, reconstructs annotations with code, checks the result with a separate LLM, and derives MASK/KEEP labels from entity relations. On Korean documents from six domains, it yields ReCoPII-Ko, a corpus with relation, privacy, and MASK/KEEP annotations, which we validate. Across 300 pairs, four annotators agree with 88.42% of its sampled KEEP labels, against 26.27% for SPY-style augmentation of the same sources. Classifiers trained on it over-mask less than under that reference on a matched test. Contextual masking fine-tuned jointly with a PII detector removes most over-masking of detection alone within a fixed leakage budget; on human-verified entity records, the four detectors meeting it over-mask at most 5.04% with at most 4.18% leakage. The detector is comparable to XLM-R-large on KDPII with about a quarter of its parameters.

</details>


### [129] [MedJev: Evaluating Zero-Shot System-One Decision Making in Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36006)
*Simon A. Lee,Jiahang Sha,Moonseong Jeong,Yunbei Pan,Jeffrey N. Chiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MedJev 是一个基于结构化问题处理的医疗辅助决策模型，在多个医学基准测试上表现出色，接近 GPT-4 的性能，并且具有低计算成本和高效的本地校准能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决开放医疗领域中高效、低计算成本的零样本决策支持系统的需求。

Method: MedJev 使用了一种无需生成文本且在单一前向传递中处理结构化问题的方法，进行本地运行和多种医学基准测试的评估。

Result: MedJev 在多个医学知识、诊断和患者层面预测测试中达到了高准确率，接近 GPT-4 的表现，同时保持了良好的置信度估算。在患者层面任务中，虽然在某些方面超过了一些强化学习模型，但也表现出不同程度的校准不足。

Conclusion: 开放的 System One 模型 MedJev 作为零样本基线在医疗 AI 领域具有广阔的发展前景，同时提供了一种轻量级、参数少的方法来校准预测，以适应当地患者群体。

Abstract: System One decision models such as Jev answer structured questions in a single forward pass without generating text, offering fast and computationally efficient clinical decision support. We evaluate MedJev, an open System One model that runs locally on a laptop, across ten medical benchmarks spanning medical knowledge, diagnosis, and patient level prediction using MIMIC IV and MIMIC IV ED. We compare MedJev against zero shot LLM baselines, published GPT results, and supervised models. MedJev achieves accuracies of 72.5 percent on MedQA, 75.4 percent on USMLE, and 80.1 percent on NEJM, approaching published GPT 4 performance while maintaining well calibrated confidence estimates (ECE 0.028 to 0.063). On patient level tasks, MedJev achieves an AUROC of 0.926 for In Hospital Mortality, exceeding XGBoost (0.902), but underperforms supervised models on operational outcomes such as ED Triage Admission (0.745 vs. 0.809). Patient level predictions also exhibit substantial miscalibration (In Hospital Mortality ECE 0.286). We introduce a lightweight, two parameter recalibration method that adapts predictions to local patient populations using 50 to 200 labeled examples, reducing ECE to 0.007. Our findings establish open System One models as a promising zero shot baseline for medical AI, combining competitive predictive performance with low computational cost and efficient local calibration. We release our code and evaluation harness to support future research.

</details>


### [130] [OmniBrain-0: A Unified Agentic Model for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36013)
*Xiaomi Embodied Intelligence Team,Yuechen Luo,Chong He,Fang Li,Shaoqing Xu,Jingkai Wang,Menglun Su,Jiaxin Liu,Shengyin Jiang,Zhiyi Lai,Long Chen,Longlong Wang,Liang Hu,Haochen Liu,Yuncheng Jiang,Hongwei Xie,Jieming Cui,Hanbing Li,Junjie Huang,Zhixiang Duan,Hexin Zhang,Weixiang Liang,Ying Gong,Yuhan Wang,Yong Pan,Pengna Li,Kangyi Wu,Ken Deng,Kun Ma,Fuxi Wen,Zhi-xin Yang,Hangjun Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OmniBrain-0 是一种轻量级的统一具身智能代理，通过共享的高层决策模型协调操作和导航，结合了感知、规划、进度跟踪和反馈驱动的恢复。实验表明，与封闭源教师相比，OmniBrain-0 在跨域任务性能上表现出色，而推理和执行开销较低，证明了具身能力的有效转移和整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需要协调多样化的技能，跟踪任务进展并从失败中恢复。OmniBrain-0 旨在解决专长政策不够可靠以及强大多模态代理依赖于昂贵的推理和外部经验的问题。

Method: OmniBrain-0 采用进阶训练框架，包括具身预训练、领域专一代理蒸馏和多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）。此外，引入了Rubric-Guided Recursive Self-Improvement (RSI) 方法，以促进代理自我提高并将其进步反馈整合到代理中。

Result: OmniBrain-0 在 LIBERO-Pro 和 LIBERO-Plus、RoboCasa365、RoboTwin 2.0 和 R2R-CE Val-Unseen、以及多个实际操作任务上表现出色。与闭环源前辈相比，它具有较低的推理和执行开销。

Conclusion: OmniBrain-0 为有效整合不同任务的具身能力提供了一种新的方法，并展示了与强大教师相比，其高效的跨域性能和训练能力。

Abstract: General-purpose embodied intelligence requires robots to coordinate diverse skills over long horizons, track task progress, and recover from failures. Specialist policies alone do not ensure task-level reliability, while powerful multimodal agents rely on costly reasoning and external experience that compact models struggle to reproduce. We introduce OmniBrain-0, a lightweight unified embodied agent that coordinates manipulation and navigation through a shared high-level decision model, integrating task understanding, planning, progress tracking, and feedback-driven recovery. Our progressive training framework combines embodied pretraining, domain-specialized agent distillation, and multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD). It distills powerful teachers' interaction experience into manipulation and navigation experts, then consolidates their decision capabilities into OmniBrain-0. Unification therefore occurs within the learned decision model rather than only at the tool interface. We further introduce Rubric-Guided Recursive Self-Improvement (RSI), a data flywheel in which a Meta-Agent turns execution evidence into refined rubrics, step-level reminders, and verified trajectories for domain-expert updates before their reconsolidation into OmniBrain-0 through MOPD. We evaluate OmniBrain-0 on LIBERO-Pro, LIBERO-Plus, RoboCasa365, RoboTwin 2.0, and R2R-CE Val-Unseen, as well as multiple real-world manipulation tasks. OmniBrain-0 combines strong cross-domain task performance with substantially lower inference and execution overhead than its closed-source teacher, demonstrating efficient transfer and consolidation of embodied capabilities into a compact unified agent.

</details>


### [131] [FinLLM-KG: Knowledge Graph-Enhanced LLMs for Financial Sentiment Analysis in Algorithmic Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36083)
*Giorgos Iacovides,Oliver Hannibal,Wuyang Zhou,Danilo Mandic*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为FinLLM-KG的框架，它结合了因果语言模型与金融知识图谱，旨在通过注入SEC 10-K文件中的公司级别的时效性知识，改进现有的孤立文章情感分析方法，并提高了解释性和透明度。实验表明，此方法能提升投资组合的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的情感分析方法存在预测孤立文章情感的局限性，并且作为黑箱操作，难以理解其决策过程。因此，需要一种能够在保持模型效果的同时，增强透明度的方法。

Method: 通过结合因果语言模型和金融知识图谱，利用SEC 10-K文件中的公司级别信息，在预测时注入这些信息。同时，将每个文章级别的情感评分分配到个别句子和提取的知识图谱事实，增加解释性。

Result: 研究仿真实验表明，FinLLM-KG框架在提升投资组合表现方面优于现有的基础模型。

Conclusion: FinLLM-KG框架改进了金融情感分析，通过引入公司级别的时效性知识，提供了更好的解释性和透明性，并展示了其实际应用价值。

Abstract: Recent advances in Generative AI have substantially improved financial sentiment analysis through post-trained financial large language models (LLMs). However, existing approaches remain confined to predicting the sentiment of each article in isolation and operate as black boxes, which limits contextual understanding and hinders deployment in high-stakes financial decision-making. To this end, we introduce FinLLM-KG, a model-agnostic framework that combines causal LLMs with a financial knowledge graph, in order to inject temporally grounded, company-level knowledge from SEC 10-K filings at inference time, without updating model weights. Within FinLLM-KG, context is supplied either as a natural-language company profile or as a small set of relevance-ranked factual triples. This enables sentiment predictions to account for broader firm-level information rather than the article alone, at almost zero computational cost and for any causal LLM. In addition, attributing each article-level sentiment score to individual sentences and to the retrieved knowledge-graph facts makes the evidence behind the prediction inspectable, thereby enhancing explainability and supporting human-in-the-loop review. Simulations demonstrate that FinLLM-KG consistently improves portfolio performance over the state-of-the-art baseline.

</details>


### [132] [3, 7, 14, 17, 42: Instruction Tuning Installs and Sharpens Number-Choice Heuristics in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36178)
*Samar Ansari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过计算而不是抽样来确定语言模型对每个整数（1到100）分配的概率，评估了18种不同的模型系列在多种参数设置下的表现，发现调优使概率分布不再均匀，减少了对整数、末尾和圆整数的偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于通过深入分析语言模型生成随机数的概率分布，揭示模型在生成数值类型输出时的不同行为模式，从而理解模型的内部机制。

Method: 研究者采用了一种新颖的方法，即直接通过加总评分的拼写形式来计算每个整数的概率，消除了抽样带来的随机误差，从而更准确地评估模型在不同参数设置下的行为。

Result: 结果表明，调优显著改变了分布图谱，使得模型更加倾向于避开圆整数和末尾数字。这也引发了对模型中隐含的一些人类数字选择偏好（如避开圆整数和选择奇数）的兴趣。

Conclusion: 结论指出，虽然调优改变了模型的行为模式，但模型对数值的偏好在不同模型之间存在差异，某些偏好并不随调优而共同出现，表明语言模型的行为在一定程度上保留了其原始模型的特点。

Abstract: We measure, without sampling error, the probability a language model assigns to each integer from 1 to N when asked for a random number. For a fixed prompt the model defines one probability vector, which we compute by summing over each integer's scored spellings rather than estimate by sampling, removing the noise that limits earlier studies [1, 4]. We evaluate 18 open-weight model lines at five ranges up to N = 100, under 29 decoding configurations, on two inference stacks; 15 of them have matched base and instruction-tuned checkpoints that both give usable answers. Instruction tuning makes the distribution less uniform in all 15 pairs. It installs one heuristic, round-number avoidance, and sharpens another, endpoint avoidance. Base checkpoints do not avoid round numbers once the endpoints are removed from those sets; tuned checkpoints avoid them, multiples of 10 almost completely. Base checkpoints return the endpoints 1 and N at 0.30 to 0.60 of the interior rate; tuned checkpoints almost never return them. A preference for odd numbers is consistent across tuned checkpoints only at N = 100, where base checkpoints also show it weakly. Endpoint avoidance and the odd preference fit the account Kubovy and Psotka [6] proposed for human number choice; round-number avoidance is not part of that account, and the midpoint avoidance it predicts is essentially absent, with one slight exception at N = 100. Tuning does not install a new favourite integer shared across models: above N = 10 no modal integer is shared by more than 42 percent of tuned conditions, and P (17) rises in 10 of 15 pairs and falls in 5. The concentration is present in the model's own distribution: at temperature 1.0, nucleus truncation at top-p 0.95 adds a median of 0.035 to the total variation distance.

</details>


### [133] [Lipi-Ghor: A Large-Scale, Diarized, Multi-Domain Bengali Speech Corpus and a Scalable Pipeline for Building It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36184)
*Sanjid Hasan,A H M Fuad,Risalat Labib,Bayazid Hasan Simon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Lipi-Ghor 是首个大规模包含讲话者分段的孟加拉语声学语言数据集，涵盖了多个领域的内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语在口语研究中严重资源不足，Lipi-Ghor 的建设旨在提供一个填补这一空白的数据集。

Method: 通过从 YouTube 视频中收集音频并结合自动打分和孟加拉语字幕，创建了一个包括 885 小时音频和分段标注的数据集。

Result: Lipi-Ghor 是首个包含大量标注音频信息的孟加拉语语料库，四个广泛使用的 ASR 模型在其中表现欠佳。

Conclusion: 该项目提供了一个可复制的管道方法以构建适用于低资源语言的高质量语料库。

Abstract: Bengali, spoken by over 230 million people, remains severely under-resourced in spoken language research. Existing open Bengali ASR corpora are confined to short, read or crowd-sourced speech and lack speaker diarization and long-form audio.
  We present Lipi-Ghor, a large-scale weakly-supervised Bengali speech corpus comprising 885 hours of audio sourced from 998 YouTube videos across 596 unique channels, spanning 17 diverse domains including audiobooks, drama, religious sermons, news, and regional dialects. Each recording is annotated in SSTT format (Speaker, Speech, Transcription, Timestamp), which combines automated diarization with Bengali YouTube caption tracks.
  To our knowledge, Lipi-Ghor is the first Bengali speech corpus to pair large-scale coverage with speaker-diarized, long-form audio annotations, both of which are absent from existing open Bengali resources. We also present the construction methodology as a replicable pipeline for any language with a sufficient captioned YouTube presence. Targeted channel curation across a handful of rich domains yields corpus scales that were previously out of reach for low-resource languages.
  We benchmark four widely-used ASR models under zero-shot and fine-tuned conditions. All four degrade consistently on emotive, dialectal, and long-form content. Because caption targets are noisy, we also release a human-annotated gold evaluation split, verified by 17 volunteer annotators over 30 hours of human-in-the-loop correction and stratified across all 17 domains.
  We release our original contributions (video-ID lists, diarization output, pipeline code, and the gold annotations) under CC-BY-4.0, and distribute caption-derived transcripts for research use only; no raw audio is redistributed.

</details>


### [134] [Frankly Speaking: The Company Election Integrity Keeps in Congressional Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36187)
*Michael A. Robinette,Christina Barsky,Amanda D. Clark,Helen Flannery,Juniper Lovato*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究分析了2010年至2025年间美国国会的10,420封电子邮件，发现民主党与共和党在使用有关选举公正性的词汇时存在显著差异，这些差异体现在词义使用上，而非词汇本身的不同。到2024年，两党围绕选举词汇使用了截然不同的背景词语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨美国两党在选举完整性概念上的分歧及其在词汇使用上的体现。

Method: 利用历时词嵌入技术追踪词汇在上下文中的语义变化，测量两党在词汇使用上的差异，分析词汇的语义邻近关系，并研究辞彙的分化趋势。

Result: 研究发现，两党在选举相关的词汇使用上存在显著差异。从2010年至2024年，民主党围绕选举词汇使用了更多强调公民权益和支持选举系统的词语，而共和党则强调防止非法选票和公民身份验证。

Conclusion: 研究结论表明，虽然两党使用相同的词汇，却赋予其不同含义。这种分歧导致选举官员需在两种理解间找到平衡。

Abstract: Election integrity is a shared commitment in American politics, but Democrats and Republicans in Congress increasingly mean different things by it. Analyzing 10,420 congressional e-newsletters sent from 2010 to 2025, we examine how the two parties invoke election terms such as ``integrity,'' ``security,'' and ``fraud.'' Using diachronic word embeddings, which track semantic change through how words are used in context, we measure how differently each party uses each term, how much each party shifts, and whether they share nearest semantic neighbors. We show that members of Congress do not need different words to tell different stories about elections. They need only place those same words in different company. Across nearly every term and year, the two parties surround election-integrity vocabulary with entirely different words. That divergence is easy to miss, because most election vocabulary is no more divergent than other words used at roughly the same frequency, and neither party's usage shifted more than the other's. Only a subset of terms grew more divergent, most notably ``certification,'' while ``rigged'' and ``stolen'' entered election discourse after 2020 and polarized there. By 2024, Democrats surround ``voter,'' ``election,'' and ``integrity'' with the language of access and administration. Republicans surround the same terms with ``citizenship verification,'' ``noncitizen voting,'' and ``border security.'' For one party, integrity means protecting eligible voters' ability to cast a ballot. For the other, it means protecting the electoral process from illegitimate ballots. Election officials, who must administer a single system under both definitions, inherit this tension.

</details>


### [135] [LLM Hallucination Detection via Low-rank Multilinear Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36191)
*Devansh Sharma,Faizul Rakib Sayem,Shahana Ibrahim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了LOTUS框架，旨在通过多元低秩分解来从大型语言模型的隐藏状态中提取紧凑表示，从而提高幻觉检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究大型语言模型频繁生成语义流畅但事实错误的响应，因此需要开发更有效的幻觉检测方法。

Method: LOTUS框架首先通过互补的统计描述符汇总变长的token表示，捕捉峰值激活、激活分布及跨层变化，然后利用低秩多元分解提取紧凑的表示，这些表示用于训练轻量级的幻觉分类器。

Result: 在多个大型语言模型和问答基准测试上，本文方法在幻觉检测性能上优于最新的基线。

Conclusion: 提出的LOTUS框架能够更有效地从大规模语言模型的隐藏状态中提取关键信息，提高幻觉检测的准确性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently generate fluent yet factually incorrect responses, raising concerns about their reliability. Recent hallucination detectors increasingly exploit internal representations, but extracting informative signals from high-dimensional hidden states remains challenging. Existing approaches often rely on selected activations or pooling-based compression, potentially overlooking discriminative information distributed across Transformer layers, feature modes, and tokens. We propose LOTUS (Low-rank Order-statistic TUcker-based Subspace learning), a multilinear low-rank decomposition framework for hallucination detection that extracts compact representations from LLM hidden states while reducing dependence on labeled data. Our approach first summarizes variable-length token representations using complementary statistical descriptors that capture peak activations, activation distributions, and cross-layer changes. We then leverage low-rank multilinear decomposition to extract compact representations that capture the underlying structure of LLM hidden states, which are subsequently used to train a lightweight hallucination classifier. Experiments across multiple LLMs and question-answering benchmarks demonstrate improved hallucination detection performance over recent state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [136] [Beyond IceBERT: Open-Data ModernBERT Encoders for Icelandic from 12M to 1B Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36230)
*Hafsteinn Einarsson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究使用ModernBERT架构重新训练了具有12M到1B参数的冰岛语模型，并与IceBERT进行比较。1B参数的从零开始训练的模型在所有评估任务中表现更好，而从具有相同结构的英语Ettin模型进行预训练的模型在所有任务中得分最高。研究表明，预训练有助于模型性能的提升，但保留源词汇表会增加资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冰岛语是高度标准化的语言，冰岛语冰山（IceBERT）研究展示了从多语言模型中获取冰岛语特定知识的可能性。然而，冰山架构不一定适用于所有语言的现代需求。因此，研究进行了更深入的分析，使用ModernBERT架构和更大的模型规模在开放数据上重新训练冰岛语模型，以提高性能并验证不同训练策略的有效性。

Method: 研究团队使用ModernBERT架构对具有从12M到1B参数的不同规模的冰岛语模型进行了训练。他们使用了9B个开放数据标记，重复了IceBERT的基准测试并添加了五个新任务，总共评估了30个大小和预算组合的模型。研究团队还对不同预训练策略的效果进行了实验，包括从零开始训练和使用与源模型匹配的词汇表的预训练。

Result: 研究结果显示，1B参数的从零开始训练的冰岛语模型在所有评估任务中显著优于IceBERT。从英文Ettin模型预训练的冰岛语模型在所有任务中表现最佳。实验进一步表明，预训练有助于提升模型性能，但保留源模型的词汇表会增加资源消耗。随着模型规模的增加，大部分性能提升来自于源模型的变换器层，这表明即使是使用单语源模型也能获得显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 冰山模型的有效性得到了验证，使用ModernBERT架构并结合适当的预训练策略可以显著提升冰岛语模型的性能。研究结果表明，对于资源有限的特定语言研究，可以从零开始训练大模型是有效的。但对于更通用的任务，从多语言或多任务模型进行预训练仍然能够提供显著的性能提升。

Abstract: IceBERT, the reference encoder for Icelandic, found that a cleaned web crawl is enough to train a strong model and that starting from a multilingual model can help on some tasks. We revisit both findings with the ModernBERT architecture. We train Icelandic encoders from 12M to 1B parameters on 9B tokens of open data, and rerun IceBERT's benchmarks and five newer tasks for 30 encoders under one protocol with confidence intervals. Our 1B model trained from scratch, to our knowledge the first monolingual Icelandic encoder of that size, is significantly better than IceBERT on all eleven tasks; warm-started from the English Ettin encoder of the same shape, it has the best score of all encoders on every task we rank. Because our model shapes match the English Ettin encoders and the multilingual mmBERT, we can also test the warm start under controlled conditions, at six model sizes and four token budgets. With our vocabulary transplanted, a warm start was never significantly worse than scratch training on the same Icelandic text, and from base size up it was not significantly worse on four tasks than scratch training on 4 times the text (8 times at base and 1B). As models grow, most of the transfer comes from the source model's transformer layers, so even an English source helps. Keeping the source vocabulary instead cost 5 F1 on question answering and nearly three times the compute.

</details>


### [137] [Detect Before You Disambiguate: Perturbation-Based Input Ambiguity Detection in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36353)
*Tiejin Chen,Xiaoou Liu,Michael Clifford,Ahmadreza Moradipari,Yitao Chen,Kyungtae Han,Nejib Ammar,Hua Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种标签保持扰动框架，用于检测输入歧义。与现有方法不同，该框架能够在不改变原始输入标签的情况下检测歧义，并在多个数据集中实现了优于现有输出不确定性基线和LLM改写基线的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常依赖于LLM生成的解释或重新表述来处理输入歧义，这可能导致在测量歧义之前已经对其进行了解释。为此，该研究提出了一个标签保持的扰动框架，以直接检测输入歧义，强调检测歧义的重要性。

Method: 该研究提出了两种检测器：一种基于黑盒规则的扰动库，另一种基于白盒的重要性导向方法，利用模型内部信号。这两种方法都遵循在不改变原始输入标签的情况下检测歧义的原则。

Result: 该方法在开放域、对话和视觉问答四个数据集上进行了实验，并显示出优于输出不确定性基线和LLM改写基线的方法。

Conclusion: 本文的研究结果突显了在尝试消歧义之前检测输入歧义的重要性，作者还提供了该方法的代码开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often answer ambiguous inputs as if they had a single intended meaning. This is problematic because input ambiguity is an upstream source of uncertainty: when a prompt admits multiple valid interpretations, the key question is whether the model should answer directly at all. Existing input uncertainty methods often rely on LLM-generated statements or clarifications, but such transformations may already disambiguate the input before the ambiguity is measured. We propose a label-preserving perturbation framework for input ambiguity detection in which perturbations remain close to the original input while preserving its label. We instantiate this principle with two detectors: a black-box rule-based perturbation library and a white-box importance-guided method using model-internal signals. Experiments across four datasets in open-domain, conversational, and visual QA show that our methods outperform output-side uncertainty baselines and LLM-paraphrase-based perturbation baselines. Our results highlight the importance of detecting ambiguity before attempting to disambiguate it. Our code is available \href{https://github.com/Xiao0o0o/input-ambiguity-detection}{\textcolor{purple}{here}}.

</details>


### [138] [AK_QMUL at ArGuard Shared Tasks: A Single Fine-Tuned JAIS for Arabic Harmful-Prompt Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36360)
*Ali Khairallah,Arkaitz Zubiaga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了AK_QMUL对ArGuard-2026共享任务的提交，在两个任务中均获得了第一名。使用JAIS-6.7B模型进行4位QLoRA fine-tuning，并使用线性分类头处理模型的最后生成表示。针对二元安全/不安全任务，使用四分类头并结合AraSafe的MSA提示进行训练。对于七分类任务，进行细调后，通过五个随机种子的平均softmax输出作为最终结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进对阿拉伯语有害提示检测的性能，特别是在多种方言下，提出了一种新的细分方法来处理语言与地域的文化差异。

Method: 利用JAIS-6.7B模型，通过4位QLoRA fine-tuning，结合线性分类头，分别针对二元安全/不安全任务（B1）和七分类有害领域任务（B2）进行实验。对于B1任务，引入AraSafe的MSA提示进行训练增强；B2任务则通过五个随机种子的平均softmax输出实现七分类器的结果。

Result: 在B1任务中，模型在所有六种方言上表现一致（93.6% - 99.8%的宏F1分数），并且预测的不安全比率与真实值吻合良好。在B2任务中，六个类别中的五个获得了0.73到0.98的F1分数，错误主要集中在Bully类上，因为其涉及到骚扰和他人伤害的语义重叠。

Conclusion: AK_QMUL的提交在二元和七分类任务中均获得了最高排名，证明了所采用方法的有效性，并强调了模型对多种阿拉伯语方言的适应性。未来可以通过增加数据集和改进模型进一步优化性能。

Abstract: We present AK_QMUL's submissions to the ArGuard-2026 shared tasks on Arabic harmful-prompt detection: a binary safe/unsafe task (B1) and a seven-way harm-domain task (B2). Both submissions use one backbone -- JAIS-6.7B fine-tuned with 4-bit QLoRA and a linear classification head over its last-token representation. For B1 we train a four-way head that factors the label by dialect register {safe, unsafe} x {MSA, other}, we augment the training fold with MSA prompts from AraSafe, and decode with a plain argmax to score safe/unsafe. For B2 we fine-tune the same backbone as a seven-way classifier and average the softmax outputs of five random seeds. Both submissions ranked first on their track, with macro-F1 0.984 on B1 and 0.790 on B2. We report results by dialect and by class: on B1 the model is accurate across all six dialects (93.6--99.8%) and its predicted unsafe rate tracks the true rate closely; on B2 six of the seven classes reach F1 between 0.73 and 0.98, with the residual error concentrated in the Bully class, which overlaps semantically with harassment and harm-to-others.

</details>


### [139] [No Tokenizer, Still a Gap: Cross-Lingual Fairness of Homoglyph and Invisible-Character Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36432)
*Alexander Nemecek,Yuqiao Xu,Joseph Falkenburg,Erman Ayday*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 此研究探讨了使用同形字和不可见字符作为水印的方法，这些水印嵌入在文本中而不依赖于生成器模型。研究了不同语言和资源水平上的嵌入容量、存活率和所需检测器的文本量。结果显示，嵌入容量在不同字母表间波动较大，且相对较低资源语言中字母表的同形字数量较少，检测器的需求也随资源水平的变化而变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探讨基于文本编辑的嵌入水印方法，尤其是它们是否能在不同级别的资源语言中有效工作。

Method: 研究团队对40多种语言，24种字母表进行了测量，评估了每个字符可以承载的隐藏位数，水印在常规处理和针对性移除后的存活率，以及检测器需要的文本量。

Result: 结果显示，隐藏位数的容量在不同的字母表间差异达3.3倍，字母表类型的差异解释了96%的变异。在拉丁字母表中，资源水平对嵌入容量没有显著影响，但在所有字母表中，资源水平提高了嵌入容量（相关系数+0.54，95%置信区间为[+0.14, +0.83]），因为较低资源语言往往使用同形字较少的字母表。常见的复制粘贴操作能够移除不可见字符，需要约2个代码点的检测器在英文或波斯文中，但在老挝文中则需要约93个代码点。

Conclusion: 结论指出，尽管存在技术差距，但同形字和不可见字符作为水印的方法能够在不同资源和语言环境中有效工作，但这些方法的表现程度会受到所使用字母表的影响。并且，在移除分词器后，水印的不平等性并未消失，而是改变了其来源。

Abstract: Homoglyph and invisible-character watermarks are edit-based watermarks. Instead of changing how a model generates text, they hide a signal in the text after it is written. They swap some characters for homoglyphs, which look the same, or very similar, but have different Unicode code points, or they insert characters that do not display, all without access to the model that wrote the text. Generation-time watermarks work less well for lower-resourced languages due to how tokenizers split their text. Because they work on individual characters, these watermarks never touch a tokenizer, so we ask whether they escape this gap. Across 40 language varieties in 24 scripts, we measure capacity (the hidden bits each character can carry), how much of it survives routine handling and targeted removal, and how much text a keyed detector needs. Capacity varies 3.3-fold and script accounts for 96\% of that variation. Within Latin script, resource level makes no detectable difference ($ρ=+0.095$, $p=0.78$), but across scripts capacity rises with it ($ρ=+0.54$, 95\% CI $[+0.14,+0.83]$), because lower-resourced languages tend to use scripts with few homoglyphs. Ordinary copy-paste, which strips invisible characters, widens the gap: a detector then needs about 2 code points of English or Persian but 93 of Lao. Removing the tokenizer does not remove the inequity, but instead only changes its source.

</details>


### [140] [CONCUR: Concordant Auxiliary Rewards for Multi-Task, Multi-Reward Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36437)
*Jixuan Leng,Si Si,Fei Wang,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Jiong Zhang,Hsiang-Fu Yu,Inderjit S. Dhillon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的优势估计器CONCUR，它可以平衡大型语言模型在多任务学习中由于任务主要奖励和辅助奖励之间关系导致的性能下降问题。CONCUR能够在这种复杂的关系中保持模型性能的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的多任务学习中，任务的主要奖励和辅助奖励之间的关系可能导致模型性能的不平衡下降。为了改进这一问题，研究者提出了CONCUR，旨在平衡这些奖励之间的关系，从而提高模型的整体性能。

Method: CONCUR通过修改辅助奖励的标准化分数来实现，确保响应在主要奖励更高的情况下不能得到更低的得分，并在主要奖励上限时给予相同得分。CONCUR-T在整个训练过程中执行这两个条件，而CONCUR-A在大多数响应同意主要奖励排序更常见的情况下放宽第二个条件。

Result: CONCUR在三个模型中，在数学、代码、指令跟随和写作等多种任务上，两种变体均优于所有基线模型和去除所有辅助奖励的模型，显示出其有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，CONCUR可以在大型语言模型的多任务学习中，平衡主要奖励和辅助奖励的关系，有效提高模型的整体性能。

Abstract: When a Large Language Model (LLM) is trained with Reinforcement Learning (RL) on multiple tasks, a task's primary reward can gate auxiliary rewards such as a length reward: on a failing response, an auxiliary cannot exceed the primary. However, group-relative advantage estimators treat a gated auxiliary like any other component, so it can reverse the primary's order, and at the primary's ceiling only auxiliaries order responses. Keeping gated auxiliaries hurts some tasks, while dropping them hurts others. We propose CONCUR, an advantage estimator that replaces the standardized scores of each gated auxiliary with the closest scores that satisfy two conditions: responses with higher primary rewards cannot receive lower scores, and responses at the primary's ceiling receive equal scores. CONCUR-T applies both conditions throughout training, and CONCUR-A lifts the second for an auxiliary that agrees with the primary's order more often than it reverses it among passing responses. On three models from two families and across math, code, instruction following, and writing, both variants score higher on average than all baselines and than when all the gated auxiliaries are dropped.

</details>


### [141] [Do AI Scientists Know What's New?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36511)
*Miri Liu,Xiayu Zhao,Encheng Xie,ChengXiang Zhai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为ClaimNov的模块，用以评估生成想法的原创性，通过在四个数据集上进行验证，发现现有的新颖性检查在拒绝非原创工作时也会误报真正的原创想法，而ClaimNov能更细粒度、解释地检测提案中的特定主张，保持了更多真正原创的提案，并显著降低了误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的新颖性检查不能有效地区分哪些部分是不新颖的，会大量地拒绝原创想法，本文旨在设计一种能有效评估科学发现管道中新颖性的分析框架。

Method: 作者设计了一个基于时间地真相的评估框架，使用了四个数据集进行验证，涵盖了1,060个提案，并引入了一个细粒度的、可解释的方法ClaimNov来评估提案中的特定主张。

Result: ClaimNov能够在1.5%的误报率下捕捉到97%的伪装论文，并在拒绝非原创工作时显著减少了误报率，同时保留了更多的真正原创提案。

Conclusion: 本文提出的ClaimNov模块可以有效地评估生成想法的原创性，相比于现有方法，它可以更精确地识别出提案中的非原创主张，减少误报并保留更多原创的提案。

Abstract: A major challenge facing AI scientific discovery pipelines is how to determine when a generated idea is meaningfully novel. Ideally, novelty checks in these pipelines must be able to reject non-novel ideas while not rejecting too many novel ideas, and they should also be able to distinguish which parts of a proposal are non-novel, as opposed to opaquely discarding entire proposals. We design an analytical framework for evaluation of novelty checks with temporal ground truth at both the proposal- and claim-level, using four datasets of 1,060 proposals built from published papers, reviewer disputes, and generated ideas matching ones the field went on to publish. Auditing existing novelty checks with this framework, we find that checks with high rejection rates on non-novel work also tend to have high false alarms on ideas that are later realized by human researchers, and observe that checks built to evaluate proposals as a whole can point to claim-level prior art only by rejecting the whole proposal. We therefore introduce ClaimNov, a fine-grained, interpretable claim-level novelty module. ClaimNov catches 97% of disguised papers at a 1.5% false-alarm rate, and its claim-level novelty profile cuts the LLM judge's false alarms from 11.8% to 1.5%. Because its verdict rests on claim-level evidence, ClaimNov also keeps proposals that build on established contributions, rejecting only 3% of ICLR-accepted proposals with a reviewer-disputed contribution and keeping 91 of 100 novel proposals with one spliced prior contribution, against 76 for the strongest existing check. Code and data are available at https://github.com/emmatliu/claimnov.

</details>


### [142] [Benchmarking Task Takeover Across Agent Handoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36523)
*Wenyuan Li,Guang Li,Dongyuan Li,Keisuke Maeda,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HSC-Bench 提供了一个基准来评估代理模型在协作任务中的责任移交能力，特别是恢复相关证据和继续继承的状态，结果显示结构化的移交状态能显著提升模型的继续任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着代理模型在长时间任务中合作，责任在模型之间转移。需要一个评估标准来衡量这种转变的质量，而单纯的评价指标如任务成功和记忆保留无法全面反映转换情况。因此，引入了一种新的基准——HSC-Bench，它由多项指标组成，不仅评估恢复相关证据，还评估继续继承状态的能力。

Method: HSC-Bench 包含1,039个任务，评估接收模型在任务接管时的两个方面：恢复相关证据和继续继承状态。使用六种不同的接收模型进行比较，评估结果显示，结构化的移交状态有助于提高对相关证据的恢复及大多数模型的继续任务性能。

Result: 六种接收模型中，结构化的移交状态对每种模型恢复相关证据都有效，对大多数模型的继续任务也有显著提升。两个任务接管流程中的最好接收者是不一样的，表明仅基于恢复证据的排名不能全面评价模型的接管能力。

Conclusion: HSC-Bench 为选择接收模型和评估交接表示提供了基础，特别是在必须继续原有工作的场景中。

Abstract: As agents collaborate on longer tasks, responsibility may pass between models before the work is complete. The receiving model must build on prior decisions, observations, and actions while addressing the remaining requirements. Evaluating this transition requires connecting the information preserved at handoff to how the receiver continues the work, a relationship that task-success and memory-retention scores alone do not capture. We introduce the Handoff State Continuity Benchmark (HSC-Bench), comprising 1,039 tasks that evaluate two complementary aspects of task takeover: recovering relevant repository evidence and continuing from established conversational, tool, and file-work state. Comparisons across six receiving models show that structured handoff state improves repository evidence recovery for every model and continuation performance for most models. The strongest receivers differ between the two tracks, showing that evidence-recovery rankings alone do not characterize performance when continuing from inherited state. By combining these comparisons with checks of subsequent responses and actions, HSC-Bench evaluates both access to prior evidence and adherence to established commitments and completed work. This provides a basis for selecting receiving models and assessing handoff representations according to the work that must be carried forward.

</details>


### [143] [Causal Localization of Evidence Effects in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36524)
*Jipeng Li,Pramod Khargonekar,Yanning Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 这篇论文介绍了一种称为可控证据聚合（CEA）的方法，通过调整输入中的符号数量和顺序，可以识别出影响语言模型决策的注意力头集合。该研究不仅在符号输入任务上验证，还在自然语言任务中验证了这些注意力头的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何通过控制方式的符号输入揭示语言模型的决策过程中的关键注意力头，以加深对模型内部机制的理解。

Method: 提出了可控证据聚合（CEA）方法，该方法通过在固定提示中调整两种符号的数量和顺序，测量冻结的Transformer模型在两个答案标记上的偏好变化。同时采用平衡的效用因果选择（BUC）方法来识别紧凑的注意力头集合并量化它们的影响。

Result: 在Pythia-410M模型中，通过选择1-12个特定注意力头，可以消除65.5-91.6%的符号数量效果。即使在仅对符号yes/no提示的选择中，去除八个特定注意力头也使均值准确性降低了8.2个百分点，而随机选择相同数量和层数的注意力头集只降低了0.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了可控符号探针能够在符号和自然语言任务中识别出对模型决策有因果影响的小型注意力头集合。

Abstract: Can controlled symbolic inputs reveal attention heads that influence a language model's decisions from natural language evidence? We introduce controlled evidence aggregation (CEA), which varies the counts and order of two symbols in otherwise fixed prompts and measures changes in a frozen Transformer's preference between two answer tokens. The preference is measured by their logit difference; the prompts prescribe no correct answer. Balanced utility causal selection (BUC) identifies compact head sets by jointly evaluating how much ablating their outputs removes the count effect and how closely patching mean outputs between count conditions recovers the effect in both directions. We derive conditional bounds linking head attribution to intervention effects and specifying when the attribution shortlist can be recovered from finite samples. In Pythia-410M, ablating sets of 1-12 heads selected separately for four ordering regimes removes 65.5-91.6% of the count effect. The sets retain their influence on count comparisons not used for selection. To test relevance beyond symbolic inputs, we evaluate a natural language task asking which state is supported by more reports. Ablating eight heads selected only on symbolic yes/no prompts lowers mean accuracy by 8.2 percentage points, versus 0.8 for random sets matched by size and layer. Our results demonstrate that controlled symbolic probes can identify compact attention head sets whose causal influence extends to natural language decisions.

</details>


### [144] [ETC-Bench: A Multilingual Benchmark for Evaluation-Side Transcript Canonicalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36527)
*Zhiheng Qian,Junhao Du,Jing Peng,Hanqi Li,Kunyang Peng,Feng Xue,Kai Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了评价端转录标准化（ETC）的概念，并构建了ETC-Bench基准测试，涵盖了12种语言中的181种现象类型。此外，引入了语义保持精确匹配规范化准确度（SENA）和现象导向代理规范化技能（TACS），以评估转录规范化和语义保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前在转录领域缺乏多语言的标准化基准测试，尤其是涉及不同语言中的多种现象类型的测试。因此，研究旨在填补这一空白，提供一种多语言的转录标准化基准，以评估转录规范化和语义保持能力。

Method: 研究通过构建一个多语言现象类型基准ETC-Bench，并引入语义保持精确匹配规范化准确度（SENA）和现象导向代理规范化技能（TACS）来评估转录的标准化和语义保持能力。通过五个基准配置和三个TACS代理的实验，验证了TACS在共享语言中的优越性。

Result: 实验结果表明，TACS比基准系统在共享语言上表现更好，证明了该方法的有效性和实用性。ETC-Bench为跨语言的转录标准化提供了系统化的测试平台。
