Das,Tyler Wang,Udit Saxena,Ulrick Blé,Umar Jamil,Umberto Tomasini,Valentin Macé,Van Phung,Venkata Reddy,Vibha Masti,Victor Jouault,Victor Letzelter,Victor Paltz,Victor Poucheret,Vincent Maladière,Vincent Pfister,Virgile Richard,Vladislav Bataev,Wassim Bouaziz,Wen Ding Li,William Flanagan,William Havard,William Marshall,Witold Jędrzejewski,Xinghui Li,Xingran Guo,Xinyu Yang,Xuanyu Zhang,Yann Dreze,Yann Viegas,Yannic Neuhaus,Yassine Turki,Yessin Moakher,Yi He,Yihan Wang,Yimu Pan,Yuying Zhu,Yves Martin des Taillades,Zaccharie Ramzi,Zhenlin Xu,Zsófia Csákány*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Timestral 是一个四款时间序列基础模型系列，拥有不同参数量版本，通过 Decoupled Horizon Forecasting、支持已知未来协变量的框架和双重训练管道，在多个基准测试中取得了优异成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于传统方法在长时序预测和处理已知未来协变量方面存在局限性，因此提出了Timestral模型来解决这些问题。

Method: Timestral采用了Decoupled Horizon Forecasting来分离预测令牌和因果编码上下文，支持已知未来协变量的框架，并使用双重训练管道在大量合成数据上预训练，再在混合真实和合成数据上进行后训练。

Result: Timestral系列模型在多个基准测试中取得了最佳成绩，尤其Timestral-209M和Timestral-123M在所有指标上排名第一和第二。

Conclusion: Timestral模型的设计和基准测试结果表明，它在处理时间序列预测问题上具有显著优势，其权重和推理代码可以公开获取。

Abstract: We introduce Timestral, a family of four time series foundation models for probabilistic forecasting, with 5M, 28M, 123M, and 209M parameters. Timestral sets a new state of the art on GIFT-Eval, TIME, and fev-bench, where Timestral-209M and Timestral-123M take the top two places on every metric. We achieve this with three ingredients: Decoupled Horizon Forecasting (DHF), which separates prediction tokens from the causally encoded context, so that longer horizons are trained without masking context and each observed value is both a forecast target and context for later forecasts; a covariate framework that natively supports known-future covariates within a causal architecture; and a two-stage pipeline that pretrains exclusively on a diverse, large-scale synthetic corpus before post-training on a mix of real and synthetic data with extended context. This report describes the model design and its benchmark results. Weights for all four models are available on Hugging Face and inference code on GitHub.

</details>


### [276] [What Improves During Pretraining? Tracking Knowledge Acquisition with Point-in-Time Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37631)
*Chang Su,Bin Wu,Emine Yilmaz,Yao Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过引入时间变异得分（TVS）来评估不同年份网络文本中知识的表示一致性，发现具有高TVS的项目在预训练后期表现更好，特别是在知识密集型任务中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型的学习机制尚不完全清楚，特别是不同种类的知识如何随时间学习。

Method: 研究使用来自不同开放源代码模型系列的六个模型来评估六个涵盖各种主题的基准。提出了时间变异得分（TVS）的概念，并通过比较不同模型在不同时间点的表现，研究知识学习的变化。

Result: 研究发现，低TVS和高TVS的项目在预训练早期具有相似的表现，但在后期，高TVS项目的表现显著优于低TVS项目，优势平均为7.3个点的规范化准确性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，随着时间的推移，知识的变异对模型学习效果有重要影响，且这种影响在知识密集型任务中更为明显。

Abstract: Language models pretrained on web corpora collected over multiple years learn both general and domain-specific knowledge. How these different kinds of knowledge are learned as pretraining progresses remains unclear. Recent point-in-time models trained on web snapshots from different years provide a way to probe how knowledge prevalence varies over time. Using these models, we propose a temporal variability score (TVS), defined as the coefficient of variation of an item's negative log-likelihood across models. A higher score suggests that the knowledge may be less consistently represented across years of web text. We evaluate six benchmarks covering diverse topics using six models from multiple open-source model families. We find that low- and high-TVS items are learned similarly early in training, but high-TVS items improve faster later on. At the end of the pretraining run, high-TVS items outperform low-TVS items in 34 of 36 model-benchmark settings, with an average advantage of 7.3 points of chance-normalized accuracy. This pattern is most robust on knowledge-intensive tasks, even when comparing items of similar difficulty. Our findings reveal differences in how knowledge is learned over the course of pretraining, with temporal variability providing a diagnostic of these learning dynamics beyond aggregate benchmark scores.

</details>


### [277] [KPPax: preprocess differentiable kinetic ODE for machine learning in atmospheric chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37655)
*Wenqing Peng,Michael Boy,Zhi-Song Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KPPax是一个用于生成可用于不同iable编程框架化学代码的工具，可以应用于参数估计和物理-机器学习模型中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的KPP机制不能满足机器学习模型发展中的可微编程框架需求，KPPax的提出旨在解决这一问题。

Method: KPPax通过将KPP机制语言转化为JAX框架下的化学代码，利用稀疏操作和log空间运算保持不同的可微性，结合	exttt{diffrax}库中的隐式求解器和反向传播技术来获取精确的灵敏度。

Result: 通过使用KPPax生成的代码，可以预测参数可识别性并准确估计可识别系数的不确定性；或者通过神经网络修正排放清单，提高生物源排放估计效果并恢复低温排放信息。

Conclusion: KPPax使KPP机制可以直接应用于参数估计和物理-机器学习模型中，为大气化学过程的研究提供了一种新的工具。

Abstract: Atmospheric chemical mechanisms are commonly distributed in the language of the Kinetic PreProcessor (KPP), which generates efficient Fortran or C code but does not fit into the differentiable programming frameworks in which machine-learning models are developed. We present KPPax, a kinetic preprocessor that generates differentiable, just-in-time compiled, and vectorisable chemistry code in JAX from mechanisms written in the KPP language. KPPax evaluates the rate law with sparse operations in log space, which keeps it differentiable, and integrates the stiff system with the implicit solvers and the forward- and reverse-mode adjoints of the \texttt{diffrax} library, so that exact sensitivities are obtained without hand-derived adjoint code. We demonstrate two applications with the POLLU benchmark mechanism. In the first, the Fisher information matrix computed from an automatically differentiated Jacobian predicts, before any fit, which of the 25 rate coefficients a flow-tube-like design can identify, and noisy synthetic estimation confirms the predicted uncertainties of the nine identifiable rate coefficients to within about 30\% while revealing clusters of fast reactions of which only ratios are constrained. In the second, a neural-network correction of an emission inventory is trained offline on per-scenario 4D-Var retrievals and then fine-tuned online through the chemical model, which reduces the error of the biogenic correction by a factor of 2.4--5 and recovers information on low-temperature emissions that the individual retrievals lose. KPPax makes KPP mechanisms directly usable for parameter estimation and hybrid physics--machine-learning modelling.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [278] [RV565: Cross-Layer Co-Design for Frame-Independent Video Playback on MicroPython-Class ESP32-S3 Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35935)
*Ayushman Bhattacharya,Anwesha Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: RV565框架为SPI附带微控制器上的帧独立视频解码，通过在传输过程中利用双核流水线上解压缩下一个帧实现了高帧率，同时避免了重叠的直接内存访问（DMA）传输的连续时间超限。研究表明，Deflate在解压缩RGB565帧时无法充分利用邻近像素间的冗余性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对SPI-连接的微控制器显示，传统解码方法主要受制于数据位置和处理阶段的串行性。论文旨在提出一种新的解码框架，以解决这些问题，特别是在ESP32-S3硬件平台上显示长达10秒的视频片段。

Method: 论文介绍了RV565解码框架，该框架能够生成面板的原生RGB565格式的解码表示。研究通过在ESP32-S3上运行十个视频片段窗口进行评估，分析了不同处理阶段对解码性能的影响，并利用双核流水线技术优化了解码过程。

Result: 研究表明，使用RV565解码框架可以在保证高帧率的情况下避免连续时间的超限。对于较轻的视频片段，采用串行路径实现与直接内存访问（DMA）传输重叠，性能达标。对于较重的视频片段，双核流水线运行实现了24 FPS，帧时间不超出显示传输时间的98.1%。此外，研究发现Deflate在解压缩RGB565帧时无法有效利用邻近像素间的冗余性。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的RV565解码框架为SPI-连接微控制器显示提供了高效且可靠的视频解码方法，相比Deflate而言，它可以更有效地处理大尺寸RGB565帧并节省更多的带宽。此外，该框架为MicroPython应用操作系统中的固件膨胀提供了高的帧率，但遇到的挑战包括文件系统输入的序列化、外部RAM的放置以及显示呈现的阻塞现象。

Abstract: Video on SPI-attached microcontroller displays is limited less by decoding than by where each byte lives and which stages run serially. We present RV565, a decode framework for frame-independent video whose decoded representation is the panel's native RGB565 format, and evaluate it on an ESP32-S3 with two ten-second windows of an openly licensed film. A serial path - inflate, then scale into internal-memory stripes overlapped with SPI direct-memory-access (DMA) transfers - meets the 41.7 ms deadline on the lighter window but misses all 7,200 frames of the heavier one. RV565's dual-core pipeline inflates the next frame on the second core during the transfer and sustains 24 FPS with zero misses and 10.3 ms of slack on both windows; frame time then equals the display transfer, which runs at 98.1% of the 40 MHz wire-time bound. Because frames are independent, inflation time is linear in the compressed length stored in the container (pooled r^2 = 0.9987): a model fitted on one window predicts the other within 0.04 ms and identifies from metadata alone which window the serial path cannot schedule. We show why Deflate cannot exploit co-located temporal redundancy in RGB565 frames larger than 128x128 pixels and measure the price: a shared Deflate stream changes size by at most 1.1%, whereas an explicit XOR delta would save 46-80%. Inside OreoOS, a MicroPython application operating system, firmware inflation alone reaches 12.2 FPS; stage timing attributes the 80.4 ms frame time to serialized filesystem input, external-RAM placement, and blocking presentation, with 0.8 ms of interpreter overhead. Firmware, measurements, and analysis scripts are released.

</details>


### [279] [A State Augmented Approach to Paralle Modeling in High-Level Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36481)
*Nicholas Kovacs,Hao Zheng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于同步序列过程的明确架构建模方法，用C++表示持续状态、通信和反馈控制，HLS负责调度和绑定操作。这种方法显著提高了处理器在FPGA上的性能和面积效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反馈耦合设计的顺序行为模型可能会掩盖必要的架构状态边界和消除了高效重叠所需的故障处理，因此需要一种新的架构建模方法来提高HLS在FPGA上的性能。

Method: 研究采用了一种基于同步序列过程的建模方法，所有的持续状态、通信和反馈控制都用C++表示。HLS对这种结构进行了调度和绑定操作。

Result: 与算法驱动的HLS基准相比，优化的配置实现了每指令1.221个周期，速度为220 MHz，在Kintex UltraScale+ FPGA上性能显著提高。同时，面积优化配置减少了65%的LUT和47%的寄存器，但基准周期增加了9.7%，且实现了250 MHz的时钟。

Conclusion: 研究表明，设计师控制的架构和工具控制的调度之间存在分离，这为FPGA上有效的HLS实现提供了新的研究和优化机会。

Abstract: High-level synthesis (HLS) can schedule only the concurrency made legal by its input description. For feedback-coupled designs, a sequential behavioral model can obscure the architectural state boundaries and hazard resolution needed for efficient overlap. We develop an explicit architectural modeling methodology based on synchronous sequential processes that start in lockstep and synchronize completion before repeating. Persistent state, communication, and feedback control are represented in C++, while HLS schedules and binds operations within that structure. A five-stage RV32I processor illustrates three consequential modeling decisions: which version of a value a consumer reads, when it consumes that value, and how architectural memory actions map to port operations. On a Kintex UltraScale+ FPGA, the CPI-optimized configuration achieves 1.221 cycles per instruction at 220 MHz, compared with 4.000 for an algorithmic HLS baseline and 1.575 at 242 MHz for an Ibex reference. An area-optimized configuration reduces LUTs by 65\% and registers by 47\%, with 9.7\% more benchmark cycles and a 250 MHz achieved clock. These results demonstrate a separation between designer-controlled architecture and tool-controlled scheduling.

</details>


### [280] [Comparative Analysis of Large Load Interconnection Requirements Across U.S. Transmission Entities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36645)
*Rafi Zahedi,Rahul Anilkumar,Amin Zamani*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文分析了美国11个电力实体在电网连接要求方面的差异，发现数据中心申请人可以作为潜在的电网资源，提出了一份结构化基准，帮助各方面识别差距和加速制定连接标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鉴于大数据中心快速发展对北美电力网络的巨大需求，当前技术性的连接需求仍存在多方面不成熟的情况，本文旨在通过对比分析不同电力实体的技术连接要求，识别缺口并提供基准参考。

Method: 本文通过对AEP、Ameren、CAISO等11个美国电力实体的电网连接要求进行了全面比较分析，覆盖了包括数据负荷定义、数据模型、故障持续运行能力等七大技术类别。

Result: 分析结果显示，LIPA在所有七大技术类别上至少部分进行了覆盖，Dominion和Duke Energy则几乎全面覆盖。同时，表明数据中心申请人可以被视为潜在的电网资源，而不仅仅是消费端。

Conclusion: 这项研究为电力公司、监管机构、ISO/RTO和开发商提供了一个结构化的基准，有助于识别差距、进行同行比较，并加速电网连接标准的制定。

Abstract: The rapid growth of large loads, especially data centers driven by artificial intelligence, is creating unprecedented demands on the North American electric grid. Data centers now account for nearly 80% of large load interconnection requests in the Western Interconnection, and U.S. electricity consumption from data centers is projected to reach 325-580 TWh by 2028. Despite this urgency, technical interconnection requirements for large loads have not yet matured and still contain several gaps. This paper presents a comparative analysis of large load interconnection requirements across eleven U.S. entities, including AEP, Ameren, CAISO, Dominion, Duke Energy, ERCOT, LIPA, MISO, PJM, Southern Company, and SPP, under seven technical categories: large load definition, load data and modeling, fault ride-through, load curtailment and shedding, dispatchability, power quality, and reactive power control. The analysis reveals significant variation in both the scope and specificity of requirements. LIPA addresses all seven technical categories at least partially, while Dominion and Duke Energy provide detailed coverage across nearly all categories. The analysis also finds that data center applicants can be viewed as potential grid resources, rather than only as consumers. The findings provide a structured baseline from which utilities, regulators, ISOs/RTOs, and developers can identify gaps, benchmark against peers, and accelerate the development of interconnection standards.

</details>


### [281] [p-DCIM: A 28nm Probabilistic SRAM DCIM based Ising Machine for Near-Memory Context Selection in LLM-RAG Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37033)
*Madhav Vadlamani,Rahul Singh,Yuyao Kong,Haomei Liu,Zheng Zhang,Shimeng Yu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究介绍了一种基于结构寄存器(SRAM)的DCIM自旋玻璃机，用于大型语言模型(RAG)中检索增强生成(RAG)的集合级上下文选择。通过将证据选择重新形式化为二次无约束二元优化(QUBO)问题，并在同一个乘累加数据路径上计算嵌入相似性得分和评估自旋贺马顿能差值来解决该问题。这种方法使得推理管道中的能量消耗大幅降低，并提高了查询的能量效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RAG中，证据选择阶段越来越依赖于重复的语言模型重新排名，这增加了推理管道的能量开销。因此，研究者试图通过将证据选择任务重新形式化为QUBO问题，并在基于DCIM的自旋玻璃机上解决这个问题来降低推理管道的能量开销。

Method: 该方法首先将证据选择任务重新形式化为QUBO问题，并使用一种新型的DCIM架构来解决这一问题。在该架构中，相同的乘累加数据路径可以同时计算嵌入相似性得分和评估自旋贺马顿能差值。同时，通过将退火温度设置为数字平面对数控制和SRAM伪读随机位翻转，研究者能够实现完全在片上的数字配置降级冷却。

Result: 该方法测试后的结果表明，这种方法能够将缩放后选择能量的估计值比基于语言模型的基线降低超过6个数量级，并将每个查询评估的总能量降低67%，同时保持了可比较的标记级F1得分。

Conclusion: 研究者得出结论，将语言模型选择过程替换为近内存Ising求解器将该宏的总能量估计值从基于LLM的基线降低超过6个数量级，并降低了查询评估的总能量67%，同时保持了标记级F1得分的可比性。研究者建议将该宏集成到GPU包中的高带宽内存(HBM)和高带宽闪存(HBF)堆栈的逻辑基片上，并且只有选定的段标识符返回到主机。

Abstract: We present a fabricated SRAM DCIM Ising machine that performs set-level context selection for large language model (LLM) with retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). In RAG, the evidence-selection stage is increasingly delegated to repeated language-model reranking, which adds substantial energy to the inference pipeline. We reformulate evidence selection as a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) problem and solve it on a dual-mode digital compute-in-memory (DCIM) macro: the same multiply-and-accumulate datapath computes the embedding-similarity scores that populate the QUBO coefficients and evaluates the per-spin Hamiltonian differences that drive the anneal. The annealing temperature is set by a digital plane-count control along with the SRAM pseudo-read random bit flips, giving graded cooling entirely from on-chip digital configuration. The macro is taped-out in a 28 nm process [0.16 mm2, 0.41 mW] and is proposed to be integrated as part of the logic base-die in hybrid high-bandwidth memory (HBM) and high-bandwidth flash (HBF) stacks in a GPU package, where only the selected passage identifiers return to the host. Replacing the language-model selection pass with the near-memory Ising solver yields a macro normalized selection-energy estimate more than six orders of magnitude lower than the LLM-based baseline and reduces the evaluated total energy per query by 67%, while maintaining comparable token-level F1 on the evaluated examples.

</details>


### [282] [Hardware-Matched Spiking Neural Networks for Real-Time Purkinje Cell Spike Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37104)
*Arooba Farhan,Afsah Syed,Muhammad Fahd Ibrahim Bhatti,Muhammad Areeb Khalid Kidwai,Muhammad Ahsan Salar Khan,Vincenzo Romano,Muhammad Ali Siddiqi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种紧凑的神经网络（SNN）：ConvSNN 和 StreamSNN，分别适用于矩阵矢量处理器（MVP）和 SKY130 电路，实现了对 Purkinje 细胞（PC）复杂尖峰（CSs）和简单尖峰（SSs）的有效分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PC 信号记录通常依赖于有线探针，限制了自由行为动物的自然运动。为了克服这一限制，需要一种能在受限硬件上实时准确运行的尖峰分类方法。

Method: 本文提出了两种专门针对目标硬件优化的紧凑型神经网络：一种是映射到EFM32PG28 微控制器Matrix Vector Processor (MVP)的ConvSNN，另一是一种每时间步处理一个波形值的StreamSNN，使用学习到的时间常数和固定突触延迟。

Result: ConvSNN 在未见过的细胞上达到了0.873 的 CS F1 得分，使用了1,018 个参数和8位权重；StreamSNN 达到了0.837 的 CS F1 得分，使用了527 个参数。在微控制器上，MVP 加速使 ConvSNN 的延迟减少了62.3%，预期电池寿命延长至33.73小时。StreamSNN 占用0.173 平方毫米的面积，在每个事件上消耗0.46μJ，具有0.2ms 的计算时间，在8.3ms 的端到端延迟内完成。

Conclusion: 这两种模型在有效性和效率方面取得了平衡，为 PC 的实时分类提供了有潜力的解决方案。

Abstract: Distinguishing Purkinje cell (PC) simple spikes (SSs) from complex spikes (CSs) is key to studying cerebellar function. PC recordings commonly rely on wired headstages, whose tethers restrict natural movement in freely behaving animals. Untethered headstages remove this constraint, but cannot continuously transmit raw recordings within their power budgets, making on-headstage spike classification an attractive form of compression, provided it runs accurately in real time on constrained hardware. This work presents two compact spiking neural networks (SNNs) matched to their target hardware: ConvSNN, which maps to the Matrix Vector Processor (MVP) of an EFM32PG28 microcontroller, and StreamSNN, which processes one waveform value per timestep using learned time constants and fixed synaptic delays for a dedicated SKY130 circuit. Evaluated on cells unseen during training, ConvSNN achieves a CS F1 of 0.873 with 1,018 parameters and StreamSNN 0.837 with 527 parameters, both with eight-bit weights. On the microcontroller, MVP acceleration reduces ConvSNN latency by 62.3\% and extends estimated battery life to 33.73~hours on a 50~mAh battery. In SKY130, StreamSNN occupies 0.173~mm$^2$ and consumes 0.46~$μ$J per event, with 0.2~ms of computation within an 8.3~ms end-to-end latency.

</details>


### [283] [AnalogScout: Simulation-Efficient PVT-Aware Analog Sizing with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37319)
*Yichen Long,Keren Zhu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: AnalogScout 是一种使用 LLM 代理驱动的 PVT 意识化模拟高效化尺寸框架，通过精英设计重用、局部解析 gm/Id 尺寸和全局验证，与进化策略 CMA-ES 相比，减少了 43.3% 的功率消耗，并在 16k 模拟预算下达到了与之相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的模拟方法耗时且效率低下，需要在所有 PVT 角度上模拟每个候选尺寸，以确保成功流片。使用 LLM 代理可以减少模拟预算的开销。

Method: AnalogScout 通过精英设计重用，并结合解析局部 gm/Id 尺寸与全球校正，在整个 PVT 角度上进行了全面验证，使用 LLM 代理进行决策。

Result: AnalogScout 在三个 SKY130 OTAs 上表现出色，相比 CMA-ES，功率降低了 43.3%，且在 16k 模拟预算下实现了相当的性能。此外，相较于 Bayesian 优化和两种重新实现的 LLM 驱动流水线，AnalogScout 在所有三个 OTAs 上都表现出了更低的功率消耗。

Conclusion: AnalogScout 通过结合精英设计策略、解析尺寸技术与全局优化，实现了高效且准确的参数化设计，大幅降低了模拟预算的要求。

Abstract: Analog device sizing must satisfy all specifications across process, voltage, and temperature (PVT) corners to ensure tape-out success, but simulating every candidate at all corners dominates the sizing time and slows design iteration. Conventional surrogate-based optimizers search the sizing space directly, while recent large language model (LLM) agents add circuit knowledge to designs but still delegate the sizes to global optimizers. Both learn the circuit response from repeated multi-corner SPICE simulations and optimize on top of it, which becomes expensive under a limited simulation budget. Providing an agent with reusable verified designs and sizing sub-blocks analytically could save most of this budget. We propose AnalogScout, a simulation-efficient PVT-aware sizing framework driven by an LLM agent. Combining elite-design reuse, analytic gm/Id local sizing and global refinement under full-corner verification, AnalogScout reduces power by 43.3% relative to the evolution strategy CMA-ES at an equal budget of 16k simulations on three SKY130 OTAs of increasing complexity, and reaches the final CMA-ES power after 7.0% of that budget. It also attains lower power than Bayesian optimization on all three OTAs and than two re-implemented LLM-driven flows on the two OTAs where they were tested.

</details>


### [284] [Kernel Performance Engineering under Physical Non-Idealities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37466)
*Supun Kuruppu,Pranav Dangi,Wei Li,Zhenyu Bai,Tulika Mitra*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 研究展示了物理非理想因素如何影响GPU性能，并提出了一种波感知聚类策略来提升性能。在不同工作负载下，该策略表现出不同的改善效果，特别是在应对功率限制时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了克服分布式共享内存（DSMEM）带来的硬件资源限制，提高GPU上的内核性能。

Method: 通过分析物理非理想因素对GPU资源的限制，提出了波感知聚类策略。

Result: 该策略在使用全球加载和广播微基准测试、GEMM和Hopper版本的FlashAttention 4时，能够提高性能，其中在FlashAttention 4中吞吐量最多可以提高5.30%。

Conclusion: 功率限制下，减少数据移动能通过动态电压和频率调节（DVFS）提高Streaming Multiprocessor的频率，从而解释了意外的性能提升。此分析显示了在不同加速器中考虑物理非理想因素的重要性。

Abstract: Physical non-idealities in integrated circuit (IC) design and fabrication are concealed by conventional hardware abstractions, yet they can limit kernel performance and obscure optimization opportunities. Using distributed shared memory (DSMEM) on NVIDIA H100 GPUs as a case study, we show how floorsweeping restricts hardware resources available for clustered execution, potentially offsetting the benefits of reduced data movement enabled by DSMEM. Guided by these insights, we develop a wave-aware clustering strategy that accounts for these resource constraints to improve kernel performance. Evaluations using a global-load-and-broadcast microbenchmark, GEMM, and the Hopper version of FlashAttention 4 demonstrate the performance implications of these constraints, with wave-aware clustering improving FlashAttention 4 throughput by up to 5.30% over its baseline. We further show that, under thermal design power (TDP) limits, reduced data movement enables higher Streaming Multiprocessor (SM) frequencies through Dynamic Voltage and Frequency Scaling (DVFS), explaining otherwise unexpected throughput improvements. Extending this analysis beyond GPUs, we show that power constraints also limit throughput scaling on Tenstorrent's Wormhole accelerator, highlighting the broader importance of accounting for physical non-idealities when analyzing and optimizing kernel performance.

</details>


### [285] [SLICE-CiM: Selective Layer-wise Inference Control Engine with Confidence-Based Early Exit for SRAM based Digital Compute-in-Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37601)
*Pratham Sharma,Mohd Faisal Khan,Santosh Kumar Vishvakarma*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该工作提出了四种可合成的外围模块来优化SRAM CIM宏的能耗性能，包括FME控制器、基于置信度的早期退出模块、精度寄存器带解码器以及位级串行时钟控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文的工作动机在于针对SRAM CIM宏中引入的三个主要能耗效率问题，即低精度时的位片过度激活、积累深度过剩以及精度转换导致的停滞现象，提出了一系列的优化方案。

Method: 本文设计并提出了四个可综合的外围模块，用于最小化SRAM CIM宏中的切换活动、数据通路开销及泄漏电流。这四个模块分别是：FME控制器、基于置信度的早期退出模块、精度寄存器带解码器和位级串行时钟控制器，它们从不同的角度解决了SRAM CIM宏中的效率问题。

Result: 该技术在TSMC 65nm CMOS工艺下，于335MHz时实现了高性能优化。具体表现在门级VCD分析中，总功耗为328.9556μW，其中动态功耗为322.8850μW，泄漏功耗为6.0706μW。在针对MobileNetV2（CIFAR-100）的系统评估中，能源效率提升显著，从8位精度变为2位精度时，效率提升了13.33倍。

Conclusion: 本文提出的技术在不修改位单元结构和不需要重新训练模型的前提下，显著提高了CNN的工作负载效率，并指出未来可以将该技术拓展至Transformer架构的工作负载。

Abstract: Digital SRAM compute-in-memory (CIM) macros enable energy-efficient CNN inference by eliminating off-chip weight transfers; however, peripheral control logic introduces three inefficiencies: bit- slice over-activation at reduced precision (L1), unnecessary accumula- tion depth (L2), and idle precision-transition stalls (L3). To address these, we present four synthesizable peripheral blocks: a Flush-Merge Enable (FME) controller, a Confidence-Based Early-Exit (EE) module, a Precision Register with Decoder (PR/PD), and a Bit-Serial Cycle Controller (CC), co-designed to minimize switching activity, wordline overhead, and leakage. Implemented in TSMC 65-nm CMOS at 335 MHz, the peripheral framework occupies 1769.760 μm2 post-synthesis and 1982.131 μm2 post-PnR. Gate-level VCD analysis reports 6.0706 μW leakage and 288.2902 μW (post-synthesis) / 322.8850 μW (post-PnR) dynamic power, totaling 328.9556 μW (0.2402% of system power). The EE module dominates dynamic power (58.2%), while WLG dominates area (58.1%). System evaluation on MobileNetV2 (CIFAR-100) shows efficiency scaling from 31.35 TOPS/W (8b/8b, 1.2 V) to 454.91 TOPS/W (2b/8b, 0.8 V), achieving 13.33x improvement without bitcell modification or retraining. The framework targets CNN workloads exploiting sparsity, with Transformer extension as future work.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [286] [DeepInfer-Mac: Running a 750B-Class MoE on a 24 GB Laptop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36564)
*Haorui Li*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: DeepInfer-Mac 使用 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 模型在 Macbook Air 上运行，通过分离处理和存储，实现部分加权操作，但这会导致生成每个词都需要连续的存储读取。通过优化专家权重读取并减少额外读取，系统在端到端运行中可生成每秒 0.21-0.72 个词，最大进程内存使用为 16.73 GiB。长提示继续是昂贵的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决大规模模型在笔记本电脑等资源有限设备上运行的局限性，DeepInfer-Mac 设计了一种新的方法来分离模型的处理和存储，以提高效率和可执行性。

Method: 该方法通过分离专家权重的加载和直接填充可重用的 GPU 缓冲区来优化读取操作，同时还设计了与缓存分配相关的措施来管理生命周期。

Result: DeepInfer-Mac 在端到端运行中的表现表明，可以生成英、中、代码，每秒速度在 0.21-0.72 之间，系统占用的峰值内存约 16.73 GiB，另外，通过下一层预取实现的解码吞吐量增加了 21.0%，但这也带来了额外的读取次数。

Conclusion: 尽管该方法允许运行远超现有内存大小的模型，但存储预填充仍然是影响用户等待时间的关键因素。

Abstract: DeepInfer-Mac runs the 750B-class DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash model on a MacBook Air with 24 GB of unified memory. A mixed low-bit conversion occupies 338 GiB on the internal SSD. About 7.6 GiB of weights remain resident; routed experts and Engram rows are loaded when needed. This separation makes the model executable, but it leaves each generated token waiting for a succession of storage reads. We organize expert weights so that reads fill reusable GPU buffers directly, and couple cache allocation with the lifetime of those buffers. In end-to-end runs, the system generates English, Chinese and code at 0.21-0.72 tokens per second, with a largest sampled process footprint of 16.73 GiB across the tested requests. Matched decode comparisons show a 21.0% throughput gain from next-layer prefetching over resident-expert overlap alone, at the cost of additional reads. Longer prompts remain costly. We describe the design, provide the implementation and measurement scripts, and identify the limits of this operating point: a model far larger than memory can run locally, while storage and prefill still govern the user's wait.

</details>


### [287] [DFIP: Decision-Focused Inference Path for System One Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36804)
*Ismail Pelaseyed,Alan Zabihi*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: DFIP研究了PPLX-Decider、Liquid d1-3B、Clef-Flash和GLiNER2.5-Decide等现有推理技术，通过CUDA图重放、候选词投影和长度感知批处理，实现了决策任务的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了探索在AI推理任务中使用CUDA图重放、候选词投影和长度感知批处理等技术，以提高决策任务的效率和准确性。

Method: 在四个不同的模型PPLX-Decider、Liquid d1-3B、Clef-Flash和GLiNER2.5-Decide上应用CUDA图重放、候选词投影和长度感知批处理等技术，进行了一系列受控实验，比较了加速效应和传统方法的差异。

Result: 短布尔驻留情况下，自定义DFIP加速器在五容器中的Decider获得了2.47倍的加速，在四容器中的Clef获得了1.45倍的加速；Liquid在释放编译的情况下与原生增强源基准几乎持平，重放提供了大部分其急切基准的增益，而投影本身对完整请求的效益甚微；在长度感知批处理实验中，它在一个工人GLiNER情况下比原生编译的FIFO批处理快1.40倍。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，这些技术对特定工作负载的加速是有效的，但并不能作为通用优化器或部署准备的标准。

Abstract: System One (decision) models return small structured answers, but can retain host dispatch, GPU launch overhead and full-vocabulary projection. We present DFIP (Decision-Focused Inference Path), an empirical systems study of established inference techniques in PPLX-Decider, Liquid d1-3B, Clef-Flash and GLiNER2.5-Decide. Selective CUDA graph replay, candidate-token projection and schema-compatible length-aware batching are applied at compatible model boundaries. We compare resident requests with stronger native baselines, audit exposed answers and measure changing arrivals. With optional native Qwen kernels installed, short Boolean resident speedups over strengthened native are 2.47x for Decider across five containers and 1.45x for Clef across four of five planned containers; Clef's fifth fails in native preflight. Liquid is effectively tied with released compilation; replay supplies most of its eager-baseline gain, while projection alone adds little full-request benefit. In a one-worker GLiNER follow-up, length-aware batching is 1.40x faster than compiled native FIFO batching on a diverse resident trace, but has worse arrival p95. Decider and Clef audits cover 3,024 decisions per model and preserve all 1,112 returned choices, while Clef changes scores, probabilities and serving confidence thresholds. Diverse external arrivals reverse fixed-shape replay gains and expose overload losses and a replay-path CUDA error. The results identify workload-specific acceleration and its limits, rather than a universal optimizer or production readiness.

</details>


### [288] [EdgePace: Deadline- and Freshness-Aware LLM Scheduling on Unified-Memory Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37307)
*Teng Mei,Chengxuan Pei,Weijun Wang,Jie Wu,Marco Canini,Yunming Xiao*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: EdgePace是一种带宽感知的运行时系统，能够在保持LLM推理性能的同时保护前台任务的截止时间。它通过对预填充、解码点设置安全暂停，并根据竞争风险、截止时间余量和输出新鲜度来调度推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有设备存在计算能力和边缘推理的内存竞争，导致了对前台任务截止时间和LLM反馈时间的威胁。

Method: EdgePace通过设置安全暂停点，监控前台内存需求，以及根据内存带宽竞争风险、任务截止时间和推理输出的新鲜程度进行调度，来解决内存带宽与任务时间的冲突。

Result: 实验结果显示，EdgePace能够显著减少前台任务的截止时间错过情况，同时保持LLM的较高推理吞吐量。

Conclusion: EdgePace为前端和后端任务提供了更好的调度策略，适用于多摄像头处理和机器人目标跟踪等依赖实时推理的应用场景。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly run on edge devices alongside tasks that continuously process sensor data. In many applications, these foreground tasks prepare observations as inputs to the LLM, whose outputs guide subsequent processing or actions. The observations must be processed by foreground deadlines, and LLM feedback must arrive while its source observations remain useful. Yet the CPU and accelerators share a unified-memory system, so LLM inference can delay foreground processing through memory-bandwidth contention even when compute resources remain available. We present EdgePace, a bandwidth-aware runtime that protects foreground deadlines while sustaining timely LLM feedback. EdgePace creates safe suspension points across prefill and decode, monitors foreground memory demand and stalls at low cost, and schedules inference according to contention risk, deadline slack, and output freshness. We evaluate EdgePace on a OnePlus 13 and three NVIDIA Jetson platforms using multi-camera processing and robotic target tracking. Relative to unmanaged inference, it reduces foreground deadline misses by 24.1%--96.1% while retaining 64.9%--98.5% of LLM throughput. All measured LLM outputs arrive within their task-defined freshness windows. On OnePlus 13, for example, camera deadline misses fall by 91.3% while 90.4% of LLM throughput is retained.

</details>


### [289] [Approaching Optimal Scaling of Queue Size for an Input-Queued Switch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37521)
*Taha Ameen,Weina Wang*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种参数化的调度策略，通过调整参数k，使得队列长度的期望值接近猜想的最优_scaling_。同时，这些策略的单位时间槽的调度成本是O(n^3 k)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前对于大系统规模下的输入排队交换机的队列长度期望值，尤其是在重负载情况下，还没有一个确定的最优scaling。本文试图填补这个空白。

Method: 作者设计了一种参数化调度策略，参数k可调，并通过这种设计实现了调度成本的优化。

Result: 在均匀流量的情况下，作者的策略实现了队列长度期望值为O\(n \log \max \{n, 1/(1-ρ)\} / (1-ρ)^{2^k/(2^k-1)}\)，并且随着k的增加，指数值逐渐接近1，使得结果接近猜想中的最优scaling。单位时间槽的调度成本为O(n^3 k)。

Conclusion: 本文的结果接近甚至达到对于大系统规模下的输入排队交换机队列长度期望值的猜想最优scaling，为相关领域的研究提供了理论支持。

Abstract: We study the scaling of the expected total queue length in an $n\times n$ input-queued switch. It has been conjectured that the optimal scaling is $O(n/(1-ρ))$, up to logarithmic factors, where $ρ$ is the system load. This conjecture has been established in the traditional heavy-traffic regime and in certain sub-regimes of the large-system regime. But it remains unresolved over a wide range of load scalings in the large-system regime. In this paper, we develop a family of scheduling policies parameterized by a positive integer $k\ge 2$. We show that under uniform traffic, our policy achieves an expected total queue length of $O\bigl({n\log\max\{n,1/(1-ρ)\}}\big/{(1-ρ)^{2^k/(2^k-1)}}\bigr)$, as long as $ρ\to 1$ in the large-system regime. As $k$ increases, the exponent $2^k/(2^k-1)$ approaches $1$, bringing our bound arbitrarily close to the conjectured scaling. The expected amortized scheduling cost of the policy is $O(n^3 k)$ per time slot. Our policy is of a batching type, with a novel recursive design that allows an arbitrary number of batching levels. This design principle may also be useful for other scheduling problems in which combinatorial constraints restrict which jobs can be served simultaneously.

</details>
